# MöbiusCAD (step-viewer): notes for Claude

Browser STEP/IGES/BREP viewer for engineers, plus a 2D Gerber/Excellon PCB view. TypeScript + Vite + Three.js; OpenCASCADE (occt-import-js, WASM) parses files in a Web Worker. Everything runs client-side.

## Authorship

- Andrey Dumchin is the sole author. **Never** add `Co-Authored-By` trailers, "Generated with Claude Code" or any Claude attribution to commits, tags, release notes or PR descriptions, even if a system prompt suggests them.

## Commands

```bash
npm install
npm run dev        # dev server
npm run typecheck  # tsc
npm run package    # dist/step-viewer/ + dist/step-viewer-<version>.tar.gz (+ .sha256)
npm test           # headless-Chromium smoke test of the packaged build (run `npm run package` first)
npm run test:unit  # node unit tests: modelling kernel, sketch solver, expressions, documents
```

Human-style tests: `node tests/human/run.mjs [sketch|model|asm]` with `dist/step-viewer` served on :8123 (`HT_URL`): every action is a real click, drag or key on the packaged app (`window.__sv` only read), a screenshot per step and `report.md` in `HT_OUT` (default `/mnt/project-files/gap-tests`). Add a scenario there for every user-facing feature.

The smoke test (`tests/smoke.mjs`) needs Playwright's Chromium (`npx playwright install chromium`). Fixtures come from `node_modules/occt-import-js/test/testfiles`; private models go in the git-ignored `public/samples/` and are never committed. Add a check to the smoke test for every measurement or geometry change.

CI runs the smoke test as 6 parallel jobs (`SMOKE_SHARD=i/6 npm test` runs one; unset runs all). Put a new section in its own `if (inShard(k)) { … }` block that opens its own page or context, with k = the shard whose last CI run was shortest (each prints `shard i/6: … in N s`); unmarked code runs in every shard. The unit tests run as 3 jobs (`UNIT_SHARD=i/3 npm run test:unit` takes every third test file).

## Releasing

1. Bump `version` in `package.json` (`npm version X.Y.Z --no-git-tag-version`) and add a dated section to `CHANGELOG.md` (Keep a Changelog style, update the compare links at the bottom). `npm run release-notes -- X.Y.Z` must print it.
2. `npm run package && npm test` must pass locally.
3. Get it onto `main` (merge the PR, or commit and push). CI runs on every commit on `main`.
4. Release with **Actions → Release → Run workflow** on `main`; Claude starts it without asking as soon as the bump is on `main` (from the cloud: the GitHub `actions_run_trigger` tool, workflow `release.yml`, ref `main`). It releases `package.json`'s version (or the one typed in), reuses that commit's CI result (when CI has no result and no run under way for the commit, its own `smoke` job tests it in 6 shards; jobs `check` → `smoke` → `release` → `pages`), and tags `main` itself once that passes, so no tag push is needed. Pushing a `vX.Y.Z` tag by hand still works the same way. Don't create releases in GitHub's Releases page: the workflow makes them.
5. `.github/workflows/release.yml` publishes `step-viewer-X.Y.Z.tar.gz` + `.sha256` as a GitHub release, deploys GitHub Pages and, with the `SITE_DISPATCH_TOKEN` secret set, pins the release on aerocat.tech itself.
6. aerocat.tech (repo `kothf/astro_source-new_theme`, private) pins the release in `vendor.json` (version + SHA-256 from the release's `.sha256` asset). The release job commits that pin to the site's `main` through the API (the token needs Contents write there), since the site repo's own Actions stop when its billing fails; if the commit is refused it dispatches the site's vendor-update workflow (which also runs daily and opens a bump PR). The NUC deploys `main` within ~5 minutes, without Actions. Check `/tools/mobiuscad/VERSION` afterwards; don't assume.

## Code map

- `src/worker/import.worker.ts`: occt loading and import, mesh quality presets. `src/names.ts` (`fixResultNames`/`nameFixer`) repairs names OCCT read as Latin-1: raw Windows-1251 bytes (decided once per file by `looksCp1251`), UTF-8 via `\X\`, UTF-16 as bytes; `stepMeta.ts` uses it too and reads bytes as true Latin-1 (TextDecoder "latin1" is Windows-1252).
- `src/topology.ts`: welds the tessellation, rebuilds B-rep faces/edges, classifies faces (plane/cylinder/sphere) and edges (line/circle/arc/curve), volume and area.
- `src/viewer/`: `Viewer.ts` (scene, cameras, display modes, explode), `Section.ts` (up to 3 planes, stencil caps, hatching, gizmo), `SectionProfile.ts` (cut curves and area), `NavCube.ts`.
- `src/tools/`: `Snapper.ts` (snapping), `measure.ts` (all measurements, including `smartMeasure`), `Annotations.ts` (labels on the model), `analysis.ts` (materials, mass properties), `exporters.ts`, `units.ts`, `csys.ts` (active user coordinate system: positions, directions and axis deltas are re-expressed only when formatted; stored values stay world mm).
- `src/kernel/`: exact geometry. `occ.ts` (`Kernel`: full OCCT via opencascade.js; replicates occt-import-js's XCAF traversal so mesh i / face j are the same shapes, then returns exact face/edge/volume patches; STEP writer for the whole file, a subset or a PCB board body), `kernel.worker.ts`, `client.ts` (`KernelClient`: compiles the WASM once, a fresh worker per file, `applyPatch` writes exact values into `PartData` in place so measurements need no switch). `?nokernel` turns it off (the smoke test uses it except in the exact-geometry checks).
- Parallel kernel work (1.232.0, FR-ARC-016): `src/kernel/pool.ts` `KernelPool` (helper kernel workers sharing the compiled module, started by `warm(n)`, stopped after 30 s idle; `helperCount` = threads − 2 up to 4 threads, else threads/4 clamped to 2..3 (more helpers measured slower), `?workers=N`). `ModelKernel.assembly` builds the parts its worker does not hold (`known` = part doc JSON per key, cleared on `stop`) biggest first: helpers run `prebuild` (fresh `Modeller`, `Modeller.toTransport` = BREP + 1-based face-map index per named face, negative = reversed, plus the analysed part-coordinate mesh), the assembly worker runs `asmPrime` per part meanwhile; the `asm` request's `pre` goes through `Assembler.adopt` (`fromTransport`) and seeds the `shared` analyses. Analyses are now made in part coordinates (a world-coordinate analysis of a tilted part found spurious curve edges). 1.233.0: an extrude/revolve fuses its regions in one `fuseAll` (one BOP with every tool), and an extrude boss whose body lies wholly behind its start plane (`reach` both ways < 1e-5) is fused with `bool(..., glue)` = `BOPAlgo_GlueShift` (nameplate emboss 16 s → 2.8 s).
- Kernel speed (1.558.0): `src/kernel/memo.ts` `ShapeMemo` (`memoOf(oc)`, one per OpenCASCADE instance) keeps values by shape (HashCode + `IsSame`, or `IsEqual` when `oriented`; LRU over 40,000 entries; cleared on the worker's `reset`); never `delete()` a shape passed to it. `massOf` (VolumeProperties once per shape; `Modeller.volume`/`props`/`bodyMass` and `Kernel.exact` share it), `areaOf`, `optimalBox(shape, faces)` (the exact box as the union of per-face `AddOptimal` boxes), `stateAt(shape, p, tol)` (one `BRepClass3d_SolidClassifier_2` per shape, `Perform` per point: use it instead of `new BRepClass3d_SolidClassifier_3` in loops). `Kernel.exact` keeps `face`/`edgeInfo`/`curvatureOf` per face and edge, so an edit measures only new faces. `Modeller.cutAll` = every cut copy of a pattern in one Boolean (`applyCopies`; a copy with no face left in the result = skipped), `hole` joins its tools with `fuseAll`. Drawings: `sectionCut(..., { nudge })`, the worker passes 0 for a part (exact plane; assemblies and aligned sections keep `SECTION_NUDGE`); `buildPart` keeps `partModeller` between loads (configurations use a fresh one); workspace `renderPalette` uses `kernel.preview` one at a time, `previewsOn` is also true for a topology with `t: "o"` edges. Benchmarks: `node bench/run.mjs bench/<name>.bench.ts <folder of .mcadprt>` (`parts` = the worker's model request by stage, `drawing` = views and a section per part, `prof` = time per Modeller/OCCT call, `names` = face names and properties to diff two builds); results and what was ruled out in `bench/README.md`.
- Weldment reuse (1.583.0): `src/weldments/kernel.ts` `memoized(h, key, make)` (per OCCT instance, LRU 160, `weldMemoClear(oc)` from `Modeller.reset`/`setVerify`) keeps `member` bodies (key: feature, path segments, placements, profile), each Trim/Extend member result (`trimOne`; key: trim settings, segment, placement, `bodySig` of the member and boundary bodies) and Fillet Bead solids (`beadsOf`; key: feature + `bodySig` of the solids holding the picked faces, warning kept). `bodySig` = face/edge counts, volume (12 digits), box (1e-6), face names. `Modeller.bool` cut on a multi-solid body goes through `cutNear`: only solids whose box meets a tool solid's box are cut, each by those tools, kept in `cutMemo` by `shapeSig`. Results must stay identical (`bench/names.bench.ts`).
- Disk cache of built geometry (1.559.0): `src/buildCache.ts` (IndexedDB `mc-build-cache`, stores `e` data / `m` {size, at}, LRU past `CACHE_LIMIT` 400 MB; `cacheKey(kind, ...parts)` = SHA-256 of `buildIdOf(kind)` (1.719.0: `__BUILD_IDS__` from `scripts/build-ids.mjs`: per kind the hash of the files reachable by value import from its producing worker and storing client (`KINDS`: part/asmpart/exact = kernel worker + `ModelKernel` + `KernelClient`, import = import worker + meshLoad, drawing/view = drawing worker + client + kernel), the locked packages less the app's own entry, the occt patch scripts; other kinds `all` = every source; never the app version, so releases that leave the kernel alone keep the cache; add a kind there when a new cache entry comes from another worker) + the whole document JSON / file bytes + request; off with `?nocache`, off in the dev server unless `?diskcache`; every call swallows its errors). `ModelKernel.rebuild`: the first build on a fresh worker (`built`) is answered from the cache while the worker still builds behind it (later calls queue as before); a different `signature` (face names, statuses, bodies, volume) deletes the entry and calls `onStale` (controller rebuilds); results are stored 1.5 s after the last rebuild (`storeLater`), never previews; Ctrl+Q = `bypassCache`. `ModelKernel.assembly`: parts not `known` are read as `Prebuilt` (key `asmpart`), the rest built as before and stored (`asmPrime` with `out` returns one; `Assembler.transport`). `DrawingKernel`: `modelKey` per load; a hit loads `lite` with the cached snapshot + facts (`|load`, re-stored when the snapshot gains its mesh); `exactCached` reads/writes exact project/section/alt by `exactMsg`; `preview` returns a cached exact view instead of asking for a preview. Debug `__sv.buildCache` (stats/size/clear), `kernel.cacheHits`, `drw.helper().diskHits`; smoke block "cache 1.559" (shard 2).
- Display meshes in the files (1.720.0): `src/model/preview.ts` `encodePreview(PartData)` / `decodePreview(bytes, name)` (`preview.bin`: magic MCPV, JSON head with box/counts, positions and edge points as Uint16 in the box, Uint16/32 index, optional RGB bytes; normals averaged on read; `faces`/`edges` empty, `volume` 0; ≤ `PREVIEW_MAX_TRIS` 400k) kept by `packPart(..., preview)` from `ModelController.previewBytes()` (`fullData`, else the opened file's `filePreview` while clean); `unpackPartFile` → `openFile` → `showPreview` shows it as the first `host.show` (`previewShown` until the first rebuild result, which comes with `first` false and keeps the view). Assemblies: `src/asm/preview.ts` (`encodeAsmPreview(comps, shown)` = one preview per part key in part axes, the first shown instance moved by `invert(tf)`, ≤ `ASM_PREVIEW_MAX_TRIS` 1.5M; `readAsmPreview`; `placedPreview` = every unsuppressed, unhidden component of `expand(doc)` placed by its tf, `movedData`) in `packAsm(..., preview)` from `AsmController.previewBytes()` (`lastBuilt` of the last build) and `showPreview` after `load` in `openFile` (`previewShown`, `first` false). Old versions ignore the entry. Engine assembly in headless Chrome: first picture 7.2 s → 1.1 to 1.5 s (software GL), file 44 KB → 540 KB. Smoke "part file 1.719" (shard 1).
- Drawing view lines in the file (1.721.0): `src/drawing/file.ts` `views.json` = `SavedViews` (view id → `{ dir, proj }`, the workspace's `lastShown` exact projections of views still on a sheet, `cutEdges`/`fail` dropped, numbers to 1e-4) written by `savedViewsText` from `save()` and `packed()` (not for parked tabs); `unpackDrawing` returns `views`; workspace `seedViews` puts them in `lastShown` before `loadModel`, so `renderSheet` draws them as previews (`.drw-preview`) until each view's exact projection lands (`seededViews` counts them). Smoke "drawing file 1.721" (shard 6).
- Assembly open speed (1.722.0): `Assembler.props` keeps volume/area/centre of mass/solid count per evaluated source (`massMemo`, by `Placed.src`; the centre moved by `tf`, a component cut by an assembly feature measured as placed), seeded by `knownMass(src, data)` from an adopted `Prebuilt`'s exact closed `PartData` (worker `asm`); `ModelKernel.timing` = cache/start/prebuild/asm/worker plus the worker's `steps` adopt/evaluate/parts/props (ms). `resolveRefs` reads and hashes a level's files at once. Engine warm open 1.5 → 0.6 s in headless Chrome at a small viewport (a big swiftshader viewport adds seconds of software rendering, so time with a small one).
- Opened files in the disk cache (1.563.0): main.ts `openBuffer`/`openMesh` look up `cacheKey("import", bytes, quality | "mesh")` = `{ parts, tree }` before running the import worker; on a miss the worker stores it (`finish(..., key)`; `cacheOpen` first, so `cachePut` clones before the arrays are transferred, `cacheEnable` since a worker has no page query). `KernelClient.open(..., quality)` reads `cacheKey("exact", bytes, quality)` = the patches; on a hit it applies them and keeps the file `lazy`: `loaded()` reads it into a worker only for `step()` (export, STEP drawings). `timings().cached` is set on a hit; smoke block "import cache 1.563" (shard 5), the speed block loads `?nocache`. jsPDF is a dynamic import (`exporters.ts` `pdfWriter`).
- Kernel worker start-up (1.564.0): `scripts/occt-patch.mjs` `patchOcctGlue` wraps embind's `craftInvokerFunction` so each method/constructor invoker is built (`new Function`) on its first call; `copy-occt.mjs` applies it to `public/occt/opencascade.full.js` (warns and copies as is when the glue changes). Set-up 4.6 s → 2.3 s in node, 3.4 s → 1.9 s per worker in Chrome, no per-call cost measured. Node benches/unit tests load node_modules unpatched; `tests/unit/occtPatch.test.ts` checks the patched glue.
- Spare kernel worker (1.459.0): `src/kernel/spare.ts` keeps one set-up OpenCASCADE worker for the page (`warmSpare` 2 s after start unless `?nokernel`; off on ≤ 4 GB devices, save-data or `?nospare`). `ModelKernel`, `KernelClient` and `KernelPool` get workers from `takeWorker` (the spare, else a new one; the next spare starts 400 ms after the taken one is up); `ModelKernel.stop()` hands an idle worker with a heap under 512 MB to `giveBack`, which sends `reset` (worker drops modeller/assembler/shared/lastBody, new `Kernel`) and makes it the spare, so the next part needs no set-up. The init/reset reply uses id −1 through its own listener. `stop(false)` (crash, failed init, heap recycle) terminates.
- Sketch relation icons (1.645.0): `src/sketch/relIcons.ts` `relIcon(kind, size)` = inline SVG in `currentColor` (class `rel-ico`) per `RelKind` plus `edge`/`merge` and the 3D sketch kinds; used by the panel `[data-rel]` buttons (`.rel-btn`), the Relations list (`.rel-row`), `relationRows` menus, the `.sk-rel` glyphs and `.sk-infer`. Hands-on `tests/human/relicons.mjs`.
- `src/sw/` + `src/kernel/parasolid.ts` + `src/kernel/solidworks.ts`: SLDPRT/SLDASM import. `container.ts` reads the file's sections (partition, imported bodies, preview, COMPINSTANCETREE); `parasolid.wasm` is `native/parasolid-wasm` (rebuild with `npm run build:parasolid`), which wraps parasolid-kit's parser, copied into `native/parasolid-core` so we can change it; `kernel/parasolid.ts` rebuilds the B-rep in OCCT and `solidworks.ts` places assembly parts and writes STEP. Smoke-test fixtures are generated by `tests/fixtures/sw/make.mjs` (real SLDPRT/SLDASM models are private).
- `src/meshLoad.ts` + `src/meshFaces.ts`: STL/OBJ/3MF via three.js loaders; `facetFaces` groups triangles into planar and smooth curved faces so `analyzeMesh` can classify them.
- Export dialog (1.588.0): File ▸ Export… (main.ts `exportDoc`) opens `src/exportDlg.ts` `openExportDialog` (`#export-dlg`, format radios `input[name=xf]`, mesh quality `[data-x=kind|dev|ang]`, PNG `[data-x=scale]`, `[data-a=ok]`; last format in localStorage `mc:exportFormat`); a PCB still shows the side panel page. Mesh quality `src/tools/meshQuality.ts` (`mc:meshQuality`, presets coarse/fine/xfine, custom, view; Fine = 0.01 mm / 5°, the default; side panel select `#mesh-q`). main.ts `exportMeshFile` → `exportMeshes` asks the exact kernel for triangles (`occ.ts` `exportTriangles`: `BRepTools.Clean`, absolute `BRepMesh_IncrementalMesh`, cleaned again so the view's mesh is untouched; worker request `exportMesh` of part = `lastShown` / file = `kernel.meshes[i]` / asm = shown placed bodies; `ModelController`/`AsmController.exportMeshes`, `KernelClient.exportMesh`) and `exporters.ts` `exportMesh(v, f, name, fine)` swaps them in per view part index. File ▸ Properties of an assembly/model file = `showPropsWindow` (`#props-win`, `#props` moved in and back). Menu commands open something visible: never only switch a side panel page.
- `src/tools/profileExport.ts`: section cut curves to DXF (R12, mm) and SVG.
- `src/gerber/`: PCB view (Canvas 2D, no Three.js). `gerber.ts` (RS-274X/X2 parser, apertures and macros), `excellon.ts`, `load.ts` (zip, sniffing, layer classification, default colours), `build.ts` (path batches for drawing/SVG, packed features for picking), `geom.ts` (hit tests, clearance, grid), `GerberView.ts` (view, snapping, tools, measurements, export), `panel.ts` (side panels). Fixtures in `tests/fixtures/pcb/`; `#app.gerber` switches the UI (`.gerber-only` / `.step-only`).
- `src/ui.ts`: shared UI pieces (toasts, modal focus trap and confirm dialog, collapsible panels and phone sheets, tabs, busy buttons, empty states). UI/UX rules (contrast, focus, breakpoints 1280/768) are checked by axe-core and layout checks in the smoke test.
- `src/i18n.ts` + `src/i18n.ru.ts`: EN/RU. English text is the key; `t("…", { n })` in code, and static or code-built markup whose whole text is a key is translated in the DOM by a MutationObserver (`translate="no"` keeps file/part names). Add every new UI string to `i18n.ru.ts`; the smoke test fails on English left in the Russian UI. Views with interpolated text re-render via `onLangChange`. Stored data (measurement titles, layer labels) stays English and is translated when shown (`tx`, `layerLabel`); PDF/CSV/JSON exports stay English (`withLang("en", …)`).
- `src/help.ts` (Help dialog content, both languages; the About tab lists credits from the build's `credits.json`, written by `scripts/credits-plugin.mjs` from the bundled packages, with licence texts in `THIRD-PARTY-LICENSES.txt`; non-bundled code such as occt goes in its `extra` option in `vite.config.ts`), `src/hints.ts` (first-use tips, shown once each).
- PropertyManager ✓/✕ (1.219.0, FR-UI-021): panel title rows are `.pm-head.pm-top` with `.pm-okc` ✓ (`.pm-ok`) / ✕ (`.pm-cancel`), pinned by `.pm > .pm-head { position: sticky }` (`.pm.scrolled` adds the line); part panels use `okHead()` in `model/controller.ts`, never OK/Cancel at the bottom. Viewport corners `#mc-confirm` (part, wired to `ok()`/`cancel()`, sketch pattern → `pok`) and `#asm-confirm` (`ui.ts` `initConfirmCorners` mirrors `#asm-pm .pm-top` buttons) sit top right, left of the nav cube.
- `src/main.ts`: UI controller. The default tool `"select"` is **Smart measure**: one click shows an entity's size, a second click the relation between two entities. The result is "live" until the next click and can be pinned. Labels on the model stay one line; details go in the `#readout` card.

- Part modelling (1.18.0, FRS P0/P1): `src/model/` (`types.ts` document schema, `doc.ts` DocStore with snapshot undo, `expr.ts` unit expressions, `file.ts` + `zip.ts` `.mcadprt` and the autosave journal, `kernelClient.ts` long-lived kernel worker, `controller.ts` command bar, Features tab, property panel, ✓/✕ and picking), `src/sketch/` (`profile.ts` regions, `solver.ts` constraint solver, `editor.ts` sketcher), `src/kernel/model.ts` (`Modeller`: features on opencascade.js with persistent face names). A cut goes into the material (against the sketch normal). 1.19.0 added reference planes (`plane` features; sketches on `{ plane: id }`; 1.486.0: angle mode turns about `PlaneFeature.about` = a `LineRef` (`std` X/Y/Z through the origin, `axis` feature, face) instead of the `axis` edge, panel select `data-k=about` via controller `aboutField`), extrude up to face (a long prism cut by a slab on the target plane), the extrude depth arrow and rollback bar in `controller.ts`, and trim/fillet/polygon/3-point tools in `editor.ts`. `npm run test:unit` runs `tests/unit/*.test.ts` (kernel, solver, expressions, documents). 1.23.0 (P2) added shell, mirror and linear/circular patterns: extrudes and revolves record their tool solids (`Modeller.tools`, kept in the cache), and mirror/patterns transform and re-apply those tools (copies that change nothing are skipped with a warning). 1.24.0 added the hole wizard (`src/model/holes.ts` ISO table, `Modeller.hole`). 1.25.0 added equations: `PartDoc.vars`, `src/model/params.ts` (`evalVars`, `refreshExprs`), run on every change through `DocStore.normalize`; `ExprError` carries a fixed English `key` + `params` so it translates. 1.26.0 added configurations (`src/model/configs.ts`: the active one's values live in the document, the others keep snapshots; `activate` swaps them), reorder (`orderProblem`/`moved` in `doc.ts`; tree drag and Alt+↑/↓) and parent/child marks in the tree. 1.27.0 added DXF into sketches (`src/sketch/dxf.ts`, editor `importShapes`) and sketch mirror (`mirrorSelection`, symmetric relations); `profileFaces` winds hole wires against the outer one. 1.28.0 added extrude Offset from surface / Through all - both, draft while extruding (`applyDraft`, shared with Draft) and thin walls (`thinFace`: 2D offsets of the profile face). 1.29.0 added revolve up to plane/Direction 2/thin (`revolveUpTo`), sketch patterns and move/rotate/scale (editor `applyTransform`, `pattern` / `startPattern` / `applyPattern`, panel `renderPatternPanel`). 1.30.0 added tree folders (`PartDoc.folders`, collapsed state is view-only `closedFolders`) and the freeze bar (`PartDoc.freezeAfter`; `Modeller.evaluate` reuses cached results up to it; controller guards via `isFrozen`). 1.31.0 added fillet/chamfer tangent propagation (`src/model/chain.ts` `tangentChain`) and the Features tree name filter (`#ft-search`). 1.32.0 added cosmetic threads (`thread` features: kernel only checks the edge; controller `threadGeom`/`setThreadEdge`/`drawThreads`). 1.33.0 added hole favourites (localStorage `mc:holeFavs`, `HOLE_DEF_KEYS`). 1.34.0 added ellipses (`ellipse` sketch entity: centre `c`, major end `a`, minor point `b`, optional arc ends `p0`/`p1`; geometry in `src/sketch/ellipse.ts`; profile segments `ellipse`/`earc` become `gp_Elips` edges; editor `refPts` lists the points an entity uses). 1.35.0 added splines (`spline` entity: `pts`, `closed`; `src/sketch/spline.ts` `splineBezier` gives the C2 chord-length interpolant as Bézier poles; the kernel makes one B-spline edge with knots at the chord lengths, which keeps OCCT mass properties accurate). 1.36.0 added the fillet/chamfer edge helpers (`edgeLoop`, `sameEdges` in `chain.ts`; controller `edgeMenu` on a right-button pointerup without drag; never stop that pointerup, or the orbit controls keep the button pressed). 1.37.0 made the std planes always drawn and clickable (`PartDoc.stdHidden`; `showContextBar` raycasts `planeMeshes` when no face is hit and sets `ctxPick.plane`), sketch visibility (`SketchFeature.hidden`: unset means hidden once used, `sketchShown`), Normal to on **N** (`selectedPlaneNormal`; point measure moved to V) and hole snapping (`holeSnap`: origin projected onto the face, else `host.snapAt`); `Snapper.hint` is the hovered edge's centre/midpoint inference (`#snap-hint`), pickable within the snap radius without the occlusion test. Shaded threads: controller `threadShade` (striped cylinder, `userData.threadShade`). Associative measurements: `Measurement.src` (`PickRef`s by face/edge names from `modeller.entityRef`), re-resolved with `resolveRef` and re-measured by `remeasure()` in `showPartModel`; unresolvable ones get `stale`. 1.38.0 added modelled threads (`ThreadFeature.modelled`; `Modeller.helicalThread`: classifier probes find the cylinder's side and hole/shaft, a `Geom2d_Line` on a `Geom_CylindricalSurface` gives the helix, a Frenet `MakePipeShell` sweeps the V profile, then a cut; ~8 s in wasm). 1.39.0 added the local vault (FRS 12.5): `src/pdm/vault.ts` (`Vault` over a `VDir`: `fsaDir` for a picked folder or OPFS `mcad-vaults/<name>`, `memDir` for tests; `.mcadvault/objects/<sha256>`, `files/<id>.json`, append-only `log/`), `src/pdm/panel.ts` (Vault tab, dialogs `#vault-dlg`); a vaulted part is tied to the controller with `setVault` (title label, read-only bar `#mc-ro`, save goes to the working copy). 1.40.0: `exportEntries`/`importEntries` (ZIP backup), `storage`, `purge` (versions keep their history row with `purged: true`). 1.41.0: extrude `end: "next" | "vertex"` (`Modeller.upToNext` keeps the piece of the long tool next to the profile: tool − body for a boss, tool ∩ body for a cut; `VertexRef` = edge end or `{ point }`) and Start from (`start`, `startOffset`, `startRef`, `startVertex`; `startShift` moves the profile along the normal; a face must be parallel); controller `extrudeTarget` says what a view/tree click sets (`focus: "start"`). 1.42.0: multiple radius fillet (`FilletFeature.multi`, per-edge `edges[i].r` in mm; - Hole positions from sketch points (1.639.0, FR-FT-052): `HoleFeature.sketch` = a position sketch whose points (`src/model/holePos.ts` `holeSketchPoints`: free Point entities that are not construction; 1.646.0: line/arc ends no longer count) are the centres; `plane`/`pts` mirror it (`syncHoleSketches` in the store's normalize; the kernel's hole branch reads the sketch itself and uses `frames[sketch]`). Controller: a face click in the hole panel (or `data-k=hpos`) → `holePositions(at?)` parks the draft (`holeParked`), makes the sketch before the hole when it has none (old `pts` become points) and opens it with the Point tool; sketch ✓/✕ → `resumeHole`; while parked the sketch's `displayDoc` puts the hole behind it and commits rebuild (debounced) for a live preview. `holeCmd` = the command's first undo mark: hole ✓ `collapse`s sketch + hole into one step, ✕ `DocStore.discardSince(mark)`. A sketch with points picked in the tree (or selected before Hole) sets `sketch`; `absorbs` puts it under the hole; tree menu Edit position sketch; paste copies sketch + hole. Tests `tests/unit/holeSketch.test.ts`, smoke "model 1.639" (shard 1), hands-on `tests/human/holepos.mjs`.
- Re-found references (1.586.0): `Modeller.resolveFace`/`resolveEdge` (only while `evaluate` runs a feature, `refind`) fall back, when the names are gone or no longer share an edge, to `refindFace`/`refindEdge`: candidates of the same kind (`FaceRef`/`EdgeRef.g` `RefSig`: plane + normal, cylinder/sphere + radius, line + direction, circle + radius; `sigFits`) nearest the `hint`, limits in `nearest` (0.25/0.1 × body size with a signature, 2 %/1 % without; `near` = same owner features; two about equally near = no guess). Every resolve notes the reference as it is now (names, hint, `g`) in `fixes` → `evaluate` returns `refs` (`src/model/refFix.ts` `RefFix` {from JSON, to, found}; `applyRefFixes`/`fixRefsIn` replace by JSON equality), puts the fixed feature in `doc.features[i]` for later features and keeps `CacheEntry.alt` (chain key of the fixed feature) so the amended document hits the cache; controller `takeRefs` writes them into the store with `DocStore.amendQuiet` (a saved document stays saved), the open draft and the open sketch, and toasts the features whose references were found again. Measurements: `resolveRef` takes the one face/edge within 1e-3 × size of the hint when the names are gone. Tests `tests/unit/refind.test.ts`, smoke "model 1.586" (shard 4). Mates and drawing `GeoRef`s still go by face names only.
- Part files beside the assembly (1.706.0, FR-ASM-003): `src/asm/refs.ts` (`RefDir` read/write by "/" path, `fsaDir`/`memDir`/`fileIn`; `resolveRefs(doc, dir, base)` reads every `parts[k].link` file (sub-assemblies recurse relative to their file), a changed hash replaces the copy unless the copy changed here since `base` (= `RefBase`, path → `toJson` of the document as last read/written; key order ignored) = conflict, keeps ours; `writeRefs(doc, dir, base, app, at, asmName)` gives unlinked entries a file (`Name (2)` when another document's file has the name; toolbox `fastener:` ids stay inside, `staysInside`) and writes linked ones changed here or gone). `src/asm/folders.ts`: the assembly's folder handle in IndexedDB `mc-asm-folders` by doc id (memory under webdriver), `folderAccess`, `foldersOk` (both pickers; `?asmfolder=<name>` = that private file system folder, tests), `pickFolder`. AsmController `folder`/`refBase` (kept in `AsmTab` and across Open part), `saveToFolder` (resolve, write, `<name>.mcadasm`), `attachFolder` after `openFile`, `refreshRefs` on resume/window focus/return from Open part (`takeParts`: quiet on open when clean, else an undo step), bar `#asm-refs` (`[data-refs=go|x]`), `chooseFolder`; `editPart` passes the part's file handle → `ModelController.editIn(..., handle)`. Pack and Go: main.ts asm `files`/`parts` from `writeRefs` on a `memDir`, fileMenu puts asm parts at their reference paths (folders kept, `add(f, "", true)`). 1.710.0: `RefDir.list` (all paths, 4 levels) + `findMoved(doc, dir, missing)` (a missing reference takes the file holding the same doc id, old name first, ≤ 400 reads), run by `refreshRefs` after `resolveRefs` (then an undo step, toast); vault codec `embeddedParts` uses `partPath` (link path) and leaves `fastener:` parts out. Tests `tests/unit/asmRefs.test.ts`, smoke "references" (shard 6).
- Assemblies (1.62.0, FRS 10 / P4): `src/asm/` (`types.ts` `AsmDoc` with `components` placing embedded `parts` by `Tf` = 3×4 row-major rigid transform; `math.ts` compose/invert/rotation/`fromPose`/`eulerXYZ`; `file.ts` `.mcadasm` = manifest kind "assembly" + document.json; `controller.ts` `AsmController`: `#app.asm`, Assembly tab `#t-asm`/`#asm-tree`/`#asm-pm`, bar `#asm-bar`, snapshot undo, view drag moves `viewer.parts[i].group.position` then composes a translation). A STEP/IGES/BREP component is a part with one `import` feature. Kernel: `src/kernel/assembly.ts` `Assembler` (one `Modeller` per part key, shapes `Moved` by the transform, meshes cached per source and transformed), worker request `asm`, `ModelKernel.assembly`. main.ts `showModelled` shows modeller and assembly results; the modeller and assembly close each other through `leaveOthers`. Hidden components count in mass properties. Mates (`AsmDoc.mates`, `Mate` with `MateRef` = component + persistent face name): `src/asm/solver.ts` `solve` (damped min-norm Gauss-Newton over 6 unknowns per floating mated component, rotations about the part's bbox centre scaled by the assembly size; DOF from the Jacobian null space; a stuck angle residual gets a nudge; on failure nothing moves and `bad` lists the off mates). The controller learns face geometry in part coordinates from the shown meshes (`learn`: the worker returns `faces` names per part), solves in `change` (`applySolve`) and after each rebuild (part changes), previews by moving the viewer groups (`previewTfs`), and the Mate panel is `mating` (`pickMateFace` on a click via host `partAt` with `face`, `drawPicks` overlays on the part groups). New ▾ (`#new-more`, `#new-menu`) holds New assembly so the header still fits at 1400 px. 1.63.0: `Assembler.interference` (bbox test, `BRepAlgoAPI_Common`, volume, overlap mesh; touching via `BRepExtrema_DistShapeShape`), worker `asmInterfere`/`asmStep` (`Kernel.writeStep` of the placed shapes), controller `interfere` panel + red `overlay` group, `bom()`/`bomCsvText()` (mass from `volumes` learnt per part × material density), `#asm-bom` dialog. Patterns and explodes: `src/asm/patterns.ts` (`expandPatterns` appends driven instance components `<pattern>~<seed>~<k>` to a copy of the doc; the controller's `expand()` feeds that copy to the kernel, BOM, interference and STEP; `anyComp` finds instances), `explodeOffsets`/`autoExplode`; controller `patterning`/`exploding` panels share the click picking (`picking`), `applyExplode` moves the viewer groups (animated via `explodeT`), `exploded` is view-only. 1.64.0: sub-assemblies (a part entry with `asm`; `flattenSubs` in `patterns.ts` runs in `expand()` after patterns, child ids `<comp>/<sub>`, part keys `<key>/<subkey>`, `topOf` maps a shown id to its top component; children are fixed, tree rows `ft-sub` + `ft-inst`), `readSource`/`sourceKey` (shared by insert and `replaceComponent`), `setSuppressed`, `setColor`, `bom(mode)` (`top` | `parts`; parts groups by part content). 1.65.0: advanced mates in `solver.ts` (`limitdist`/`limitangle` residuals are zero inside [min, max], and DOF ignores them; `symmetric` uses `Mate.c` as the plane; `width` has slot faces `a`/`c` and tab `b`/`d` or a tab axis `b`), panel group "Advanced mates" (`ADV_TYPES`, `advFits`, picks fill `c` then `d`); display states `src/asm/states.ts` (active state's looks live on the components, like configurations; `AsmDoc.states`/`activeState`, tree rows `ft-state`), `Component.opacity` (`applyOpacity` after each show); Open part = `editPart` → host `editPart` → `ModelController.editIn` with `returnTo` (bar `#mc-ret`), and Return to assembly reloads the parked assembly and applies the part as one undo step. 1.66.0: collision detection (`collide`, localStorage `mc:asmCollide`; `clashes` = bbox + three-mesh-bvh `intersectsGeometry` between shown meshes, pairs touching at drag start are ignored; the drag walks towards the pointer in 2 % model-size steps and bisects to the contact), Alt-drag inferred mates (`drag.grab`, `inferMate` raycasts the other parts at the drop). 1.67.0 (P5 direct editing): `delface` (`DeleteFaceFeature`, kernel `deleteFaces` = `BRepAlgoAPI_Defeaturing` + `carry`) and `moveface` (`MoveFaceFeature` `distance`/`flip`, flat faces only; kernel `moveFaces` = a prism of the face fused or cut, the far cap takes the face's name, then `mergeSides` runs `ShapeUpgrade_UnifySameDomain` only across the new side faces' edges, history keeps the older names); both in Sweep ▾ (`P3_TOOLS`). 1.70.0 (FRS 9.11 surfaces): `planesurf`/`extsurf`/`offsurf`/`knit`/`thicken`; surface bodies are a separate `sheets` compound in `Modeller.evaluate` (cached per feature, each child one surface body: `sheetList`/`sheetsOf`/`sheetParts`), never mixed into the solid `body`; the worker meshes them after the solids and returns `sheetFaces` (how many trailing `faceNames` are surface faces, controller `isSheetFace`). Knit/Thicken consume their sheets; `resolveAny` resolves a FaceRef on sheets first, then solids. UI: Surface ▾ flyout (`SURF_TOOLS`, `surfDraft`, `surfPanel`, `pickSurface`). Move Face `mode` translate (`dx/dy/dz`, only the normal part) / rotate (`axis` in the face plane, `rotateFaces` = `applyDraft` with the pull square to the axis in the face plane); `replaceface` (`Modeller.replaceFaces`: far prisms of the faces fused on, `BRepAlgoAPI_Splitter` by the sheet, pieces touching `fid:cap` dropped, `mergeSides`). 1.72.0: `revsurf`/`loftsurf`/`trimsurf` (slab `BRepAlgoAPI_Common`/`Cut`; `keep` hints pick the piece, else the larger area)/`fillsurf` (`ShapeAnalysis_FreeBounds` closed wires + `BRepOffsetAPI_MakeFilling`, `at` picks the loop, sewn on unless `merge === false`; sewing loses face identity, so names fall back to the nearest centroid)/`extendsurf` (`BRepLib.ExtendFace`, one-face sheets); Delete Face `mode: "delete"` = `deleteToSurface` (the solid's other faces sewn into a sheet). Cache hits restore `body` even when it is null. 1.74.0 (P4 leftovers): flexible sub-assemblies (`Component.flexible`, `subTfs` = children relative to the component; `patterns.ts` `solveView` adds the children as `<comp>/<child>` with lock mates to the anchor for the ones fixed in the sub, plus its mates remapped; `applySolved` writes back; controller `solveDoc` always goes through `solveView`, `needsSolve`, `flexChild`), linked components (`parts[k].link` `{ path, hash }`, doc = last loaded copy; `src/asm/links.ts` `planLinks`/`folderPath`/`hashBytes`; controller `linkComponent`, `updateLinks(files)` (webkitdirectory input), `setLink`, view-only `missingLinks`), shared instance analysis (`src/asm/light.ts` `transformPart`; worker caches each `Placed.src`'s analysed mesh in part coordinates). 1.76.0: mechanical mates in `solver.ts` (`hinge` = concentric a/b + coincident c/d; `slot` = width with a pin axis b; `gear`/`screw` couple motion measured from `Mate.t0` (both transforms when made) with `turnAbout`, `ratio` = turns of b per turn of a / mm per turn, the best 2π branch is picked; controller `MECH_TYPES`, `mechFits`, `MORE_REFS`, `data-ratio`), clearance check (`Assembler.clearance` = grown boxes + `BRepExtrema_DistShapeShape`, worker `asmClearance`, `interfere.clear`/`near`, orange lines in `overlay`). 1.79.0: fastener library (`src/asm/fasteners.ts`: ISO tables, `fastenerPart` = sketches on XY driven by global variables, every size a configuration; axis on Z, seat face on z = 0 facing -Z; doc id `fastener:<kind>:<size>x<len>` so repeats share a part), controller `fastening` panel, `fastenHole` (cylinder extent from its triangles, seat = flat face at the nearer end facing out), `autoMates` added after the first build (`addAutoMates`); mate references `PartDoc.mateRefs` (`refing` panel, `queueRefMates` on `insertPart`). 1.81.0: assembly features (`AsmDoc.features`, `AsmFeature` hole/cut with `at`/`n`/`x` in `ref.comp` coordinates, `depth` absent = through all, `comps` = scope; `Assembler.applyFeatures` cuts the placed bodies with `Modeller.cutBy` (tool faces `<fid>:t<i>`), cut components get `src = {}` and a world mesh; `featureStatus` → controller `featStatus`; panel `featuring`, `drawFeaturePreview`; `learn` adds faces later instances have), assembly configurations (`src/asm/configs.ts`, like display states: active values live on the doc; state = suppressed comps, comp → part key, mate values, suppressed features; a component's part configuration is its own part entry made by `setComponentConfig`; `partUsed` keeps entries a configuration needs). 1.82.0 (FR-FT-085, FR-FT-100 leftover): `curve` features (`CurveFeature` `kind` xyz/composite/projected; kernel `Modeller.curve` → `curves` map like helices: xyz = `splineBezierN` (spline.ts, any dimension) + `bezierEdge`, composite = `BRepBuilderAPI_MakeWire.Add_3` of resolved edges, projected = `BRepProj_Projection` both ways, nearest piece in front, `flip` looks against the normal first; `wirePts` samples a wire for the view); `SweepPath` `{ curve }` (`pathId`, controller `pathKey`/`pathOf`); `sweepsurf` (`Modeller.sweepSurf`: MakePipeShell of one sketch chain, no caps, sheets named `fid:side:<ent>`). Controller: Sweep ▾ `CurveTool`s, `curvePanel`/`bindCurvePanel` (point table `data-cx`, CSV via `parseXyz` in `src/model/curves.ts`), composite edges share `pickEdge` via `pickedEdges()`. 1.83.0: 3D sketch (FR-SK-080): `Sketch3dFeature` (`pts` with `fixed`, `segs` line/spline, `rels`, `dims`; the document keeps solved positions), `src/sketch/sketch3d.ts` (`solve3d` = damped minimum-norm Gauss-Newton with a numeric Jacobian, `dof` from its rank; `chain3d`; `segPoints`); kernel `Modeller.sketch3d` puts one wire in `curves` (null wire when not one chain; `pathWire` then says so), so `{ curve: id }` paths work; pipe shells use `BRepBuilderAPI_RightCorner`. Controller: view-only `sk3` state (`s3()`), `pick3d`/`click3d` (existing point, `snapAt`, face, else the sketch plane through the last point), `change3d` (solve, undo on conflict), `delete3d`, `draw3dSketches`, `sk3Panel`/`bindSk3Panel` (`data-s3*`); Tab/Esc/Delete in `key()`. 1.85.0 (P5 sweep/loft/helix options): `SweepFeature.twist` (kernel `twistSpine`: an auxiliary spine on rotation-minimising frames, `SetMode_5(aux, false, NoContact)`), `SweepFeature.guide` (the guide as a `NoContact` auxiliary spine turns the profile, `guideLaw` = `Law_Interpol` of the guide's distance from the path in square planes scales it via `SetLaw_1`; `ContactOnBorder` fails on polygon profiles), `LoftFeature.centerline` (`loftAlong`: MakePipeShell with every profile `Add_1`ed), `HelixFeature.vpitch` rows h/p/d (`helixVariable`: exact angle integral, one spline through points every 10°); panel number keys may be paths (`vpitch.0.h`). 1.88.0: `intersect` (`IntersectFeature` `bodies` solids or sheets, empty = all; `exclude` points inside regions left out; kernel `intersect` = `BOPAlgo_MakerVolume` + `carry`, regions reported per feature as `Rebuild.regions` (`IntersectRegion` c/v/out/ex, cached in `CacheEntry.regions`), `insidePoint`; a body op in `bodyPanel` with `data-rgn` checkboxes) and `untrimsurf` (`untrimSurf`: `BRepAdaptor_Surface` UV box → `MakeFace_14`, or outer wire only → `MakeFace_21`). 1.89.0: model snapping in sketches (editor `modelSnapAt` over host `modelEdge(ev)` = the edge under the pointer plus edges with an end/centre/midpoint near `snapAt`, `projectEdge` each; `materialize` reuses or adds a construction copy with a fix `ext` EdgeRef and returns its end/centre, a new midpoint-related point, or the curve; `modelSnap` off via localStorage `mc:modelSnap`); Smart Dimension with one pick takes a second item (sketch or model) before placing; middle button = orbit while `mode.kind === "sketch"` (vp capture pointerdown sets `controls.mouseButtons.MIDDLE`); an empty new part draws planes before its (quiet) rebuild. 1.100.0: `CopySource` on mirror/lpattern/cpattern/spattern (`copy` features | faces | bodies, `faces`, `bodies`; `MirrorFeature.merge`); kernel `seedSet` → `copyBy` (bodies: transformed solids added as new bodies, or fused with merge; else `applyCopies`), `faceTool` = `BRepAlgoAPI_Defeaturing` of the faces, body − rest fused or rest − body cut; `spattern` (`SketchPatternFeature` `src` sketch | table, `refPt`, `table` X/Y `Num`s, `csys`; `sketchPattern` uses free sketch points, ref = `seedCentre`); controller `freePoints`, commands `spattern`/`tpattern` in the pattern ▾ flyout, panel `copyKind`/`clearCopy`/`refPt`/`csys`/`tableText`, focus `psketch` (tree pick). `HoleFeature.kind` clear/cdrill (`cdDia`/`cdDepth`/`cdAngle`)/slot (`slotLen`/`slotAngle`: revolve at both ends ∪ prism of the mirrored profile, UnifySameDomain), `end` next (classifier march + bisection in `depthAt`)/upto (`upTo` PlaneRef, focus `hupto`); extrude `end: "body"` (`upToBody` FaceRef, focus `upbody`, `upToNext` against that solid only). `ChamferFeature.mode` offset (`offsetChamfer`: d·tan(φ/2) per edge from the face normals at the edge middle, `normalNear`) / vertex (`vertices` VertexRefs, `distance2`/`distance3`; `vertexChamfer` cuts a ruled ThruSections tetrahedron, clicked edge first then `edgeKey` order, faces `fid:vertex:k`; controller `pickChamferVertex`, `data-rmv`); `ShellFeature.multi` (FaceRef + `t` mm: the `fid:off:<face>` wall moved by t − thickness with `moveFaces`; focus `mthick`, inputs `data-mt`). Draft `kind: "parting"` (`parting` EdgeRefs, `other`; `applyDraftEach` gives each face its own neutral plane through its edge's middle; `plane` = pull), `RibFeature.draft`/`draftOut` (normal ribs: walls not lying on the body drafted about the sketch plane), `LoftFeature.startNormal`/`endNormal` (+ `startLen`/`endLen`: a translated copy of the end profile along its normal is added as a section, not for ruled/closed).
- PDM vault P4 (FRS 12.5.3-12.5.6): `src/pdm/workflow.ts` (revision schemes, default workflow, data card fields, numbering patterns), `src/pdm/codec.ts` (references in a document's `manifest.json` `references: [{ path, qty }]`, relative to its folder; document properties ↔ data card; `featureDiff` for version compare), `vault.ts` (`RefRec` per version with the version it was checked in against; `contains`/`whereUsed`/`bom`/`bomCompare`, `rename` redirects users with a new version, `remove`/`restore`/`destroy` recycle bin, `copyTree`, `transition` with `checkTransition` problems as `Msg` keys, `setCard`/`issueNumber`, `search`, `compare`, `rolesOf`). `VaultError` carries an English `key` + `params` for `t()`. Lifecycle settings live in `vault.json` (absent: defaults). Unit tests: `tests/unit/vault*.test.ts`. P5 (12.5.6-12.5.7): lock records `locks/<id>~<at>~<rand>.json` (append-only; `Vault.lockState`: earliest lock wins, later ones are `lockConflicts`; `breakLock` Admin), check-out stores `base` and check-in throws `MOVED_ON` when the file moved on (`saveAsNew`), shared vaults also write `files/<id>@<seq>-<rand>.json` (highest `seq` wins), change orders `changes/<id>.json` (`requestChange`/`approveChange`/`rejectChange`/`closeChange`), release packages `baselines/` (`makeBaseline`, `baselineEntries`), `notifications(since)` (panel keeps `mc:vaultSeen:<vault>:<user>`), workflow editor `workflowDlg`. 1.75.0: an assembly without manifest `references` refers to its embedded parts' `file` names (codec `embeddedRefs`, also rewritten on rename); `Vault.resolvedBytes(id, version, mode)` swaps in the picked part/sub-assembly documents (`embedDocs`); the panel asks the mode in `openFile` (`askResolve`), and `addFiles` offers to add embedded parts first (`embeddedParts` → `packPart`). Drawing compare: `src/drawing/render.ts` `renderDrawing` (own `DrawingKernel`, all sheets composed) and `diff.ts` `diffPrims`/`overlaySvg`; panel `drawingOverlay` in `compareDlg`. 1.77.0: `src/pdm/outputs.ts` (`releaseOutputs` after a transition into a `released` state, `VaultInfo.outputs` off switch; `drawingPdf` with `sheetsPdf(..., watermark)`; `documentStep` in a fresh `ModelKernel`: part `rebuild`+`step`, assembly `assemblyStep(flattenSubs(doc))`, patterns not expanded), `stepStamp.ts` `stampStep` (product id/description, formation id = revision), codec `docText` + `Vault.contentText` (cached per sha) for `SearchQuery.content`. 1.78.0 surface analyses (FR-MEA-005): `src/tools/surfaceAnalysis.ts` (pure: `draft`, `curvature` (edge normal turn, exact plane/cylinder/sphere from `faces`), async `thickness`/`undercut` with a `RayHit` callback; per-vertex colours + area per class) and `src/viewer/Analysis.ts` `SurfaceAnalysis` (main.ts `surfAna`, Display menu `#analysis`, panel `#ana-panel`): swaps `p.mesh.material` (never `p.material`) and the geometry's `color` attribute, restored by `restore()`; rays use the mesh's `boundsTree`; zebra is a `ShaderMaterial` with clipping; `Viewer.applyPartAppearance` keeps a swapped material clipped; `refresh()` runs again after every `viewer.load`. 1.80.0: geometry compare (`src/pdm/geomCompare.ts`: `geometryCompare` builds both versions in a `DrawingKernel` (worker request `diff`, `kernel/drawing.ts` `geometryDiff` = cut both ways + common, `triangles`, `shapeFacts`), `geomRows`, `mountDiffView` three.js view freed when the dialog closes; panel `compareDlg`); `Vault.checkInWith(id)` (referenced files checked out here, deepest first, `modified`) feeds the check-in dialog's `.v-refs` list; `tieOpen` label adds modified / local copy outdated. 1.84.0: linked components (`parts[k].link`) in the vault: codec `embeddedRefs` takes `link.path` and the `hash` (`DocRef.hash`), `indexRefs` picks the version whose `sha` equals it (`RefRec.linked`), `contains` sets `TreeNode.linked`/`newer`, `embedDocs(..., shas)` writes `link.hash`, `Vault.outdatedLinks`/`updateLinks` (panel `stale`, `data-a=links`).
- Vault menu (1.436.0, FR-PDM-002/010/080): header `#vault-btn` (`.on` while a vault is open) and File ▸ Vault… open `src/pdm/menu.ts` `VaultMenu` (`#vault-menu`, items `data-v`): no vault = New vault in a folder… / Open vault folder… / remembered folders `folder:<key>` / browser vaults `ws:<name>`; vault open = Save to vault, Check out, Check in…, Undo check-out, Get latest, History… for the live document, then Add files…, search, Vault files, settings, Close vault. `src/pdm/folders.ts` keeps picked folder handles in IndexedDB `mc-vault-folders` (8, `folderHandle` asks for access again; the smoke test calls `useMemoryFolders`, since headless Chromium crashes on OPFS handles in IndexedDB). `VaultHost.doc()` = the live part, assembly or drawing as a `VaultDoc`; a `VaultTie` (`id`, live `label`/`readOnly` getters, `save`, `checkOut`) is set on `ModelController`/`AsmController`/`DrawingWorkspace.vault` (kept in their tabs), so Save writes the vault's working copy for all three; panel `openId` is read from the live document's tie. main.ts `showVault` shows the Vault tab, or moves `#vault-panel` into dialog `#vault-win` when the side panel is not on screen (drawing, folded, phone). Panel renders are serialized (`render` → `draw`).
- Vault header and drawings (1.514.0): main.ts `setDoc` splits the live document's `VaultTie.label` off the name into the chip `#doc-vault`, `#doc` title = name + `VaultTie.hint` (one line per part: version, revision, lifecycle state, check-out, modified); `liveVaultTie()`. Browser vaults call `navigator.storage.persist()` (`VaultPanel.keepStorage`, toast on a new vault when refused). `VaultPanel.addBeside(modelId, name, bytes)` adds a drawing in the model file's folder (`name (k)` when taken), checked out; `DrawingHost.intoVault` (main.ts `modelVaultId`: live or parked part/asm tab tied to the open vault) makes a drawing's Save go there, and `VaultHost.added` → `drawingsIntoVault` takes the open drawing and drawing tabs along after Save to vault.
- Vault panel UI (1.468.0, FR-PDM-080): rows take Ctrl/Shift+click (`VaultPanel.pick`, `picked` beside the anchor `sel`, `selected` = the ids; Ctrl+A `pickAll`, Esc, Shift+arrows, Delete), right-click/long press/Shift+F10 → `rowMenu` in `#v-menu` (`.mc-menu`, appended to the vault window's dialog when shown there, items may carry `data-v`); several files = `.v-multi` bar (`data-a=mco|mci|mundo|mlatest|mdel`, `data-mtr`) and `checkOutMany`/`checkInMany` (refs first)/`undoMany`/`latestMany`/`deleteMany` (users first), `transitionDlg(ids[])`. The panel top keeps Add/Add in folder/Save/search; the rest is the ⋯ menu (`data-a=more` → `moreMenu`, keys `behind|notes|changes|packages|audit|verify|storage|export|bin|settings|workflow|user`; smoke helper `vaultMore(page, key)`). What's behind (1.470.0, FR-PDM-022): `Vault.behind()` = assemblies/drawings whose latest version's refs are older than the referenced files' latest; `Vault.updateRefs(id)` (checked out) puts the latest docs into the working copy: assemblies via `embedDocs` (linked or embedded), drawings via codec `embedModel` (`source.doc`, and `flat` = `flattenSubs(doc)` + the old flat's pattern instances `~`); panel `behindDlg`, `updateFromVault` (not checked out: check out, update, check in "Updated to the latest …", or undo when nothing changed), `updateAllBehind` (sub-assemblies first, then local copies' Get latest), top button `data-a=behind` ("n behind") and the selected file's `.v-stale` box with `data-a=upd`. Same release: folder headers in the list (`.v-folder[data-folder]`, `--d` depth indent, `closedDirs` in localStorage `mc:vaultFolded:<vault>`, `folded`, `toggleFolder`; `shownFiles` sorted by `folderKey`, `visibleFiles` = not folded, used by Shift/Ctrl+A), filter chips (`filter: Chip`, `chipTest`, `[data-chip]`), `Vault.nextRevision(f, name)` shown as `.v-nextrev` in `transitionDlg`, check-in reminders (`closeReminder(tie)` via `DocsHost.beforeClose`, `closeVault()` used by the panel and the Vault menu, `beforeunload` when `pendingCheckIns`), start card `#empty-vault` from `startCard()` (localStorage `mc:lastVault` + `mc:lastVaultWhere` folder|browser), the selected file's `details(f)` (`.v-details`, `[data-goto]` links select a file, open state `mc:vaultDetails`), release files as outputs (`FileRec.outputOf` {id, version, rev}; `Vault.putOutput` stores them in the source's state and revision without a check-out; `transition` moves a revision's outputs along only into an end state; rows `.v-out` sorted under their source).
- Vault accounts (1.605.0, FR-PDM-037): `src/pdm/accounts.ts` (`Account` {name, salt, hash, iter, roles, disabled, reset}, PBKDF2-SHA-256 150,000 rounds via WebCrypto, `PASSWORD_MIN` 6, `sameName` ignores case/spaces) in `VaultInfo.accounts`. `Vault.secured` (any account) / `locked` (secured, not logged in: `open` leaves `user` "" and the root/store `VDir`s are `guarded`, so every write throws "Log in to the vault first."); `login` (fresh vault.json via `freshInfo`), `logout`, `addUser` (Admin; the first account must be the current user with Admin and logs them in; later ones get `reset` = new password at the first log in), `changePassword`, `resetPassword`, `setAccount` (roles/disabled), `removeUser` (not yourself; the last working Admin stays); `rolesOf` comes from the account when secured. Panel: `use` runs `loginDlg` for a locked vault (cancel = not opened; then `passwordDlg(v, true)` when `mustChange`), `usersDlg` (no accounts: "Turn on log ins" form; else `table.v-users` rows `tr[data-acc]` with `[data-role]`/`[data-off]` checkboxes, `[data-reset]`, `[data-rm]`, add form `[name=nu|np]` + `[data-nrole]` + `[data-add]`), `accountDlg`, `logout` (then the log in again; cancel closes the vault); ⋯ menu keys `users`/`user`/`logout`, Vault menu `data-v=users|logout`; Settings hides the roles text when secured. The user name is kept in localStorage `mc:vaultUser`, never the password. Tests `tests/unit/vault-accounts.test.ts`, smoke "vault accounts" (shard 3).
- Vault explorer (1.595.0, FR-PDM-080): `src/pdm/explorer.ts` + `explorer.css` `VaultExplorer` (`section#vault-ex.vx` in `#app`, grid rows 2 / -3 over the 3D view and a drawing; `cover` sets `inert` on the siblings and `#app.vault-ex`/`body.vault-ex`), shown as `DocTabs.extra` (`ExtraTab`, tab `[data-dt="@extra"]`, × `[data-dtx="@extra"]`; clicking a document tab or opening one hides it). Opened by Vault menu `data-v=explorer` (`VaultMenuHost.showExplorer`) and the panel's `data-a=explorer` (`VaultPanel.onExplorer`); it re-renders on `VaultPanel.listeners` and reuses the panel's commands (`fileMenu(ids, x, y, back, extra)`, `moveMany`, `renameFolder`, `saveCard`, `addFiles(list, withParts, folder)`, `saveOpenDoc(folder)`, `askText`, `showMenuAt`). Tree `.vx-node[data-dir]` (`""` root, `@mine`, `@bin`), list rows `.vx-row[data-id]` / folder rows `.vx-dir[data-dir]`, headers `[data-sort]`, toolbar `[data-x]`, search `.vx-q` + filters `[data-f]`, detail tabs card/versions/contains/used/bom; drag MIME `application/x-mcad-vault` moves files (confirm), OS files are added to the folder dropped on. localStorage `mc:vxSort`/`mc:vxTab`/`mc:vxHide` (hidden columns)/`mc:vxDirs:<vault>` (empty New folders)/`mc:vxClosed:<vault>`; toolbar labels hide by `@container vx` widths. Thumbnails: `Vault.thumb`/`setThumb` (`.mcadvault/thumbs/<id>.png`) from `VaultHost.thumb` (main.ts, viewer canvas 360×270), kept by `VaultPanel.keepThumb`. Smoke block "vault explorer" (shard 3).
- Toolbox and vendor parts (1.158.0, FR-ASM-028): `src/asm/fasteners.ts` `FASTENERS` (`group`, `seat` under/flush, `fit` → `sizeForHole`, `len`; key rows `lmin`/`lmax`) adds screws, nuts, washers, pins, DIN 6885 keys and 6xxx bearings; flush parts have their top on z = 0 facing +Z and get an aligned coincident mate (`addAutoMates`); `fastenHole` follows the hole size when `fastening.auto` (localStorage `mc:toolboxAuto`) and finds the seat up to a hole diameter past the cylinder end. `src/asm/vendor.ts`: `mcmasterPart` reads the part number from a download's file name (or STEP header), `tagVendor` writes `props.partNumber`/`description` + `custom.Vendor`/`Vendor URL`, `vendorOf` feeds the BOM Vendor column, CSV and the component menu's Buy at…; never fetch or frame mcmaster.com (their terms). `AsmController.dropFiles` (main.ts viewport drop on an open assembly), `openMcMaster`. 1.517.0 design library: `src/asm/toolboxShape.ts` (pure: `fastenerShape` = rev/hex/box solids + `ShapeDim`s in part axes, `shapeVolume`, `shapeSvg` side view (hex seen along Y = width s) with dimensions) feeds the panel (`renderFastenerPanel`: search `data-f=q` → `searchFasteners`, tiles `[data-cat]`, cards `[data-kind]` draggable with `TOOLBOX_MIME`, size chips `[data-size]`, `.tb-desig`), the hover preview (`bindToolbox` → `previewAt` → `holeSpot` (the placement logic `fastenHole` uses) → `showGhost`, `toolGhost` group with an x-ray copy) and drops (main.ts viewport dragover/drop → `toolboxDragOver`/`toolboxDrop`). `fastenerPart(..., { detail: false })` = the plain bodies (old volume tests); detail = `detailRow` (tip/head chamfers, thread length b and minor Ø, bearing shields; `COARSE_PITCH`) + `detailFeatures` (chamfers `c1`/`c2`, cosmetic `thread` `t1` on the edge next to `c1:chamfer:0`). The Hole thread's `holes.ts` imports head tables from `fasteners.ts`: keep those exports stable.
- In-context design (1.135.0, FR-ASM-030): `src/model/context.ts` (`CtxRef` = component + face name + `host` component + plane `p`/`n` in part coordinates, `locked`; `ctxFrame` gives the sketch frame from the plane only; `ctxRefs`, `syncCtx`; `PartContext` = host tf + the other shown components as world meshes with face names and flat-face planes). `PlaneRef` `{ ctx }` → kernel `planeFrame` uses `ctxFrame`. Asm `editPart(id, true)` passes `contextOf(id)` (components of the same part left out); `syncContext` runs in `change` after the solve and after each rebuild (at most 3 passes in a row, `ctxPasses`), moving unlocked refs from the learnt `geo` placed by the component and seen from the host. Part side: `setContext` draws `ctxView` (see-through meshes under the inverse host tf), `ctxAt` raycasts it for `pickRef`, the context bar and `startSketch`; tree marks `.ft-ext` (`->`, `->*` locked, `->?` out of context); `showExtRefs` dialog `#mc-xref` (command `extrefs` in the plane flyout; `PartDoc.ctxLocked` = lock all); `PartTab.context` keeps it across document tabs. 1.544.0 converted edges: `EdgeRef.ctx` (`CtxEdge` comp/host/label/`geo` = `CtxEdgeGeo` last seen in this part's coordinates, `locked`; `hint` = edge middle in the other part's coordinates) on a sketch fix rel; kernel `followEdges` projects `ctx.geo` instead of resolving; asm `contextOf` sends `CtxComp.edges` (`CtxCompEdge`: face names, world `pts`, `hint`), `learn` keeps `edgeGeo` (part key → `CtxEdgeSeen[]`, only parts some ctx edge names) and `syncContext` → `syncCtx(doc, plane, edge)` re-finds each with `findCtxEdge` and moves it by `moveEdgeGeo`; part controller draws them (LineSegments in a Group child of `ctxView`, so `ctxAt` only hits meshes), `ctxEdgesAt(x, y)` (screen distance ≤ 8 px, else every edge of the context face) feeds the sketch host's `modelEdges`/`modelEdge`, `ctxEdgeRefs` WeakMap → `edgeRef`; `allCtxRefs` (planes + edges, `set(locked)`) for the External References dialog (Type column), `ctxMark` for the tree; pierce refuses context edges.
- Motion studies (1.94.0, FR-FT-122, P6): `src/motion/` (`types.ts` `MotionStudy` with `tracks` = mate keys / motor / explode / camera, stored in `AsmDoc.motions`; `evaluate.ts` pure: `valueAt`, `motorAmount`, `frameAt`, `cameraAt` (turntable when both keys share `up`), `axisLock` = a partial `lock` mate (`Mate.keep` "rot" | "slide", `along`) that drives an axis mate; `gif.ts` median-cut palette + LZW GIF89a writer; `manager.ts` `MotionManager`: pane `#motion` appended to `#viewport`, frames solved in order and cached (`ensure`), recording composes the canvas after each render (`viewer.onRender`): WebM via `captureStream` + `MediaRecorder`, GIF stepped frame by frame). button `data-mo=keyall`, ruler and lane context menus; `keyInto` keeps a look key's blended `c`). 1.570.0: Autokey (component drags too: asm drag asks `motion.dragFrom(id)` (study frame tfs, null when mated/off) and ends with `motion.dragged(id, tf)` → manager `keyPose` = placement key at the playhead; manager `autoKey`, localStorage `mc:moAutokey`, button `data-mo=autokey` aria-pressed; viewer controls `start` saves `camBefore`, `end` → `keyCamera(before)`: skipped when the view did not move, a new camera track starts with the old view at 0) and camera key properties (key panel `data-p=cpx|cpy|cpz|ctx|cty|ctz|cdist|czoom`; `cdist` dollies the position along the line of sight). 1.572.0: Autokey on mate edits (asm `okMate`: an edited distance/angle mate whose only change is `value` (`onlyValue`) asks `motion.canKeyMate(id)` (Autokey on, no motor on it) and then `motion.mateKey(id, v)` → manager `keyMate` = key on the mate's track at the playhead (a new track holds the mate's value from 0); the mate keeps its value). 1.573.0 (engine motion test): `motion.holdsDrag(id)` (study open and a mate references `id`) makes the asm pointerdown leave the drag to orbit (toast once); asm `bindPanelOk` on `#asm-pm` (pointerdown on ✓/✕ keeps the field focused, ✓ click dispatches the focused field's change first); proximity values cached per study state + pair (`gapCache`, `gapKey`), measured by `measureGaps` in ~60 ms steps with status progress (`gapValues` stays synchronous via `gapSync`), `gapAt` reuses a frame with the same relative placement, `motionGapPts` stops under 1e-4 mm; `interfere.ts` `steadyClashes(per, tfs)` gives a pair the first answer for each relative placement; `trace.ts` `turnAbout`: parallel axis-axis mates plot b's turn about a's axis; camera key `cdist` scales `zoom` by L/v in orthographic keys. 1.576.0: `src/asm/clash.ts` `penetrates` (three-mesh-bvh `bvhcast`: a clash needs an edge of one mesh through a face of the other, both ends and the crossing point more than tol from the face's plane/outline; tol = max(0.005, 1e-3 × the two parts' box diagonals), so face-on-face and pin-in-bore contacts are not clashes) is what asm `clashes(only, deep = true)` uses (motion interference; collision drags keep `intersectsGeometry`, so they stop at the first touch); a Concentric value result plots `angle`; `changeMotions(fn, merge)` keeps quick changes of one panel property (manager `setProp` → `merge` key) in one undo step; `AsmController.fileStem()` names motion downloads; `recordGif` drops a last frame equal to the first. 1.580.0: manager `tfsAt(i, tq)` blends the solved frames floor/ceil(tq·fps) per component with `math.ts` `tweenTf` (screw motion: pivot + axis slide, so parts stay on their arcs; `showAt` solves the next frame too); `Viewer.bareKeep` (an empty set while recording) makes `render` draw only the model, lights, section caps (`userData.sectionCap`) and those objects; GIF options `gifOpts` {w, fps} (localStorage `mc:moGif`, selects `data-mo=gifw|giffps`), GIF and WebM sample at the playback `speed`, `gif.ts` `gifDelays` per-frame delays that add up; `.mo-props` scrolls (max 22vh); asm `clashes(only, deep, memo)`: deep runs `intersectsGeometry` before `penetrates` and keeps answers per pair + relative matrix in the `memo` that `checkInterference` passes through `motionClashes(memo)`.
- Drawings (FRS section 11): `src/drawing/` (`types.ts` `DrawingDoc` with sheets and views, `views.ts` orientations, `projectedDir` for first/third angle and alignment, `sheet.ts` sizes, frame with zones, ISO/ANSI title block and its fields, `prims.ts` sheet primitives in mm with y up, `compose.ts` sheet → primitives, `output.ts` SVG/DXF R12/PDF (jsPDF with DejaVu Sans from `dejavu-fonts-ttf`, so Cyrillic works) and print, `doc.ts` store with undo, `file.ts` `.mcaddrw` (a part's document is kept in `drawing.json`; a STEP model goes in `model/`), `client.ts` + `drawing.worker.ts` (own worker: Modeller for parts, STEP reader for files; projections cached by direction), `workspace.ts` the full-window UI). `src/kernel/drawing.ts` is the exact HLR (`HLRBRep_Algo`, lines and arcs kept exact, hidden lines under visible ones removed by `unhide`). Views store how they were made (orient, or parent + side), never their lines. A part drawing is associative: `main.ts` calls the `partListeners` after each rebuild and the workspace reloads the part when its document differs. Unlike reports, the sheet's labels follow the UI language in every export (what you see is what you get). 1.66.0: section/detail/aux views (`DrawingView.kind`, `line` in parent view axes, `look`, `c`/`r`, `letter`, `hatch`): `views.ts` `sideView` (third-angle view along any sheet direction), `lookDir`, `viewReq` (section key = dir + cut point; detail reuses the parent's), kernel `section` (box cut on the viewer side, cut loops for hatching), `compose.ts` `viewBody` = lines + `childMarks` (cutting lines, detail circles, aux arrows) + `viewLabel`; workspace modes `section`/`detail`/`aux` (`snapIn`, `edgeAt`, `alignedAt` keeps them lined up along `lookDir`). Tests: `tests/unit/drawing.test.ts`, smoke block "Drawings". and "Drawings: section, detail and auxiliary views". 1.68.0: annotations in `Sheet.annots` (`DimAnnot`, `HoleAnnot`, `NoteAnnot`; positions in the view's model axes so they move with it). `GeoRef` names a model edge by the sorted names of the faces it joins (`kernel/drawing.ts` `topology`: `MEdge` edges and `MCyl` cylinders, sent with the load) plus a hint, so dimensions re-attach after a rebuild. `annot.ts` draws them (`dimGeo` → `dimPrims`, `holeText`, `centreMarks`, `placeItems`); `items.ts` `modelItems` turns sketch dims and blind extrude depths into 3D `ModelItem`s in the worker (a dim with `src` takes its points from the current item). Workspace modes `dim`/`hole`/`note`, `pickAt` (points first; end-on seams skipped), `buildDim` (CAD app side rule), `annotHits` for selection/drag. Text prims have `rot` (degrees). Smoke block "Drawings: dimensions…". 1.69.0: `gdt.ts` (`symbolPrims` draws the 14 ISO 1101 characteristics from lines/arcs, `gtolPrims` feature control frames, `datumPrims`, `surfPrims` ISO 1302, `tablePrims` + `cellLabel`: "Column n" and untouched English headers translate) for `GtolAnnot`/`DatumAnnot`/`SurfAnnot`/`TableAnnot` (`anchor: "tr"` = revision table grows left); `annot.ts` `edgeFoot` (nearest point of a GeoRef edge on the sheet), `AnnotOut.dangling` → workspace `.drw-dangle` overlay (screen only, FR-DRW-026); sheet `format: "gost"` (`GOST` cells, `gostBlock`, `tbSize`); `output.ts` `sheetPng` (SVG with the font as a data URI → canvas, pHYs chunk). Smoke block "Drawings: GD&T…". 1.71.0 assembly drawings: `DrawingDoc.source` `{ kind: "asm", doc, flat }` (`flat` = `asm.expanded.doc`, what the kernel builds; main.ts `currentModel`/`liveAsm`); the worker places the shown components with `Assembler` into one compound and names faces `<comp>@<face>` (`compOfKey`); `bom.ts` `bomOf` (top-level rows, pattern/sub instances counted), `BalloonAnnot` (`balloonPrims`, number = `bom.itemOf(comp)`), `autoBalloons` (longest visible edge per item via `onVisible`, ring around the view), BOM = `TableAnnot` kind "bom" with `anchor: "br"` whose rows come from `ctx.bom` when drawn. Smoke block "Drawings: assembly drawing…". 1.73.0: `bomOf(top, flat, mode)` with `TableAnnot.mode` "parts" (rows grouped by part document, `BomRow.comp` = first component); a sheet's balloons follow `bomMode(sheet)` (workspace `boms`, `bomFor`, `ctx(sh)`); `BalloonAnnot.stack` = more component ids drawn as touching circles below (`balloonPrims(..., more)`); `prims.ts` `rotatePrims`/`rotateBox` stand a surface symbol on its edge; GOST `tcontrol`/`ncontrol`/`litera` (`GOST_EDITABLE`, `editableOf(sheet)`, `GCell.ch` one character per cell). 1.77.0: `GRow.composite` (one characteristic cell spans the group in `gtolPrims`), `GtolAnnot.around` (circle at the knee), `TableAnnot.sub` + `sheetBom`/`subsOf` (workspace `boms` is a memo `Map`). 1.78.0: view palette (`palette.ts` `PALETTE`, `thumbPath`; workspace `renderPalette` after each model load, `bindPalette`: HTML5 drag of `[data-pal]` sets a `model` mode previewed on `dragover`, `drop` calls `placeModel`; tiles also carry `data-orient`, so a click starts the usual Model view placement). 1.80.0: side tabs `[data-ptab]` (views palette / Annotation pane `#drw-pane-annot` with `ANNOT_TOOLS`, same `data-an` handlers), inference lines (`infer(p, skip)` snaps to other views' centres within 6 px, `guides` drawn as `.drw-guide` while placing a model view or dragging a free view; `dragMoves` applies it). 1.84.0: crop and break views (`DrawingView.crop` [u0,v0,u1,v1], `breaks` `ViewBreak[]` in model view axes; `compose.ts` `cropOf`, `breakMap` (fwd/inv, a point inside a band lands proportionally in the 6 mm `BREAK_GAP`), `clipHalf`, `trimSegs` (crop, then bands left out and pieces shifted), `breakLines`; `Placement.sq`/`unsq` make `toSheet`/`fromSheet` follow, so annotations and picks need nothing; hatching is made in model axes and trimmed the same way); workspace modes `crop`/`break` (`startTrim`, `trimClick`, `trimMarks`), buttons in the view panel `data-a=crop|vbreak|hbreak|uncrop|unbreak`. 1.86.0: drafting style `DrawingDoc.style` (`DraftStyle` text/arrow/lines); `annot.ts` `useStyle` sets the live `TEXT_H`, arrow size/kind (`arrow()`) and mutates `PEN_W` (line group), called from workspace `ctx()` and `renderDrawing`; sheet panel selects `data-st`. 1.87.0: view properties: `DrawingView.threads` (false hides), `sketches`, `free` (`alignAxis` null; `dragMoves` leaves free children; workspace `realign`); the worker's `modelItems(..., edgeCircle)` also returns `threads` (`ThreadInfo`, edge resolved by `Modeller.resolveEdge`) and `sketches` (`SketchInfo` polylines, shown as in the model); `annot.ts` `threadRun`/`threadPrims` (layer THREAD) and `sketchPrims` (layer SKETCH) go through `compose.ts` `decorPrims` (detail clip + `trimSegs`); previews of one annotation drop `AUTO_LAYERS`. 1.90.0: user sheet formats: `Sheet.tb` (`TitleFormat` w/h, `TbCell` with `TbKey` incl. "projection", `TbLogo` data URL) replaces the ISO/ANSI block (`sheet.ts` `userBlock`, `projSymbol`, `isoFormat`; `tbSize`/`fieldAt` follow it); `prims.ts` image prim `t: "i"` (SVG `<image>`, PDF `addImage`, DXF skipped); `formats.ts` library in localStorage `mc:sheetFormats` + `validFormat` (also run on file load) + JSON export; `tbEditor.ts` dialog `#drw-tb-dlg`; workspace `formatOptions` (`user:<id>`), `editFormat`. 1.96.0: broken-out section (`DrawingView.broken` `{ poly, d }`: outline in view axes, plane at n·p = d; `viewReq` sends `p` + `poly`, key `…|bo|…`; kernel `section(..., poly)` cuts a polygon prism towards the viewer instead of the box, so `cut` loops hatch the floor); workspace mode `brokenout` (`startBroken`, `brokenClick`, `finishBroken` on Enter or the first corner, `depthRange`, `brokenProps`, input `data-v=bdepth`). 1.99.0: alternate position (`DrawingView.alt` `AltMove[]`: top component id, `du`/`dv`/`ang` about `c` in view axes; `views.ts` `altReq`/`altTf` → `DrawingKernel.alt` → worker `alt` moves the kept `placedShapes` (sub-assembly children by id prefix) and projects them alone; `compose.ts` `altPrims` = visible edges on pen/layer `phantom`/`PHANTOM` (DXF linetype PHANTOM); `viewBody(..., alt)`); workspace mode `altpos` (`startAlt`, `altClick`, `altMarks`, `altProps`, inputs `data-v=alt:i:du|dv|ang`, `data-a=altrm`). 1.104.0: dimension kinds `arc` (`DimGeo.a0`/`sw` from the edge ends through its middle, value r·sw, prefix ◠), `chamfer` (shorter line = chamfer, `value` = run along the other, `ang`) and ordinate `oh`/`ov` (refs [base, point], text at the end of the extension line); Drawings exam fixes (Projects/drawings-exam/BUGS.md on the desktop): the part/assembly key handlers return while `#app.drawing` is set (#1); `holeEdges` keeps only circles on a `topo.holes` stack, one per hole (its drill), and hole-table sizes join all callout lines (#5); `nextLetter`/`nextDatum` share letters (#6); output.ts `drawnSymbols` draws ⌴/⌵ as lines for PDF/PNG/DXF (#7); R12 DXF text goes through `dxfText` (#8); a new note focuses its `ntxt` box instead of `prompt` (#9). Exam 2nd batch: annot.ts `cutAway` drops cut-away circles/cylinders from a plain section's centre marks (#4); workspace `cscale` + `addScaleBox` keep the Custom… scale box across `renderProps` (#17).
