Big assemblies (drawings exam round two): kernel `projectAuto` (the worker's views, alternate positions and `section`) runs `projectFast` from `FAST_EDGES` (1500) edges up (`?fasthlr` = always, via the worker's `init`): `projectPoly` at diag/1000 + 0.1 rad, then `exactify` rebuilds chained chords as exact lines (`viewCurves` straight edges) and exact arcs of end-on circles, `mergeLines` joins pieces, `underLines` drops hidden polylines on visible lines; `Projection.fast`. New drawings (1.518.0) open with an empty sheet and the view palette (`newFromModel` places nothing; Standard 3 views = `data-a=std3`; smoke helper `newDrawing3(pg)`); main.ts `warmDrawing` starts the drawing worker 4 s after a model shows (`DrawingKernel.warm`, `sparesOn`, not under webdriver unless `?drwwarm`), palette thumbnails are `low` jobs (`project(dir, true)`: `nextJob` runs a view's first, a view asking for the same key promotes it via `urgent`) and `dropGone` always wants the palette keys (dropping a running one recycled the worker: ~4 s per view placed while the palette drew). Standard 3 views: `views.ts` `std3Layout` tries each scale (enlarged views ≤ 85 % of the room, reductions may fill it) at positions beside/above the title block (`titleBlockAt`/`tbSize`), clear of it. 1.476.0: two drawing workers (`client.ts` `Lane` = a helper worker, started after each `load` of a model under `FAST_EDGES` edges when `helperWanted()` (4+ threads, not ≤ 4 GB, not save-data; `?drwhelper=0|1`), loaded with `lite` (no topology/items/tables); exact `project`/`section`/`alt` requests go through `exact` → `pump`: one at a time per worker, the helper takes jobs without `cfg`, a failed helper job goes back to the main worker; helper stopped after 60 s idle; `helperState()`, debug `drw.helper()`), and `projectFast` falls back when the polygonal HLR gives no visible lines: `BRepTools.Clean` + diag/300 mesh, then the exact `project` (`Projection.fail` = getter errors, logged). Worker errors: `occtMessage` (OCJS `getStandard_FailureData`), workspace `kernelError` explains a bare `OpenCASCADE error <n>`. 1.478.0: worker recycling (projection replies carry `heap`; past `HEAP_RECYCLE` 1.2 GB (`?drwheap=MB`) the client's `recycle()` starts the main worker over between calls with the stored `src`, keeping the projection cache and the `configs` facts (1.582.0: the worker builds a configuration when a request names one it lacks, `shapeOf` → `buildConfig`); `call` waits on `recycling`; an exact job failing with `OUT_OF_MEMORY` is retried once after a recycle; `kernelError` says not enough memory). The restart loads `lite` (facts kept), and `onRestart`/`restarting` let the status line say so; workspace `track(key, p)` counts exact projections and alt requests in flight (`projecting`) for "Projecting the views… n to go". Cause: `projectPoly` leaks ~100 MB per call on an engine assembly even with every wrapper deleted (the leak is inside `HLRBRep_PolyHLRToShape`'s compounds). 1.480.0: `polyEdges`/`viewCurves` delete every wrapper and `projectPoly` reuses one `HLRBRep_PolyHLRToShape` per oc (`polyReader`; a deleted one keeps its memory), ~100 → ~40 MB per fast view; the rest is OCCT's `HCompound_1`. 1.481.0: `topology` checks hole ends against the hole's own solid (`Bore.s`, lines in `holeStacks` kept per solid, `out(p, solid)`), with one `BRepClass3d_SolidClassifier_2` per solid made once and a box test first (engine assembly 45 s → 1 s); assembly hole callouts equal each part's own. 1.482.0: a full load returns a `ModelSnap` (binary BREP from `kernel/drawing.ts` `shapeBin` + names, materials, placed component ids); the client keeps it and sends it with every `lite` load (recycle, helper), which the worker reads (`readSnap`: `readBin` + `childShapes`) instead of rebuilding (engine 12 s → 0.3 s; `helperState().snap` = its bytes). 1.483.0: binary, and the first fast `project` of the whole shape sends the snapshot again with its triangulation (`shapeBin(..., true)`, `mesh`, worker `snapMeshed`; client `onmessage` takes `d.snap` from projection replies), so a restart skips the ~6 s engine mesh (11 MB). 1.487.0: workspace `renderSheetSoon` (one microtask redraw for everything that lands at once) after projections, previews and alt requests, each asked once (`projecting`, `previewAsked`), so arriving views no longer redraw the sheet once per earlier render (engine sheet 66 redraws → 9, 60 s → 40 s; debug `drw.renders()`); `renderPalette` starts in a `setTimeout` so the sheet's previews go to the worker first. 1.491.0: kernel `section` moves the cutting plane `SECTION_NUDGE` (1e-3 mm) towards the viewer (OCCT's cut is pathologically slow with the plane on a revolved face's axis) and `cutSolids` cuts each solid alone (bbox wholly behind = kept, wholly in front = dropped; `CutPart` keeps `cutter.Modified` per solid for `sectionEdges`, which moves points back), `shapeBox` = bbox only; client `drop(keep)` rejects queued jobs whose key is gone (`DROPPED`, ignored by workspace `viewFailed`), stops the helper and recycles the main worker when the running job is gone; workspace `dropGone` after every store change, `lastShown` (last exact projection per view, drawn as a preview while a restyled one comes), Auto balloon toasts while the view is not projected; debug `drw.drop()`. 1.494.0: section previews (kernel `section` = `sectionCut` (the Booleans, `SectionCut`) + `sectionFinish` (exact lines, cut edges, frees); `sectionPreview` = `projectPoly` of the cut at diag/300 with the same `cut` loops; worker request `secpreview` keeps the cut in `stash` keyed by the section's fields, and the following `section` request with the same key reuses it (freed on the next section key or `load`); client `preview(req)` sends `secpreview` when `req.p`, `sectionPreviewsMade`; workspace `previewFor` no longer skips sections, `renderOverlay` draws a placed view's preview, `warmSides` asks both sides' previews before their exact lines; `stash` keeps up to two cuts). 1.495.0: `shapeBox` keeps each shape's tight box in a `WeakMap` by wrapper (`AddOptimal` took ~1 s on the engine for every view); `shapeInfo`, `projectAuto`, `alignedShape` and the worker's shading and previews use it. 1.497.0 meshed fast views at 0.3 rad; 1.510.0 put `FAST_ANGLE` back to 0.1, since OCCT's polygonal HLR never returned on the engine's Top/Left views at 0.3 (Standard 3 views stuck at "2 to go"; 0.1 = 45 s). 1.501.0: a drawing of the assembly open in 3D loads from that assembly's own build: workspace `loadModel` asks host `asmSnap(flat)` (main.ts → `AsmController.drawingSnap`: null unless `flat` equals `expanded.doc`, after the build in flight) → `ModelKernel.drawingSnap` → kernel worker `asmSnap` (shown placed shapes as `shapeBin` + names, `placed` with `part`/`name`/mats) → `DrawingKernel.load(src, pre)` sends it as `snap` on a full load; the drawing worker's `fromAsm` reads it and only computes topology (engine 11 s → 0.1 s + 1.2 s topology), reply `pre: true` (`preLoads` in `drw.helper()`); on a mismatch it builds as before. 1.512.0: workspace `auxPick` lets Auxiliary fold about a sheet sketch line (`drawnLineAt`: line, rect side or poly leg; parent = view under the click, else the nearest), stored like an edge in the parent's model axes, before trying `edgeAt`. 1.504.0: `sideViewAt` places section/aux views by the sheet's projection (first angle: the view goes where the arrows point, `look` = −side; third angle: on the side it looks from); `compose.ts` `CUT_RUN` (8 mm) runs cutting lines out so their 6 mm thick ends clear the view. 1.509.0: `?drwangle=<rad>` overrides `FAST_ANGLE` (worker init → `setFastAngle`; each fast view logs its mesh + HLR and exact-curve times); an empty fast view retries at 0.1 rad, then diag/300 at 0.5 rad (each after `BRepTools.Clean`), and falls back to the exact HLR only under `EXACT_FALLBACK_EDGES` (4000; `projectAuto` passes the count), else throws `NO_FAST_LINES` (workspace `kernelError` explains it): the exact HLR on the engine held the worker for 20+ min (exam round five). 1.515.0: side views of round parts: workspace `annotPick` (= `pickAt(..., edgeOn = true)`) for every leader tool and `pickRef` (an outline pick keeps `s`; hole mode keeps `m.s`); `pickAt` skips seams (a line whose key is one round face's name, `MCyl.f`/`MRev.f`); `annot.ts` `edgeFoot` puts a circle reference that is not end-on on `refLine` (outline or edge-on line), `leaderEnd` (note, gtol) = that line's middle, balloons the same, `holePrims(..., side)` ends the leader on the hole's line; previews pass the view's `cyls`. Cone outlines (exam #50): `MRev.r0`/`r1` (cone radius at v0/v1), `GeoRef.rv` = `e` names the cone face, `h` = `coneMid`, `s` the side; `annot.ts` `coneOutline` (used by `refLine`, so linear, angle and chamfer dims and leaders take it); `pickAt` offers them as line picks; `buildDim` makes an angle from two non-parallel lines of any kind (outline, edge-on circle), `dimGeo` ang/chamfer read lines through `refLine`. Tests `tests/unit/drawing-side.test.ts`, smoke block "annotations on the side view of a round part". 1.551.0: drawing command bar tabs (`CMD_TABS` layout/annot/sketch: buttons `[data-ctab]` in `.drw-cfoot` under the bar, panes `.drw-cg[data-cpane]`, `showCmdTab`, localStorage `mc:drwTab`; `BAR_LAYOUT` lists the Annotation tab: every tool once, `{ menu: "dims" | "tables" }` ▾ groups; the side panel has no Annotation pane any more, only the view palette, tree and properties; `renderStatus` switches to a started tool's tab; ▾ menus are `position: fixed` placed by `placeMenu`, since `.drw-cmds` scrolls and clipped them; smoke helpers `drwCmd(pg, a)` / `drwTool(pg, k)` pick the tab and menu) and cross holes (kernel `topology` gives a non-circle edge lying on a cylinder face `MEdge.rg` `Ring[]` = that cylinder's circle (`ringOn`, smallest radius only; `reach` takes curve samples so the hole cylinder reaches its saddle rim); `annot.ts` `ringTopo(topo, n)` (memoised; used by `viewAnnots` and workspace `factsOf`) turns them into `"c"` edges for a view along the ring axis, and into `rim: true` stand-ins side-on (no edge-on line, but the cylinder's outline picks); tests `tests/unit/drawing-crosshole.test.ts`, smoke "cross hole in a round shaft").

Smoke helpers `annotDlgOk(pg)` / `annotDlgGone(pg)`. 1.600.0: `GtolAnnot.dim` may also name a hole callout or a note (`annot.ts` `isGtolHost`, `HostBox` {box, al}: dims centred, a callout lines up with its text side (`holePrims` returns `al`), a note by its `al`; `heldFrames` in `viewAnnots` and `sheetNotes`; workspace `hostAt`/`gtolHost`); `clearOfLines` moves a held frame off its host's lines (`hostSegs`: level ones push it down, steep ones ending within 3 mm of the text push it sideways); `DimAnnot.lstyle` "straight" / `bend` (shoulder length, sheet mm) for radius/diameter text outside the circle, panel select `data-d=lstyle` ("" / straight / along) + input `data-d=bend`. 1.562.0: `GtolAnnot.dim` (dimension id in the same view: `viewAnnots` holds such frames back and draws them at `gtolUnder(a, box, dec)` = centred `GTOL_UNDER` under the dimension's text box, stacking; `pos` unused while attached; workspace `dimAt`/`gtolDim`: a gtol-mode click on a dimension attaches, a drag of the frame drags the dimension, `renderAnnotDrag` takes the frames along, a free frame dropped on a dimension attaches, panel `data-a=gdetach`, `deleteAnnot` removes a dimension's frames; debug `drw.hit(id)`).
- Half-working features sweep (1.601.0): `DatumAnnot.on` (dimension or tolerance frame; `view` absent on a sheet-level frame) and `SurfAnnot.on` (dimension); `annot.ts` `canHold`, `segFoot` (nearest host line, kept inside its ends), `onHosts` drawn after `heldFrames` from the hosts' `segs` / frame boxes (`frames`), also in `sheetNotes`; workspace `holderAt` on the first click of datum/surf mode, `deleteAnnot` removes everything hanging on the deleted one (frames, datums, finishes, transitively). Drawing Ctrl+C / Ctrl+V: module-level `clipAnnot`, `copyAnnot`/`pasteAnnot` at `lastPt` (tied annotations only into their own view). Sweep scripts `sweep-vault.mjs`, `sweep-display.mjs`, `sweep-file.mjs`; hands-on `convert.mjs`. - Weldments (FR-FT-121, P6): `src/weldments/` (`profiles.ts` EN/DIN section library as rounded-polygon loops centred on their box, `profileArea`, `anchorPoint`; `types.ts` `member`/`trimext`/`endcap`/`gusset`/`weldbead` features and `CutItem`; `kernel.ts` runs inside `Modeller.evaluate` through `WeldHost` (the modeller's private helpers): one solid per member segment, faces `<id>:<ent>:side:k|start|end`, mitre = half-space box on the bisector plane, butt = the through member extended, the other cut square to its face; Trim/Extend fuses a prism up to the boundary, cuts it and runs `unify`; `cutList` (worker → `Rebuild.cutList`) groups members by profile/length/end angles, plates by size, beads by leg; `refs.ts` feeds `featureRefs`; `ui.ts` Weldments ▾ flyout, panels (`weldPanel`/`weldChange`/`bindWeldPanel`), picks (`weldPick`, focus `wbodies`/`wbound`/`wfaces`/`wf1`/`wf2`), `#wld-cut` dialog + CSV). `rememberWeld` from controller `ok()` for new weld features, `recallWeld` wraps `weldDraft`'s drafts; localStorage `mc:weldLast:<type>`). 1.396.0: beads on any root curve (kernel `weldBead`: `normalAt` falls back to `ShapeAnalysis_Surface.ValueOfUV` + `BRepLProp_SLProps` for other surfaces; prisms/revolves only for lines/circles between flat/cylindrical faces (`simple`), other roots get `Run.sec(d)` (sections by arc length via `GCPnts_AbscissaPoint`); a chain of such runs is lofted per window in one `pushLoft` (ThruSections, `CheckCompatibility(false)`, else the sections turn as the root goes round; a whole closed chain repeats its first section, so the ring has no caps), since OCCT's fuse dropped one of two touching lofts; bead joins are checked by volume, retried with `glueAll` (GlueShift), else left as pieces). 1.410.0: weld table on drawings (`WeldTableAnnot` `t: "wtable"`, `view`, `pos`, `prec`; the drawing worker's `buildPart` runs `cutList` when the part has a weld bead and sends the bead items (less `cutProps` `excl`) as `ModelFacts.welds` `WeldInfo` {legs, inter, length, qty}; `annot.ts` `weldRows`/`WELD_COLS` (item, continuous/intermittent/staggered, `z<legs>`, length, qty, then a total row when 2+); workspace mode `wtable` like `btable`, props `data-d=wprec`). 1.422.0: cut list table (`CutTableAnnot` `t: "ctable"`, `view`, `pos`, `prec`, `beads` false = no bead rows, `mass`; the drawing worker sends every non-excluded item of `splitSubs(cutList(...))` as `ModelFacts.cuts` `CutInfo` {item = the dialog's number, kind, desc (typed `cutProps` desc, `own`), length, a1/a2, plate, inter, qty, kg = volume × item/part density}; `annot.ts` `cutCols`/`cutRows` (plate and bead texts translated with the cut list dialog's keys); workspace mode `ctable` like `wtable`, props `data-d=cbeads|cmass|cprec`). 1.431.0: cut list balloons (the drawing worker also sends `ModelFacts.cutFaces` = face name → cut list item, from `bodyInfo` names; `annot.ts` `edgeItem(k, ctx)` (BOM component, else the first face of the edge key in `cutFaces`) and `stackItem` (`cut:<item>` stack entries) number balloons, `cutDesc` is the table's description; `bom.ts` `autoBalloons` takes `BalloonItems` (`bomItems(bom)` for assemblies); workspace `cuts` (no BOM), `balloonItems()`, `cutStackRows`). 1.441.0: weld tags (`BalloonAnnot.weld` = number from `ModelFacts.weldFaces` face name → weld table row via `annot.ts` `weldItem`, stack entries `weld:<row>`; `BalloonItems.mine` keeps weld tags and cut list balloons apart in `autoBalloons`; workspace `tagBeads(view)` runs when a wtable is placed and from its panel button `data-a=wtags`, tags are `shape: "box"`). 1.565.0: cut list masses from body materials (`types.ts` `itemDensity(c, p, bodies, density)` = item `mat`, else `bodiesDensity` of its bodies, used by the dialog, the CSV and the drawing worker; `bodyMatName` = the bodies' one material name, "" = the part's, null = mixed → option label *Body material (…)* / *Body materials (mixed)*, CSV `Mixed`). 1.566.0: `CutTableAnnot.mat` Material column before Mass (`CutInfo.mat` from `types.ts` `itemMatText` = own, else the bodies' distinct materials with the part's for bodies with none, joined ", "; panel checkbox `data-d=cmat`). 1.567.0: Model items bring weld symbols (`WeldInfo.sym` = `types.ts` `beadSym(f)` of the bead feature, found as the bead body's key; workspace `insertItems` (async) runs `autoBalloons` with `weldItem` per view in order and turns each into `weld.ts` `beadWeld(b, row, w)`, `WeldAnnot.row` = weld table row, one per row per sheet). 1.569.0: `cutList` lists bodies no weld feature owns (`other`: a slab = largest flat face (`plateTop`) + a parallel flat face at t with area × t within 2 % of the volume → `plate`, else `CutItem.kind` "body" with `box` = edge-point extents along X/Y/Z, longest first, key `o|…`; `cutKey` appends the box; order member, plate, body, bead; texts "Body {l} × {w} × {h}"). 1.574.0 (weldments exam, Projects/weldments-exam/BUGS.md on the desktop): `cutProps[key].bodies` = the item's body keys when typed; `types.ts` `cutPropsOf(items, props)` (own key first, else the properties of a key no current item has whose bodies share one with the item, same kind) is used by the dialog, tree, CSV, stock and the drawing worker, so properties follow a length change (#17); the weld table keeps beads excluded from the cut list (#14); the drawing worker's `usedMats` is undefined when a body has no material (#13). `Sk3Point.at` {sketch, pt} (controller `click3d` → `sketchPointAt`: points of shown 2D sketches before the 3D sketch, 8 px; the point is fixed) → kernel `follow3d` moves it there and runs `solve3d` (E-3D-FOLLOW on a conflict, warning when the point is gone), returned in `Rebuild.sketches` (now 2D or 3D) and taken over by controller `followEdges` with `amend`; `featureRefs` lists the sketch; unfix or typed coordinates drop `at` (#6). `TrimExtendFeature.more` (kind body: more boundary bodies, `weldPick` toggles them by member segment or face name; kernel `trimExtend` gives each member end its nearest boundary and trims both ends when they differ) (#4). `Viewer.extraBounds` (part controller: `shownBox` = the sketches and curves `drawDecor` drew, `boxing` while `lines` draws them, plus the open sketch's `sketchBox`) is added by `computeBounds` and `updateClipRange` (#1). Tree multi-select (#12): controller `treePicks` = body:/cut: rows Ctrl+clicked beside `selected` (`multiRow`, cleared by a plain click or when `selected` is another kind), drawn by `drawFeatureFaces`; `openMenu` acts on all (`setBody(keys[])` / `pickBodyMaterial(keys[])` one undo step, Hide bodies, sub-weldment items with the union). 1.575.0 (#8): Locate profile fits along the profile normal (`pierceSpots` → `n`, `Viewer.fit(animate, box, along)`); a wheel over any `.mc-handle` in `#mc-layer` is re-dispatched to the canvas. Tree ICONS cover weld types (fallback •); `weldDraft` member ticks only segments no unsuppressed member uses. 1.577.0: `CutTableAnnot.part` (Part number column after Qty, `CutInfo.part` from `cutProps`), `CutInfo.sub` (sub-weldment name: `cutRows` puts a "Sub-weldment: {name}" row before its items), `annot.ts` `wrapCell` wraps Material cells at 24 characters and `gdt.ts` `tablePrims` takes "\n" in a cell (row height + `LINE_H` per line); `stockTotals` `left` = sum of the cutting plan's offcuts (#11). Exam #16: `Modeller.onFeature(i, of, name)` before each feature built again; the worker posts `{ id: -2, progress }` at most every 150 ms → `ModelKernel.onProgress` → controller `showProgress` (`#mc-busy` text + `--p` bar); controller `fullData` (last mode-none result) is shown again with `canvas.mc-stale` (greyed) 250 ms into the first rebuild after a rolled-back view (`shownRolled`, `rebuildGen`). Exam #2/#7/#9/#10: `DEFAULT_SNAPS` has "mid"; editor `snapPointRaw` lands an H/V-held point on a hit curve (`intersections` with the guide line) keeping `hv` + `curve`; 3D sketch list `[data-s3sel]` plain click = only that item, Ctrl/Shift toggles, `[data-s3picked]` lists them; `ui.ts` `WARN_MS` 10 s for warn toasts; the sketch panel plane label is `refLabel(sk.plane)` (`.pm-skplane`). 1.581.0 (speed): `trimExtend` leaves the extension prism out when it changes nothing: a plane cut whose half-space holds the whole end outline (`gone`), a planar trim whose contact `t` < −1e-3 without it, a body cut whose kept piece has only `side:` faces, the other end and the boundary's faces (tool faces named `<end>~` first, then renamed; else extend and cut as before); `contactAlong` = `BRepAlgoAPI_Section` edge samples + each shape's edge samples `stateAt` IN the other, instead of the Common; `mergeBodies` tries `glueAll` (kept at one solid of the summed volume). Checked identical with `bench/names.bench.ts` (now also body keys); `bench/feat.bench.ts` = time per feature. Booleans between rounded tubes stay 0.5 to 1.5 s each in WASM (corner cylinders crossing); a multibody hole cut split per solid was no faster, and one-shot bead fuses renamed merged bead faces (left as is).
- View navigation (1.107.0, FRS 13.13 FR-UI-101..104): `src/viewer/navigation.ts` `bindNavigation` (own window key listener, acts only when the page or the canvas has focus; Zoom to area takes the pointer in the window capture phase and turns the orbit controls off; Space picker `.nav-pick`, buttons `data-nv`), `Viewer` `rotateView`/`panView`/`zoomView`/`zoomToRect`/`previousView` (camera history `views`, pushed by `animateTo` and the controls' `start`, cleared on a fitted `load`), `animating`; main.ts `selectionBox` feeds Zoom to selection.
- Commands (1.107.0, FRS 13.13): `OPTION_KEYS` + `rememberOptions`/`recallOptions` (localStorage `mc:opts:<command>`, choices only) for FR-UI-043; `DocStore.history` + controller `stepName`/`undoTip`/`historyMenu` (Undo ▾ = `data-fly="undo"`); `lastCmd`/`activeCmd` set in `command()`, Enter in `key()` repeats; `ok(repeat)` + `pinned` (localStorage `mc:pin`, panel button `data-k=pin`) reopen the command after the rebuild; `commandMenu`/`okItems` put OK/Cancel on a right-click without drag (before the fillet edge helpers); `renderWhatsWrong` fills `#mc-errors` (`.mc-wrong-h`, rows `data-wid`).
- Command bar (1.121.0, FR-UI-117..119): `src/commandBar.ts` (`CmTabId`, `setCmTabs`/`selectCmTab`/`onCmTab`; the tab used last is kept per tab set). `#cm` sits under the header; each pane in `#cm-body` names the tabs it serves in `data-cm-pane` (`#cm-eval` = measure tools, `#mc-tools`, `#asm-tools`), and its owner re-renders on `onCmTab`. main.ts `syncCmTabs` picks the tabs from `#app`'s class; part `renderBar` builds the tab's groups (`CM_FLY` flyouts), `renderQuick` fills `#mc-quick`, the assembly fills `#asm-quick`. New modules add a tab or a group with `mc-b` buttons, never their own bar. 1.451.0 (FR-SK-004): while sketching only the Sketch tab shows the sketch tools (it ends with Extrude/Cut/Revolve); the other tabs keep their commands, and `startFeature` exits the sketch and selects it first; `runCmd` looks only on the Sketch tab while `#app.sketching`. Smoke helper `runCmd(page, label)` finds a command on any tab or flyout. Make assembly from part = `AsmController.fromPart` via host `openPart`. Ctrl+K search and `src/sketch/check.ts` (`checkSketch`, panel `.pm-skchk`) came in the same release, as did typed sizes while drawing (editor `typedFields`/`placeTyped`).
- File menu (1.327.0, FR-UI-010): `src/fileMenu.ts` `FileMenu` (`#file-menu`, opened by the header's `#file-btn`, which replaced Open file, and by the drawing bar's `data-a=file` via `DrawingHost.fileMenu`); main.ts supplies the actions as a `FileHost` (`fileMenuLive` = what is shown, `saveLive`, Save all activates each dirty tab in turn via `DocTabs.dirtyTabs`, `print3d`, `packDocs`). Shortcuts in a window capture listener (Ctrl+O, Ctrl+P, Ctrl+Alt+N/W/S; Ctrl+S / Ctrl+Shift+S stay with the controllers). Pack and Go = dialog `#pack-dlg`, every open document (+ an assembly's parts under `parts/`) in one zip. Recent documents: `src/recent.ts` keeps the files themselves in IndexedDB `mc-recent` (8 entries, ≤ 64 MB each; a group opened together is one entry), added by `openFiles` and the `saved` host hooks of the part, assembly and drawing.
- Sketch text (1.137.0, FR-SK-020): `text` sketch entity (`p` start point, `text`, `h` capital height, `ang`, `font`, `bold`/`italic`, `gl` = cached outline keyed by `textKey`, so profiles and the kernel need no font); `src/sketch/text.ts` (`textLoops` = lines + 4-pole spline segs, `textPolys`, `textBox`; `textOutline` loads DejaVu via `?url` and three's opentype parser); editor `placeText`/`setText` commit once the outline is ready; panel `textBox`/`bindTextBox` (`data-tx`). `scripts/unit.mjs` maps `?url` imports to file paths.
- 1.139.0: Section at the sketch plane (FR-SK-006: controller `skSection` + `syncSketchSection` from `renderBar`, host `sketchSection` → a "Sketch" `SectionManager` plane in main.ts) and the configuration table (FR-CFG-003: `configs.ts` `configTable`/`applyConfigTable`/`parseCsv`, column heads `$STATE@Feature`, `key@Feature`, `D1@Sketch`, `$VAR@name`, `$MATERIAL`; controller `importConfigTable`).
- 1.141.0: Geometry pattern option (FR-FT-074): `CopySource.vary` (feature copies only); kernel `copyBy(..., { doc, frames })` → `varyCopies` re-runs `sweep` for extrude/revolve seeds with the sketch frame moved by each instance transform (id `<fid>:<tag>`, its tools collected then dropped from `this.tools`), other seeds copied; panel checkbox `data-k=geometry`.
- 1.456.0: extrude Direction 2 with any end condition (FR-FT-011): `ExtrudeFeature.end2` (`Dir2End`, absent = blind by `depth2`; set whenever Direction 1 is not blind, so a stale `depth2` in older files stays off) + `upTo2`/`upToOffset2`/`reverseOffset2`/`upToVertex2`/`upToBody2`; `types.ts` `hasDir2`, `dir2Apart` (blind both ways stays one prism), `dir2Feature` (Direction 2 as a one-way extrude the other way); kernel `sweep` → `extrudeTools` per direction, Direction 2's far cap renamed `:start`, the halves of the side faces joined by `unifyAlike`; controller focus `upto2`/`upbody2`, `extrudeTarget` returns `upto2|vertex2|body2`, the `depth2` drag arrow (`dir2Blind`). Tests `tests/unit/extrudeDir2.test.ts`. 1.460.0: draft per direction (FR-FT-014): `ExtrudeFeature.draft2` (present, even at 0° = off, means each direction tapers on its own from the sketch plane; set by the panel when Direction 2 or the draft is turned on, absent in older files whose one draft tapers the whole two-way prism); `types.ts` `draftApart` makes `dir2Apart` true for blind both ways, `dir2Feature` takes `draft2` (blind only); Direction 2 side faces become `fid:side2:<ent>` when `draft2` is set; panel `draft2On`/`draft2.angle`/`draft2Out` in the blind Direction 2 group.
- 1.421.0: Selected Contours (FR-FT-010): `ExtrudeFeature`/`RevolveFeature.contours` = a point (sketch coordinates) inside each picked region, absent = the plain profile. `src/sketch/regions.ts` (pure: `sketchRegions` = sampled curves cut at crossings, dangling ends dropped, faces traced, clockwise outlines become holes; `regionAt`, `regionPoint`); kernel `profileFaces(..., contours)` → `contourFaces` (a big plane face split by every exact curve with `BRepAlgoAPI_Splitter`, pieces holding the points by `BRepClass_FaceClassifier`, edges named by the nearest curve), `unifyAlike` joins same-named halves after the fuse. Controller `contourRegions`/`needsContours` (profile error + regions → required box, so it gets focus), `pickContour` (ray on the sketch plane), `drawContours` (sketch + orange fills), `contoursGroup` (`data-focus=contours`, `data-k=clearContours`). 1.463.0: picked fills/outlines are `CONTOUR_BOSS` orange, or `CONTOUR_CUT` (the cut preview's yellow) when `op === "cut"`; fill meshes carry `userData.contourCut`. 1.428.0: `PlaneSurfFeature.contours` (`ContourFeature`, `hasContours`, `sketchRegionsOk`), hover pre-highlight `hoverContour` (`ctHoverGroup`, mesh `userData.contourHover`, from `bindHover`; `regionMesh` shared with `drawContours`). `ui.ts` `askFileName` (`#name-dlg`) names a first download save (exam #9).
- 1.142.0: text along a curve (FR-SK-020): `text` entity `along` (curve id) + `flip`; profile.ts `textPath(ents, e)` samples the curve into a polyline, and text.ts `placer(e, p, path)` puts each outline point at arc length s0 + h·x (s0 = nearest point to `p`, closed curves wrap) lifted h·y along the left normal; every `textLoops`/`textPolys`/`textBox` caller passes `textPath`. `textBox` is a polygon (baseline out, top back; the middle point is the top end). Panel `alongField` (`data-tx=along|flip`).
- 1.144.0: slot tools (FR-SK-017): editor `cslot` (centre + end, `slotCentres`), `aslot`/`aslot3` (`src/sketch/slots.ts`: `centreArcAxis`/`threePointArcAxis`, `arcSlotEnts` = construction centre arc, outer/inner arcs on its centre, caps tangent (`inner` on the outer arc) and equal); `slotSizes` adds width (cap diameter) + length/radius dims when `slotDims` (localStorage `mc:slotDims`), `openSizes` chains `editDim(id, then)`. Solver: arc–arc tangency at a shared end is the collinear-radii form (the distance form has a zero gradient there); rank threshold 1e-6 (central-difference noise).
- 1.152.0: 3-point rectangles and parallelogram (FR-SK-013 P2): editor tools `rect3`/`crect3`/`pgram`, corners from `src/sketch/shapes.ts` `quadCorners`; rels perpendicular (not pgram) + two parallels, `crect3` adds a construction diagonal with its midpoint on the centre.
- 1.157.0: conics and equation curves (FR-SK-021): `spline` entities with `kind` conic/parabola (pts = start, top vertex, end; `rho`) or eqn (pts = ends, `eq` text with `mode` xy/param, `flip` set by mirror); `src/sketch/conic.ts` `entBezier` gives every spline kind's Bézier poles (use it, not `splineBezier`, for entities), `entConic` the exact rational quadratic that profile segments carry as `rq` and `segEdge` builds as a rational `Geom_BSplineCurve_2` (cubic Bézier chains with many spans throw OCCT's planar mass properties off by ~1e-3). Editor tools `conic`/`parabola` (clicks) and `eqn` (panel `curveBox`, `addEquation`, `setCurve`).
- 1.164.0: style spline (FR-SK-019 P5): `spline` `kind: "style"` = the points are the control polygon of a clamped uniform B-spline of degree min(3, n − 1) (`conic.ts` `styleBspline`; `styleBezier` = Boehm knot insertion + degree raise, so `entBezier` covers it); profile segments carry `bs` {poles, knots, mults, deg} (reversed with the segment) and `segEdge` builds one `Geom_BSplineCurve_1`. Editor tool `sspline` (Line ▾), never closes; the control polygon is drawn for every `hasControl` entity. OCCT mass properties on multi-span B-spline faces stay ~2e-4 off (adaptive `VolumeProperties_2` was worse).
- 1.167.0: perimeter circle (FR-SK-014 P2): editor tool `pcircle` (Circle ▾); each click records the snapped curve (`draft.curves`, hover `draft.hoverCurve`), `perimFit` turns them into `PerimItem`s and `shapes.ts` `perimeterCircle` runs Newton from the circle through the clicks (tangency sides fixed by that start circle, `inner` for circle-in-circle); the circle gets tangent rels to the curves and onCurve to clicked points.
- 1.215.0: spline tangent and equal curvature (FR-SK-050/051): solver `curveJoint` (ends shared or held by a coincident rel, `System.coin`), `endGeo` = outgoing tangent + signed curvature (splines from the end Bézier piece of `entBezier`), `jointTerms` (tangent: outgoing tangents antiparallel; `curvature` rel adds k0 + k1 = 0 scaled by the size); interpolating splines have natural ends (k = 0), so Equal curvature to an arc needs a style spline or conic. Editor `validRelations` offers both for a spline + line/arc/spline with a joint. Sketch orientation (FR-SK-002): `SketchFeature.xAxis` `{ edge, flip }`; kernel `frameOf` projects the straight edge onto the plane (pointing the way of the plane's X first), else warns and keeps the plane frame; Edit sketch plane mode `edit.xAxis`/`pickX` (`pickSketchX`, panel `data-a=xpick|xflip|xclear`); `featureRefs` lists the edge's faces.
- 1.234.0: pierce relation (FR-SK-051): `SkRel` `t: "pierce"`, e = [point], `ext` = the model edge; the solver holds the point like a fix; `external.ts` `piercePoint` (lines infinite, arcs as whole circles, other curves by `pts`, nearest crossing); kernel `followEdges` → `Modeller.pierce` (exact for lines/circles, curves sampled + bisected), no crossing = dangling; editor `validRelations` offers it for one point, `startPierce`/`pierceAt` (convert tool, `piercing`) replace the point's fix; `reattachAt` handles pierce rels.
- 1.240.0: linked sketch offsets (FR-SK-034): `SkRel` `t: "offset"` e = [source, copy, then (source end, copy end) pairs at open chain ends], `of` = the lead rel whose |offset| the pair keeps (solver: line = signed distance of the copy's start from the source line, arcs/circles = radius difference; ends held on the normal through the source end); the lead pair's dimension is a line-line distance or `SkDim.off` (radius difference, `dimPoints` along the ray to the text). Editor `offsetAt` → `offsetSide` per side (`OffsetBuild`/`OffsetMade`), options `offsetBoth`/`offsetCap`/`offsetLink`, `capOut`/`capPreview`; panel `data-a=oboth|ocap|olink`.
- 1.251.0: automatic relations while drawing (FR-SK-052): editor `snapPoint` returns a `Snap` with `rel` from `inferDir` (perpendicular to the chain's previous line, tangent to its previous arc, parallel/perpendicular to `inferLine` = the last line hovered with the line tool); `drawClick` adds `[ref, line]`; `suppressed()` = Ctrl/Meta held or `autoRels` off (localStorage `mc:autoRels`); `showInfer` draws `.sk-infer` by the cursor. Relation glyphs (FR-SK-053): `showRels` (`mc:skRels`), hovering a `.sk-rel` sets `hoverRel` → `relHot` entities drawn in the hover colour. Panel checkboxes `data-a=autorels|showrels`.
- 1.266.0: sketch drag habits: drag to merge (FR-SK-043: `drag.point` + `mergeTarget` = point/origin → coincident, else curve → onCurve, own entities excluded; drop adds it via `tryAdd`, Ctrl releases free), copy by drag (FR-SK-044: Ctrl+pointerdown on a selected item starts `drag.copy`; `copyItems(d)` copies the selection plus relations/dims wholly inside it, no fix/pierce/ext; a Ctrl+click without moving deselects via `untoggle`), drags start after 3 px; status bar cursor x, y (FR-SK-047: `cursorUV` in `statusHint`, skipped while re-attaching or piercing).
- 1.275.0: sketch context menu (FR-SK-045): controller `sketchMenu` on a right-button pointerup without drag in a sketch (the `contextmenu` default is prevented there) shows editor `contextMenu(e)` rows (selects the item under the cursor first; line tool mid-chain = `doubleClick`, returns null) plus Exit sketch / Discard; editor `selectionBox` feeds `sketchMenu` Zoom to selection and main.ts `selectionBox` via `sketchSelectionBox`.
- 1.282.0: Instant 2D (FR-SK-067): editor `grip` (`.sk-grip` in the label layer, `syncGrip` from `syncLabels`, placed by `placeLabels`) on the one selected driving dimension (`gripGeo`: linear = dimension line end at the second point, `dir` from the first, k = 2 for `dia`; radius/diameter = rim towards the text; none for angles, ordinates, `off` or `val.expr`); `gripDown` drags with `gripDrag` (base copy, zoom step, Shift ÷10), solves a copy per step, render draws ruler ticks; `endGrip` commits one "Change dimension" or restores (Esc). FR-SK-068: right-click while placing (`contextMenu`, tool dim with `dimDraft.dim`) toggles `dimDraft.lock`, which makes `placeDimDraft` keep the type (label `.locked`).
- 1.290.0: quick snaps (FR-SK-046): `src/sketch/snaps.ts` (`SnapKind`, `SNAP_KINDS` labels, `DEFAULT_SNAPS`, `parseSnaps` for localStorage `mc:skSnaps`; pure `quadrants`/`intersections`/`tangentPoints`/`perpendicularFoot` over `SnapCurve` segments and circles/arcs). Editor `snaps` (preferences filter `snapPoint`: end vs centre points via `centreIds`, nearest, hv, `inferDir` kinds; mid/quadrant/intersection via `snapCands` + `useCand`, which makes a midpoint/intersection point on a click), `quickSnap` + `setQuickSnap` (cleared by a placing `drawClick`, `setTool`, Esc; `onQuickSnap` → controller `renderQuick`), `quickAt` (only that kind, 3× radius; tangent/perpendicular/parallel/hv from the line chain's last point), `drawing`. Controller `quickSnapButton` (`#mc-quick [data-fly=qsnap]`), `quickSnapMenu`, `sketchMenu` row "Quick snaps ▸", panel `.pm-snaps` checkboxes `data-snap`.
- 1.298.0: Fully define sketch (FR-SK-045 leftover): editor `fullyDefine()` (H/V rels for lines within 1°, diameter/radius dims, then hdistance/vdistance from `ORIGIN` to points, curve points and nearest first; each kept only when `tryAdd` lowers the DOF; positions staggered beside the box; one commit) and `fullyDefineUi()` (toasts); sketch panel `data-a=fulldef` beside the status, context menu row on empty space; `nextDimName(sk)`.
- 1.303.0: sketch grid (FR-SK-041): `src/sketch/grid.ts` (`autoStep` 1-2-5 from mm/px, `gridStep` coarsens a fixed spacing ×5 past `GRID_MAX_LINES`, `gridLines` major every `GRID_MAJOR` and through the origin, `snapToGrid(p, step, keep)`); editor `grid`/`gridSnap`/`gridSpacing` + `setGrid`, `syncGrid` from `placeLabels` (LineSegments `userData.skGrid`, rebuilt when step/box/theme key changes), `snapPoint` wraps `snapPointRaw` and grid-rounds only results with no id/curve/rel (hv keeps the locked coordinate); controller prefs localStorage `mc:skGrid`/`mc:skGridSnap`/`mc:skGridStep`, panel `.pm-grid` `data-a=grid|gridsnap|gridstep`. Alignment inference (FR-SK-042) in the same wrapper: `alignAt` (the last 4 `woke` points = snapped point hits while drawing, cleared by `setTool`; within `ALIGN_PX`, the chain's last click and points within 2× snap radius skipped) sets x/y before the grid, `showAlign` draws dashed `LineSegments` `userData.skAlign`, `aligned` lists them; only for `drawing` tools and the `hv` snap preference.
- 1.310.0: quick bar sketch toggles (FR-UI-055): controller `sketchToggles(ed)` after `quickSnapButton` in `renderQuick` (`[data-qt=grid|gridsnap|autorels]`, aria-pressed), `sketchToggle(k)` flips the editor, saves `mc:skGrid`/`mc:skGridSnap`/`mc:autoRels`, ticks the panel boxes and re-renders; the panel boxes call `renderQuick` too.
- 1.316.0: mouse gestures (FR-UI-024): `src/gestures.ts` (pure: `GestureConfig` in localStorage `mc:gestures` via `parseGestures`, `GESTURE_CMDS`/`DEFAULT_GESTURES` per mode part/sketch, slots 0..7 clockwise from up, 4 directions = even slots, `gestureSlot`) and `src/gestureUi.ts` (`gestureGuide` `.mc-gesture`, `gestureDialog` `#gst-dlg`, opened by Display ▾ `#gestures-btn`); controller `gestureStart` on a right press in mode none/sketch (stops the press, so the orbit controls never pan; window move/up/Esc listeners, a release within 4 px without the guide is left to the right-click menus), `runGesture` (`view:<name>` → host `view`, else `command`).
- 1.394.0: Touch gestures (FR-UI-091): `src/longPress.ts` (pure `pressAfter`/`pressDue`, `LONG_PRESS_MS` 550, `LONG_PRESS_PX` 10; `bindLongPress()` from main.ts = window capture pointer listeners for `pointerType` touch, a second finger or a move drops the press; when due it dispatches a `contextmenu` MouseEvent marked `longPress` (`isLongPress(e)`) on `elementFromPoint`, so every `contextmenu` listener works by touch; the browser's own touch `contextmenu` and the click after a fired press are swallowed). Part controller: the viewport `contextmenu` listener handles `isLongPress` on the canvas (`down` cleared so the lift picks nothing): sketch → `sketchMenu`, mode none → `showContextBar`, else `edgeMenu`/`commandMenu`. Orbit/pan/zoom by touch are OrbitControls' own. Tests `tests/unit/longPress.test.ts`, smoke block "touch 1.394" (CDP `Input.dispatchTouchEvent`).
- 1.319.0: measured values into inputs (FR-MEA-002): `src/valueDrop.ts` (pure `parseShown` for one shown value with an optional R/Ø/S prefix and mm|cm|m|in|ft|° unit; `dragValue(el)` on the readout `.cp` values in main.ts `renderReadout`, data type `application/x-mcad-value`; `bindValueDrops` = document capture dragover/drop on text/number inputs, `.drop-val`; `putValue` fills the bare number when the input's next sibling shows the same unit (`plainText`), else the expression, base mm/deg for type=number, then `input` + `change`).
- 1.324.0: hover link (FR-UI-061): controller `bindHover` (tree `pointerover`/`pointerleave`, canvas `pointermove` throttled by rAF → `featureAt` = owner of the face under the pointer), `setHover(id, from)`/`drawHover` into its own `hoverGroup` (redrawn by `drawDecor`): from the tree `featureFaceMesh` (shared with `drawFeatureFaces`) lights the faces unless the row is selected; from the view the row gets `.ft-row.hover`. Only in mode none.
- 1.329.0: Preferences (FR-UI-080): `src/prefs.ts` (pure `Prefs`, `PREF_STORE` localStorage keys (the sketch ones keep their old names, so the sketch panel and quick bar still write them), `parsePref`, `loadPrefs`/`getPref`, `setPrefs` → `onPrefs` listeners with the changed keys, `stepFor(kind, unit)` spin steps, `autosaveDelay`, `qualityFactor`) and `src/prefsUi.ts` `openPrefs(host)` (`#prefs-dlg`, pages Modelling/Sketch/Mouse via `bindTabs`, inputs `data-p=<key>`, units/decimals `data-x`, OK stores, Defaults = open page). Controller: `sketchPrefs(editor, prefs)` at sketch start and on change, `rotateToSketch` gates the normal-to view, rebuild passes `qualityFactor` + `verify` (worker → `Modeller.setVerify` drops the cache; `evaluate` runs `BRepCheck_Analyzer` on a changed body and warns; `mesh` runs `BRepTools.Clean` when the quality changes). main.ts applies `middle` (controls MIDDLE, the controller's pointerdown keeps rotate in sketches) and `wheel` (`zoomSpeed` −1).
- Settings (1.593.0, FR-UI-080): `prefsUi.ts` `openPrefs(host, page?)` is now the Settings dialog (`#prefs-dlg.settings`: header `#set-q` search, nav `[role=tab]#pt-<page>` for general/units/view/mouse/modelling/sketch with `.set-n` match counts, pages `#pp-<page>`, rows `.set-row[data-find]` (English + shown text; `prefs.ts` `settingMatches`), fields `data-p` = prefs, `data-h` = host values lang/theme/tips/units/decimals, `data-sksnap` = sketch snaps; `data-a=reset` (page, or the shown rows while searching) / `resetall` / `ok`; last page in localStorage `mc:settingsPage`). Opened by the header `#settings-btn`, File ▸ Settings… (`FileHost.settings`), Ctrl+, and the sketch quick bar's `[data-qset]` (`ModelHost.openSettings("sketch")`). New prefs `shade` (`mc:skShade`), `snaps` (`mc:skSnaps`, `snapsText` = JSON in menu order) and `importQuality` (`mc:importQuality`, replaces the Display menu's mesh select for opened files). The sketch panel no longer holds model snap/automatic relations/relation glyphs/shade/snaps/grid (Settings ▸ Sketch; the quick bar keeps Grid, Snap to grid, Inferences, Add dimensions and Shade `[data-qt=shade]`); the Display menu lost units, decimals, mesh, gestures and Preferences; Help lost Show tips (Settings ▸ General). Per-tool Add dimensions / For construction stay in the sketch panel, as in other CAD apps.

Selectors on `#mc-tree` keep working.

Names and grades are shown inside `translate="no"`. Weldment cut list `matOptions` (optgroups), `compose.ts` `materialPattern` goes by category first. Add a material = one `ROWS` line with a real source; tests `tests/unit/materials.test.ts`. 1.375.0: `CHECKED` (material name → public page without the scheme that its density, E and strengths were compared with) becomes `Material.ref`, shown as "Checked against:" in the dialog; add an entry only for a page that really shows the values. 1.382.0, 1.385.0, 1.399.0, 1.405.0, 1.407.0, 1.413.0, 1.416.0, 1.417.0, 1.420.0, 1.426.0 and 1.429.0: 149 of 170 rows have a `ref`; a library row without one shows `.mat-unver` ("Not checked against a public reference yet.") in the dialog. not for sub-assemblies). 1.453.0: material per body (`PartDoc.bodies[key].material` `MatRef`, absent = the part's; body row menu **Material…** → controller `pickBodyMaterial`/`setBody({ material })`; the worker adds `area`/`com`/`cov` per body (`Modeller.bodyMass`) to `Rebuild.bodies` when there are several bodies or one has a material; `inertia.ts` `bodyMasses` + controller `bodyMasses(fallback)` feed main.ts `runMass`, which combines the shown bodies in place of the one part; sheet metal properties still use the part's material). 1.458.0: assemblies too (`Assembler.ownBodies` = per-body volume/com/cov in part coordinates, cached on the source, only for parts with a body material; the worker sends them placed (`inertia.ts` `moveBodies`) as `parts[i].bodies`; controller `partMasses(i, fallback)` → `massProperties`' density callback may return `MassBody[]` in the part data's coordinates; BOM `bodiesKg`). 1.462.0: drawings (drawing worker `bodyMats` / `Assembler.bodyMats` give each solid's material in TopExp order as `Owner.mats`; `solidOwners` uses them while the solid count still matches, keys `<comp>#<k>` for a component's bodies; cut list table kg = item `mat`, else `materials.ts` `bodiesDensity` of its bodies, else the part's; debug `drw.section(dir, up, p)`); 1.471.0: the worker's facts carry `mats` (distinct body materials) and `kg` (bodies weighed by their own density) for the title block, and `CompInfo.mats` per component; `bom.ts` `BomRow.material`, `bomMats(comps)` → `sheetBom(..., mats)`, `BOM_COLS` gains "Material" at `BOM_MAT_COL` 4 (older 4-column BOMs stay so; panel checkbox `data-d=bmat`); debug `drw.fields()`); picker buttons `data-a=import|export`, hidden `input[data-a=file]`).

- 1.371.0: named views (FR-UI-104: `NamedView` {name, t, p, h} in `PartDoc.views` / `AsmDoc.views`, kept across undo/redo (`DocStore.keepViews`, asm `step`); `ModelController.namedViews()` / `AsmController.namedViews()` return a `NamedViews` (list/save/remove; the part uses `store.amend`), main.ts falls back to `sessionViews` per model file; navigation.ts `namedHtml`/`saveNamed`/`refreshNamed` add the row to the Space picker (`data-nv=view:<name>`, `data-nvdel`, `.nav-vname`); `Viewer.getView`/`setView`) and the heads-up view bar (FR-UI-022: main.ts `#hud` in `#viewport` with `data-hud=area|prev`; while `#app` is `.model` or `.asm`, `#views`, `#projection` and `#display-mode` are moved into it and back after their comment markers). Same release: `LinearPatternFeature.total`/`total2` (Up to distance: spacing = distance to the last instance, panel select `data-k=total|total2`), `seedOnly` (direction 2 copies only the seed, no i>0 & j>0); `CircularPatternFeature.dir2`/`sym`/`count2`/`angle2`/`equal2` (copies turned the other way, keys `-i` in `skip`, tags `im<i>`; ones landing on a direction 1 copy are dropped; panel `data-k=cdir2|sym|equal2`). 1.503.0: Up to reference (`LinearPatternFeature.upTo`/`upToOffset`, `upTo2`/`upToOffset2` PlaneRefs; kernel `reach` = distance from `spotCentre` along the direction to the plane less the offset, shared over n − 1 gaps; E-PAT-REFPAR across, E-PAT-REFBACK behind; panel select `data-k=total|total2` value "ref", focus `pref`/`pref2` (view pick or tree plane), `featureRefs` lists the planes).

- 1.523.0: Jog line (sketch tool `jog` in Trim ▾): editor `jogShape(p)` (first click = `draft.ids[0]` line + `pts[0]`; cuts along it and step h square to it, inside the line) previewed through `offsetPreview`, `jogClick` splits the line into 5 (original keeps its start, `collinear` rest, `parallel` step, two `perpendicular` sides; H/V/fix copied to the rest, its length dims dropped).
- 1.527.0: Segment (sketch tool `segment` in Trim ▾, FR-SK-032): editor `segmentAt(p)` with `segCount` (2..100) and `segMode` points (onCurve points; circles always) / segments (the line or arc cut into `segCount` pieces, lines collinear + equal to the first, H/V/fix copied, its length dims dropped); panel `data-a=segn|segmode`.
- 1.527.0: Add dimensions on creation (FR-UI-052): editor `addDims` (pref `addDims`, localStorage `mc:skAutoDims` since 1.585.0, default off like `slotDims`: Andrey wants new curves left under defined) → `drawClick` `finish` runs `autoDims(made)` (rect tools/pgram: two side lengths; polygon: its circle's diameter; circles diameter, arcs radius, lines length) through `addTypedDims` (now returns the added ids, places arc labels) and `openSizes` after the commit; `pointerDown` accepts an open Modify box then `syncDraft` moves the chain's points to the solved sketch; toggles: quick bar `data-qt=adddims`, panel `data-a=adddims`, Preferences.
- 1.585.0: construction geometry (editor `render`: `cons` entities go to `consSegs`, a dashed `LineMaterial` (`userData.skConstruction`) in the definition colour; `forCons` + `setForCons`/`onForCons`: `toggleConstruction` with nothing selected flips it, `drawClick` `finish` marks new non-point entities `cons`; bar `data-c=cons` pressed, panel `data-a=forcons`); the hovered entity (`hot`) draws 4 px thick and a hovered point as a 12 px dot, and the dim tool keeps `hover` while `dimDraft.dim` follows the cursor; `#st-skdef` (controller `syncSketchStatus`) + panel `.pm-legend`; `.sk-pass` on the label layer lets non-select tools click through `.sk-rel`/`.sk-dim`; `sketchToggle` stores only the changed key.
- 1.587.0: sketch feedback batch 1: solver `SolveResult.over` = driving dimensions whose Jacobian row lies in the span of the rows before it (`redundantDims`, Gram-Schmidt; relations first, then dims in order, so the newest is named), `isOver(r)` = conflict or `over`; editor colours only `badEnts` (items of the conflicting/over rels and dims, plus curves whose points all are) red, `.sk-dim.bad`/`.sk-rel.bad`; `addDimension` asks `ui.ts` `chooseModal` ("Make Dimension Driven?", `dialog.choose-dlg [data-choose=driven|driving]`) instead of making it driven silently; `makeDriven(ids)` + assistant `data-mkdriven`; drawing tools set `hover` from the snap (`snapHover`), hover colour turquoise.
- 1.590.0: sketch feedback batch 2 (gap list S4/S5/S6): editor `entityProps()` (one selected point/line/circle/arc: x/y, x1..y2/len/ang, cx/cy/r/sweep, mm and radians) and `setEntityProp(key, v)` (targets solved like a drag on a copy; refused, nothing changed, unless every target is reached; commit "Move point"/"Edit line|circle|arc"), controller `entityBox`/`bindEntityBox` (`.pm-entprops`, inputs `data-ep=<key>`); `regionFill` (sketch `regions.ts` `sketchRegions` triangulated, cached by the entities' JSON in `fillCache`, mesh `userData.skRegions` = region count, under the curves; `shadeRegions`, localStorage `mc:skShade`, panel `data-a=shade`); `openEnds()` (line/arc/elliptical arc/open spline ends used once, not counting coincident/midpoint/intersection/onCurve/pierce/ext fix rels or construction copies) drawn as 11 px Points `userData.skOpenEnds`; legend swatches `.sw.fill`/`.sw.open`.
- 1.591.0 (gap list M4/M2/M1): `src/viewItems.ts` (`VIEW_ITEMS` planes/axes/points/csys/sketches/curves, localStorage `mc:viewItems` = the types turned off, `viewItemShown`, `setViewItem(k | null, on)`, `onViewItems`; part controller `drawDecor`/`drawPlanes`/`draw3dSketches` skip a type turned off unless picking planes or it is selected); heads-up `#hud [data-hud=items]` (`.on` when any is off) opens `.hud-items` (`input[data-vi]`, `[data-vi-all=1|0]`), hidden over assemblies. Appearances: `PartDoc.faceColors` (face name) / `featureColors` (feature id) → kernel worker colours `brep_faces` by `shown.names` (face > feature > body); controller `setAppearance(kind, key, color | null)` (one undo step), `pickAppearance` (hidden `input[type=color]`), geometry menu Face colour… / Feature colour… / Remove appearance, tree menu Feature colour…. Push/pull: `pushFace` = the flat face `showContextBar` picked (cleared by the next pick, Esc, a rebuild), `drawPushArrow` (key "push", from `drawArrows` when no feature arrow), `startPushDrag` makes an instant new `moveface` draft (`pushAt` keeps the arrow's base), `movePush` = signed distance (`flip` when negative), label `#mc-pushval`; release = `ok()`. Smoke "model 1.591" (shard 3).
- 1.596.0 (gap list A1/A2, S7, S8, M7): asm `isolate(ids | null, "hide" | "trans")` (view only: `isolated`, `applyOpacity` hides the other top-level components' parts (`isoHid`) or fades them to `ISO_FADE`; bar `#asm-iso` with `select[data-iso=mode]` and `[data-iso=exit]`; Esc exits; component and multi menus Isolate / Make others transparent). Sketch editor `doubleClick` in Select on a line/arc = `select(chainOf(id))`; `showDims` (localStorage `mc:skDims`, quick bar `data-qt=showdims`) filters `sk.dims` in `render` (selected ones stay). `topology.ts` marks `EdgeInfo.tangent` (seams, or at the chain's middle welded vertex: facet normals within 20° and the exact plane/cylinder/sphere normals (`surfDir`) within 1°, else vertex normals within 12°; `wNorm` per welded vertex and face) and puts tangent segments after the sharp ones in `edgeSegs` (`PartData.sharpSegs`); `Viewer.tangentEdges` (localStorage `mc:tangentEdges`, Display menu `#tangent-edges`, `setTangentEdges`, `edgeSegsOf`).
- 1.597.0 (gap list M3/U2/M10/S12/U4): `src/selStatus.ts` `selSummary`/`showSelStatus` fill `#st-sel` in the status bar (one edge: length, R, sweep; one face: area, R; several: count + sum) from main.ts `selStatusOf` (Smart measure picks) and controller `drawMulti` (Ctrl/box selection, `multiStatus`); controller `renderSelList` = `#mc-sellist` in `#viewport` (rows `li[data-sel=f:<i>|e:<id>]` kind + owner feature, `[data-slx]` ×, `[data-slclear]`), gone when the selection is used; `startFeature` keeps `preSel` (picked edge/face + `multi`) for `preselect(draft, sel)`: fillet/chamfer take every edge of picked faces, mirror a flat face as plane, lpattern `dir`/cpattern `axis` an edge or face, draft/offsurf/split several faces, body commands one face per body (combine/indent: main = first). A Ctrl press skips the push arrow in `arrowHit`, and `drawPushArrow` draws none while `multi` is set (`ctrlPick`/`boxSelect` clear `pushFace`). Pref `projectFace` (`mc:skProjectFace`, Settings ▸ Sketch): `startSketch` on a face passes its edges to `createSketch`/`editSketch` → editor `projectEdges` (construction copies with fix `ext` rels, "Project face edges" step). Tool cursors: `hoverCursor.ts` `toolCursorOf`/`setToolCursor` set `canvas[data-tool]` from editor `setTool` (pencil + shape, or crosshair + icon). Hands-on test `tests/human/select.mjs`.
- 1.598.0 (gap list A3/A4/D1/D2/U1): asm `ExplodeView.lines` (Explode panel `data-a=lines`, view menu Show/Hide explode lines → `setExplodeLines`) drawn by `drawExplodeLines(off, k)` from `applyExplode`'s `draw` (`explodeLines` group, one dashed `LineSegments` `userData.explodeLines` = count, bbox centre at the assembled place → now). Mate bar `#asm-matebar` (`renderMateBar(fits)` from `renderMatePanel` once `a` and `b` are picked, at `mateAt` = last pick + 18 px; `[data-mbt=<type>]`, `[data-mb=val|flip|ok|cancel]`; `removeMateBar` on cancel/close). Drawings: `src/drawing/formatPaint.ts` `PAINT_KEYS`/`canPaint`/`paintFormat` (same kind only; keys the source lacks are removed) → workspace `painter` (🖌 `data-a=fpaint` in the properties head, `paintAt` on clicks, `svg.drw-painting`, Esc/empty click ends; the source stays selected); `src/drawing/dimAlign.ts` `alignDim` (h/v of one view: dimension line onto the source's) + `spaceDims(dims, box, s, 10, 8)` → workspace `aligner` (⇉ `data-a=dalign`, `alignAt`) and view panel `data-a=dspace` → `spaceViewDims`. `src/shortcuts.ts` (`comboOf`, `RESERVED`, `parseShortcuts`, `loadShortcuts`/`saveShortcuts` localStorage `mc:shortcuts` {command id: combo}, `commandFor`, `assign`) → Settings page `keys` (`#pt-keys`, inputs `data-sc=<id>` read-only, keydown sets, Backspace clears, `[data-scx]`; `PrefsHost.commands` = controller `commandList()` = `searchable("none")`), part controller `key()` runs a custom one before the built-in keys in mode none/sketch. Hands-on tests `tests/human/asm2.mjs`, `drawing.mjs`, `keys.mjs`.
- 1.599.0 (gap list S9/S10/S11/M5/M6): `hoverCursor.ts` `DimCursor` (`dimh|dimv|dima` + `l` = locked) / `dimCursorOf(t, locked)`, set by editor `render` while the tool is `dim`. Plain fix rels (no `ext`) draw as `.sk-rel.sk-fix` with `LOCK_SVG`, always (not gated by `showRels`); on a point it sits above right of it (`placeLabels`). main.ts `normalTo` keeps `normalSeq` {n, before = `viewer.getView()`, step}: N = normal, again = other side, a third time `setView(before)`. `Feature` = union `& FeatureNote` (`comment`); `Modeller.evaluate`'s chain key drops `comment`, so a comment rebuilds nothing; controller `editComment` (`ui.ts` `askNote`, dialog `#fcomment-dlg`; `#note-dlg` is the drawing note dialog), tree `.ft-note[data-a=note]` + row `title`, menu Add/Edit comment…. `showInstant`: a second double-click on the feature whose dims show (`instantGone` covers the first click hiding them), or one without dims, opens its `sketch` with `editSketch`. Hands-on test `tests/human/polish.mjs`. Same release (M8/M9/S13/D3/U5): controller `measuring` + `setMeasuring` (📏 `.pm-meas[data-a=pmmeasure]` prepended to `.pm-okc` by `measureButton` after `renderPanel0`, key M, Esc ends; `picking` is false while on, so main.ts Smart measure takes view clicks; host `clearMeasure`); `drawFeatureErrors` (mode none: failed features' absorbed sketches red 0xe03131 + `.mc-callout.mc-ferr[data-ferr]`, click = `editFeature`); editor `editTextInPlace` (`.sk-textedit` input in the label layer, from `doubleClick` on a text); `dimAlign.ts` `rapidSlots`/`rapidRow` → workspace `rapidDim` (targets `rect.drw-rapid[data-rapid=top|bottom|left|right]` in the dim preview, the click uses the snapped row); `src/coach.ts` `COACH` cards (`.pm-coach`, `[data-coach=ok|off]`, seen in localStorage `mc:coach`, off under webdriver unless `?coach`, reset by `setTips(true)`) inserted by controller `coach()`. Hands-on test `tests/human/polish2.mjs`.
- Silhouette edges (1.638.0): `src/sketch/silhouette.ts` (pure `silhouettes(RoundFace, n)`: cylinder/cone with the axis along the sketch plane = generator lines at ±(axis × n), radius fitted along the axis, the longest stretch on the faceted face (`onFace`, tol 3 % of the radius, ends bisected), skipped where a line edge of the face lies on it (fillet tangent edges, seams); sphere = its circle when wholly on the face; `lineEdge`/`circleEdge`). Part controller `silhouettesFor(n)` (cache per part data + normal) and `silhouetteAt(x, y, n)` (≤ 12 px on screen, host `visibleAt`) feed the sketch host's `modelEdge` (first when ≤ 4 px) and `modelEdges`; `silRefs` gives `EdgeRef` {faces: [face, face], hint, `sil: true`}; kernel `followEdges` → `Modeller.silhouette` (the face's cylinder/cone generator at the angle square to n over its V range, the side nearer the hint; sphere circle). Same release: `Snapper.edgeNear` (nearest visible edge whatever point snap won) → host `edgeNear`, used by the sketch callbacks when `edgeAt` gives no edge; editor `modelSnapAt` takes round/curved edges seen edge-on (projected as lines). Tests `tests/unit/silhouette.test.ts`, hands-on `tests/human/filletdim.mjs`.
- Hidden model edge copies (1.647.0): editor `materialize` (a snap to a model edge's end/middle/centre while drawing, a Select click on a model edge, a Dimension pick) marks the construction copy it makes `SkEnt.ref`; `hiddenRefs()` leaves refs out of `render` (and their own points, and the glyph of their On edge fix) unless selected, hovered or dangling, `dropUnusedRefs()` in `commit` removes refs nothing else uses, `toggleConstruction` turns a ref into ordinary geometry; revolve axis defaults skip refs. Hands-on `tests/human/snapref.mjs`, smoke "model 1.647" in the 1.89 block (shard 3).

- 1.535.0: right-click menu on geometry (FR-UI-025): controller `geometryMenu` (from `commandMenu` in mode none after `emptyMenu`, and a long press) runs `showContextBar` for the icons above, then `showMenu` with the owner feature's Edit feature / Edit sketch / Suppress, Hide body (`bodyAt`), Normal to (flat face, `ctxPick` kept for `command("normal")`), Zoom to fit, Delete, under a `.mc-menu-h` feature name.

- 1.539.0: box selection in parts (FR-SEL-008): controller `boxDown` (window capture: left mouse press in mode none with Smart measure (host `measureTool`) where `emptyAt` finds no part, plane or snapped edge turns `controls.enabled` off for the drag), `boxMove` draws `.mc-selbox` (`.cross` right to left), `boxUp` stops the pointerup and runs `boxSelect(rect, crossing, add)`: faces seen by `probeGrid` rays (crossing) or wholly inside and seen (window, small faces by triangle middles), edges by `polyTaken` with samples inside the box not behind a face; `SEL_FILTER` applies, Ctrl merges into `multi` (`mergeSel`). Pure helpers in `src/boxSelect.ts`, tests `tests/unit/boxSelect.test.ts`.
- 1.540.0: sketch dimension arrowheads (FR-SK-068): `SkDim.arrows` in/out (absent = automatic: editor `arrowsOf`, outside when the dimension line is under 3.2 arrow lengths, radius/diameter inside); `dimGeometry(dm, line, arrow)` calls `arrow(tip, dir)` (filled triangles, `arrowLen` = 9 px, one mesh `userData.skArrows` in `render`), outside arrows get 2.2-length tails; `linearEnds` is shared; `syncArrowDot` puts `.sk-adot` at `arrowDotAt` (first end `fa` of a linear dimension, the far rim of a diameter; the Instant 2D grip keeps the other end) on the one selected dimension, a click = `flipArrows(id)` ("Flip dimension arrows" undo step); context menu row Flip arrows.
- 1.541.0: several components in assemblies (FR-SEL-007/008): asm controller `picked` (top-level ids; empty = the one `selected`), `selection()`, `setPicked`, `togglePick` (Ctrl+click in the tree or the view, never drags), `boxDown`/`boxMove`/`boxUp` (window capture, empty space via host `partAt`, host `measureTool` "select") → `boxComponents(rect, crossing)` (every shown mesh vertex projected: window = all inside, crossing = any, plus `probeGrid` hits) with `mergeSel`; `drawPicked` tints with meshes sharing the parts' geometry in `selGroup` (`userData.picked`; 2+ picked, or a box), redrawn after each build; `multiMenu` (one `change` for hide/show/fix/float/suppress) and `deleteComponents`; `viewMenu` = right-click without drag (or long press) on a component in the view; status `Selected: n`.
