Tests `tests/unit/hoverCursor.test.ts`.
- 1.547.0: Dome (FR-FT-103): `DomeFeature` `faces` (flat), `distance`, `flip` (dent), `elliptical` (round faces only); kernel `Modeller.dome`: a round outer wire = profile (arc rim → apex, apex → centre, centre → rim) revolved about the axis (sphere R = (a² + h²)/2h, or a quarter `gp_Elips`), else `MakeFilling` through the outer edges + `Add_4` peak over the centroid, sewn with the face into a solid; fused/cut with `bool(..., glue = !round)` (`bool`'s glue now covers cut too); faces `<id>:dome:<k>`, the old face `<id>:old:<k>`; controller treats it like Move Face (face list, `faces` focus), command in the Bodies ▾ flyout. Tests `tests/unit/dome.test.ts`, smoke "model 1.547" (shard 6, Move Face block).
- 1.548.0: Wrap (FR-FT-104): `WrapFeature` `sketch`, `face` (cylindrical), `kind` emboss/deboss, `depth`; `src/sketch/wrap.ts` `segSpline` turns every profile `Seg` into an exact 2D (rational) B-spline, kernel `Modeller.wrap` maps its poles to the cylinder's (angle, height) = (π + e·P / r, d·P) with u0 towards the sketch plane at angle π (clear of the seam), d flipped to keep the sketch handedness (the map is affine, so exact), `MakeEdge_30` pcurves + `BuildCurves3d_2`, `MakeFace_21` + `ShapeFix_Face`, `MakeThickSolidBySimple` with the sign whose centroid is further out (or in) than the wrapped face's (shaft vs hole from the face normal at its middle), glue fuse/cut; faces `<id>:base|top:k`, `<id>:side:k:q`; controller focus `faces` takes one cylindrical face, Bodies ▾ after Dome, `absorbs` hides the sketch. Tests `tests/unit/wrap.test.ts`, smoke "model 1.548" (shard 6, after Replace Face).
- 1.549.0: Sketch Picture (FRS 2.2): `SketchFeature.pictures` `SketchPicture` {id, name, src data URL, c, w, h mm, ang, alpha, flip, free}; editor `syncPictures` (from `render`; one textured `PlaneGeometry` mesh per picture in the scene, `userData.skPicture`, renderOrder 2980 under the curves, matrix from the sketch frame), `addPicture` (96 dpi, lower left on the origin), `setPicture` (locked aspect unless `free`), `removePicture`; controller command `skpic` (Sketch tab beside DXF) → `insertPicture` (FileReader, ≤ 2048 px via canvas), panel `pictureBox`/`bindPictureBox` (`.pm-pics`, inputs `data-pic=<id>:x|y|w|h|lock|ang|alpha|flip|del`). Shown only while the sketch is edited. Smoke "model 1.549" (block 6, after K = Add relation).
- 1.554.0: sketch blocks (FRS 2.2): `SketchFeature.blocks` `SketchBlock` {id `blk<n>`, name, ents, rels in local coordinates, insertion point 0,0} and `inserts` `BlockInsert` {id `bi<n>`, block, x, y, ang, scale}; `src/sketch/blocks.ts` (pure: instances are materialised as fixed entities `<insert>~<ent>` held by one fix rel `<insert>~fix`, so solver/profile/kernel need nothing; `placeInserts`, `makeBlock(sk, ids)` (base = box middle, rels wholly inside go in, points shared with the rest stay), `insertBlock` (reuses a same definition), `moveInsert`, `removeInserts` (unused definitions go), `explodeInsert`, Edit Block = `beginEditBlock` (`SketchFeature.editBlock` {insert, known}: no fix, definition rels/dims (`SketchBlock.dims`, lengths × scale) on `<insert>~<id>`; `placeInserts` leaves that insert alone) / `finishEditBlock` (instance + entities drawn since (not in `known`) back into block coordinates, rels/dims wholly inside go in, all placed again; controller `ok()` finishes it on Exit sketch), `blockFile`/`readBlockFile` `.mcadblk` {format "mobiuscad-block"}); editor: a select click on an instance selects it whole and drags it ("Move block"), Delete removes inserts, trim/fillet/offset/… toast to explode first, `makeBlock`/`insertBlock`/`setInsert`/`explodeBlock`/`renameBlock`; controller commands `skblock`/`skinsblk` (Sketch tab after `skpic`), `insertBlockMenu`, panel `blockBox` (`.pm-block`, `data-blk=name|x|y|ang|scale|edit|explode|save`, `.pm-blkedit` with `finish` while editing); `relationList` hides `~fix` rels. Tests `tests/unit/blocks.test.ts`, smoke "model 1.554" (block 6).
- 1.554.0 also: arc length and arc conditions (FR-SK-060): `DimKind` "arclen" (e = [arc], r × ccw sweep a → b; editor `arcLenSet` takes an arc + both ends as three picks, `dimGeometry` draws a concentric arc through `pos`, label prefix ◠, no grip/arrow flip) and `SkDim.cond` `ArcCond[]` per `e` entry (c/min/max: solver `dimFn` adds ±r to `distFn`, editor `dimPoints` wraps `dimPoints0` and moves the circle end along the measure; with a line the circle is always the second point); Shift+click a circle in Dimension → `dimDraft.side` → `shiftConds` (towards the other item = min); context menu `arcCondRows` → `setArcCond` (re-measures, "Arc condition" step); drawing `modelItems` skips both.
- 1.552.0: Flex (FR-FT-105): `FlexFeature` `bodies` (empty = all), `kind` bend/twist/taper/stretch, `axis` x/y/z, `toward` (bend), `angle`/`factor`/`distance`, `trim1`/`trim2` (in from the bodies' ends), `origin` (absent = box middle, no UI yet), `hard` (false = no split); pure `src/model/flex.ts` (`flexMap`, `flexPoint`: region s1..s2 bent/twisted/tapered/stretched about s0 = the triad, rigid or affine run-on outside, `flexAffine`); kernel `Modeller.flex` → `flexBody`: `BRepAlgoAPI_Splitter` at the trim planes, faces two pieces share dropped, outside pieces (and a stretched middle) by `affineMove` (gp_Trsf when rigid, else GTrsf), middle faces by `flexFace` (`NurbsConvert`, periodic surfaces `Segment`ed, degree ≥ 3, knots by span along the axis, poles mapped then 4 Jacobi steps to interpolate the map at the Greville points, edges from pcurves via `MakeEdge_31` + `BuildCurves3d_2`), all sewn, solid, names by identity else nearest centroid, `unifyAlike`; `frames[id]` = triad (n = axis, x = bend direction) + `len` [s1, s2, half sizes] for controller `drawFlexPlanes`. A body op (`isBodyOp`, `flexPanel`, Bodies ▾ after Scale). Volumes: use adaptive `VolumeProperties_2` (the quick one is ~5e-4 off on these B-splines). Tests `tests/unit/flex.test.ts`. 1.553.0: trim plane drag arrows (controller `drawFlexArrows`: `placeArrow` from the bodies' ends `len[4]`/`len[5]` along ±n, keys `trim1`/`trim2`, `moveArrow` clamps 0..room) and triad position (panel checkbox `data-k=tcentre`, inputs `data-k=ox|oy|oz` → `origin`). 1.554.0: triad drag arrows keyed `ox|oy|oz` along world axes from `origin ?? frame o` (only while the Flex is open; `startArrowDrag`/`moveArrow` set the `origin` component).
- 1.557.0: Boundary Boss/Cut (FRS 2.3): `LoftFeature.boundary` (`profiles` = Direction 1, `guides` = Direction 2 curves); kernel `Modeller.boundary` (with 2+ curves, else `loft`): corners by `BRepExtrema_DistShapeShape`, curves oriented first → last profile and checked to go round every profile in one order (E-BND-CROSS/ORDER), each cell between neighbouring curves = `GeomFill_BSplineCurves_2` Coons patch from curve pieces resampled into splines, sewn with the end caps, `MakeSolid` + `ShapeFix_Solid`, faces `<id>:side:<i>:<k>`/`:start`/`:end`, then `applyTool`; controller commands `boundary`/`boundarycut` (Sweep ▾; draft = last two closed sketches + curves made after them), panel branch in `p3Panel` (Direction 1 `lp*`, Direction 2 `lg*`, merge). Tests `tests/unit/boundary.test.ts`, smoke "model 1.557" (shard 6, after Wrap).
- 1.550.0: blind tap drill depth (FR-FT-051): `src/model/tapDepth.ts` (pure: `autoTapDepth` = thread depth + ½ major Ø, `minTapDepth` = + 2 pitches, `tapDepthLinked`, `keepTapDepth(f, linked)` raises a short drill or moves a linked one; tapped (thread not through) and ptap only, an `expr` depth is never touched); controller calls it after kind/size/fit, End condition and typed thread depth/diameter, and the panel shows `[data-warn=tapdepth]` while `tapTooShallow`. Tests `tests/unit/tapDepth.test.ts`, smoke "model 1.550".
- 1.143.0: sweep end tangency (FR-FT-080): `SweepFeature.endTangent`; kernel `sweepAlong` adds a second section with `MakePipeShell.Add_2(wire, endVertex)`: the profile turned by the least rotation from its sketch normal to the end tangent and moved to the path end (not with a guide law, circular profile or closed path); panel select `data-k=endTan`.
- 1.161.0 (Leuenberger exam fixes): Hole position table (controller `holeAxes`: on a face square to a world axis the columns are world axes, stored pts stay in the face frame); revolve keeps a profile line on the axis when the profile only closes with it (`axSkip`); hole head sizes at their automatic values scale with a typed diameter; `assembly.ts` downcasts moved faces with `TopoDS.Face_1` (else `Kernel.face` rejects them and no assembly part gets exact geometry); `solver.ts` accepts a stalled solve within 1e-5·L; `navigation.ts` `arrowsFree` lets the arrows turn the view while a plain button has focus. 1.174.0: `patch.ts` `patchFace` turns a mesh sphere/other face into the exact cylinder; `Viewer.viewEnd`/`animEnd` make rotate/pan/zoom keys start from where a running camera move ends. 1.189.0: the asm Move panel shows `math.ts` `poseAngles(tf, typed)` (controller `typedAngles`), so typed angles survive ±90° about Y. 1.196.0: `rotateView` tilts about the screen right axis when looking straight down/up and clamps the tilt between the poles.
- Document tabs (1.129.0, FR-DOC-003, FR-UI-120): `src/docTabs.ts` `DocTabs` (`#doc-tabs`, grid area `tabs` above the status bar; fixed at the bottom over a drawing). One document is live; the others are parked: `ModelController.park`/`resume` (`PartTab` keeps the `DocStore`, so its undo history; `load(..., keep)` never reuses a parked store), `AsmController.park`/`resume` (`AsmTab`), `DrawingWorkspace.park`/`resume` (`DrawingTab`), model files keep their bytes (main.ts `modelSrc`) and are opened again. Controllers call host `leaveOthers` → `docs.before3d()` and `opened(label)`; the drawing calls `beforeOpen` → `beforeDrawing()`; a drawing tab keeps the 3D document under it live (`under`). Edit part in place runs in `inPlaceSwitch` (the part takes over the assembly's tab). Asm host `openParts` feeds Insert component's "Open document" entries.
- Sheet metal (FR-FT-120, P6): `src/sheetmetal/` (`types.ts` features `smbase`/`smflange`/`smhem`/`smflat` and `GAUGE_TABLES`; `sheet.ts` pure model: panels = flat-pattern outlines + rigid transform `M` (flat → folded, panel occupies z ∈ [0, T]), bends = strips of width `bendAllowance` rolled about an axis (`makeBend`), `addFlange` trims the panel (material inside/outside) with reliefs, `flatLoops` = union of panels and bend strips; `kernel.ts` `evalSheet` builds prisms/revolves and fuses them via `Modeller.sheetApi()`; `flat.ts` DXF/SVG; `ui.ts` `SM_TOOLS` (the Sheet Metal command bar tab), `smDraft`/`smPanel`/`bindSmPanel` (`data-sm` controls)). Face names `<fid>:p<n>:top|bot|<edge>`, bends `<fid>:b<n>:in|out|…`; a picked edge between `…:top` and `…:<edge>` names the panel edge (`findEdge`). `Modeller.evaluate` carries the `SheetState` in its cache (`CacheEntry.sm`) and returns it as `Rebuild.sheet`. The Flat-Pattern feature stays last (startFeature inserts before it); Flatten toggles its suppression. Tab (`smtab`: `smDraft("smbase")` makes it once a base exists; `tab` maps the closed sketch into the host panel's flat coordinates, beside the panel only). Sketched bend / jog (`smbend`/`smjog`: one-line sketch on a sheet face, `linePanel` finds the panel and side, `foldPanel` splits it with `clipOuter` (half-plane clip of line/arc loops) into the fixed panel, bend strips and new panels in the same flat coordinates, so the flat pattern is unchanged; the jog gap comes from the offset being linear in it). 1.138.0: cuts by ordinary features show in the flat pattern: `flatBody(api, st, fid, folded)` cuts each panel prism by the folded body (skipped when the body's volume equals the panels + bends), moves what was taken out by root ∘ M⁻¹ (`flatTrsf`) and cuts it from the pattern; `topEdges` then stores the pattern's top outline as `SheetState.flatEdges`, which `flatDrawing` prefers. Cuts across bends aren't unrolled. 1.180.0: edge flange length arrow (controller `drawFlangeArrow`: on the tip of the `<fid>:p0` panel from `last.sheet`, key `length`, only while folded). 1.194.0: mitred corners: `edgeFlange` finds picked edges meeting at a corner of one face (`flangeCorners`), runs those flanges on (`FlangeSpec.ext0`/`ext1`) and `mitre` clips each with `clipOuter` (edge `miter`) where the two flange mid-planes meet, `SmFlangeFeature.gap`/2 back. 1.206.0: break corner (`smcorner`: `edges` = thickness edges between two side faces of one panel, `kind` chamfer/fillet, `size`; `breakCorner`/`cornerOf` edit the panel outline (new piece `x<n>`) and cut the corner with a `SheetOp` cut carrying `segs`, whose `ent` with ":" names the new side face `<pid>:x<n>` (`segCutTool`); controller `pickSmEdge` rejects top/bot edges for it). 1.236.0: closed corner (`smclosed`: `faces` = flange end faces, `kind` overlap/underlap, `gap`; `closedCorner` finds the nearest panel ahead with `planeHits`/`beside`, `moveEnd` moves the end piece (a strip fused as a panel with the same id, or a `segs` cut), then the neighbour's end facing it with `endFace`; controller `pickSmFace`). 1.250.0: flat pattern view in drawings (`views.ts` `FLAT_CONFIG` = "@flat", a reserved view configuration; the drawing worker builds `sheet.ts` `flatPatternDoc` (Flat-Pattern on or added) and moves it by `flatToXY` (root⁻¹), so the view is orient "top"; facts carry `bends` (`flatBends`), drawn by `annot.ts` `bendPrims` (CENTER pen + note, `DrawingView.bendLines`/`bendNotes`); workspace palette tile `data-pal="flat"` (`Mode.cfg`) and `configField`). 1.259.0: miter flange (`smmiter`: `sketch` = open chain of lines on a plane square to an edge, starting at it (`miterProfile`), `edges`, `radius`, `gap`; `miterFlange` maps the chain into the start edge's (outward, normal) plane, each line = one level of `addFlange` per edge (level ≥ 2 on `<prev pid>:tip`), setbacks outer when the bend turns like the first (the profile is the first bend's outside) else inner; corners from `flangeCorners` on level 1 are cut each level by `planeCut` with the bisector plane through the face corner (coplanar flanges defeat `mitre`'s mid-plane method); ids `<fid>:p<k·edges + j>`). 1.271.0: cuts across bends unrolled (`removedPieces` also cuts each `bendSolid` by the folded body; `unrolled` takes `BRepAlgoAPI_Common` of the removed piece with the bend's neutral surface (radius ba / angle, revolved bend line), maps each face's outer wire to the flat strip (t along the bend line, φ·ρ across) and cuts its prism from the flat pattern). 1.284.0: bend table on flat views (`BendTableAnnot` `t: "btable"`, `view`, `pos`, `prec`; `annot.ts` `bendTags` (top to bottom, then left to right by the bend line middle), `bendRows`, `BEND_COLS`; a btable on a view makes `bendPrims` draw tags in place of notes; workspace mode `btable` = click a view whose facts have `bends`, then place; props `data-d=bprec`). 1.296.0: swept flange (`smswept` = `SmMiterFeature` with type "smswept"; `sweptProfile` turns lines + tangent arcs into the virtual-sharp polyline and per-corner arc radii; `miterFlange(..., arcs)` gives each level its own inner radius `rs[k]` = arc − T when the bend turns like the first (the profile is its outside), else arc; sharp corners keep `radius`). 1.302.0: lofted bend (`smloft`, a base feature like `smbase`: `sketch1`/`sketch2` open line/arc chains, `facets` per arc; `sheet.ts` `loftedBend` samples both at merged arc-length parameters, splits each quad on the shorter diagonal, joins coplanar triangles into panels, unfolds the neutral surface (profiles = bottom, `offsetDir` mitres per vertex) and records `SheetState.folds` (sharp fold lines, r 0, in `flatBends`/`flatBendLines`/`flatDrawing`) and `faceted` (flatBody skips `removedPieces`); kernel `meshSolid` sews the facets (`SheetOp` kind "mesh"), unifies coplanar faces and names them by plane + nearest piece; `isSheetBase` in `types.ts` replaces the `smbase` checks). 1.307.0: cross break (`smcross`: `faces` = panel `:top|:bot` FaceRefs, `radius`, `angle`, `flip`; `sheet.ts` `crossBreak` adds `SheetState.crosses` (`CrossLine` per panel: the outline's farthest pair, then the points farthest either side of it), no op, the body is unchanged; `crossBreakLines` (folded on the face, or the flat top) drawn by controller `drawDecor`; `flatDrawing` layer CROSS_BREAK; controller `pickSmFace` takes only top/bot faces for it). 1.314.0: vent (`smvent`: `sketch`, `rib`/`spar` widths; `sheet.ts` `ventLayout` = lines with an unshared end are ribs (skipped), `sketchProfile` loops: the largest is the boundary, the others must be circles inside (spars); `ventPanel` checks the sketch lies on a panel top/bottom; kernel `ventCut`: boundary prism ±1.02 T about the sketch plane minus rib strips (run one width past the ends) minus spar rings, cut from the body; no sheet op, so the flat pattern gets it through `flatBody` like any cut). 1.321.0: forming tools (`smform`: `face` = a panel `:top|:bot` FaceRef, `tool` dimple/rib/louver/xhole, `pts` in the panel's flat coordinates, `size`/`width`/`height`/`draft`/`angle`, `flip`; `sheet.ts` `formTools` checks and places them and adds `SheetState.forms` (`FormMark`: `loop` = sheet put back under the form, `mark` = footprint, `pierce` = extruded hole pre-pierce radius `piercedRadius`); kernel `formBody` fuses the outer shape on the face it stands out of and cuts the inside (walls in by T/cos(draft), top down by T; louver hood moved down T·√(1+(h/w)²)); `flatBody` leaves forms out (`removedPieces(..., keep)` subtracts the footprints) and cuts pre-pierce holes; `flatDrawing` layer FORM_TOOL; controller `placeForm` (a click on a top/bot face adds a point via `flatPointOn`), panel rows `data-sm=px:i|py:i|rm:i`, `formSizes` defaults from T). 1.328.0: Unfold/Fold (`smunfold`/`smfold` = `SmUnfoldFeature` `face` (fixed panel top/bot), `bends` ids, empty = all; `sheet.ts` `unfoldSheet` keeps `SheetState.unfold` {fixed, anchor, bends} while panels/bends keep their folded M; `bendLinks` (start panel = same M, far panel = outline edge on the strip's far line), `placeSheet` BFS of rigid motions G (flat bend: G_B = G_A∘M_A∘M_B⁻¹), `sheetPlace`, `placedState`, `unfoldBendLines`; kernel `reposition(from, to)` rebuilds the sheet where `to` puts it (flat bends = `stripSolid` named `<bend>:top|…`), moves each piece's removed material rigidly, `unrolled` (fold → flat) or `wrapped` (flat → fold: top-face loops mapped to (s/ρ, ±t) Geom2d lines on cylinders r−ε, r+T+ε, `BRepFill.Face` sides, sewn), added material with the nearest panel; other sheet features throw E-SM-UNFOLDED, `smflat` folds back first; controller `pickUnfold` toggles bends via `bendOfFace`, sets the fixed face). 1.333.0: corner relief (`smrelief`: `corners` = keys `<bend>+<bend>` (sorted), empty = all, `kind` rect/circle/tear, `size` or `ratio` × T; `sheet.ts` `reliefCorners` = bends on one face (same M) whose start lines cross near an end of both with each strip running away from the other's face, `reliefLoop` in flat coordinates (rect u ∈ [−w, baA + w] along `oa` × v along `ob` from `c0`; circle at the centre-line crossing, r = half the zone diagonal + w; tear r = w at `c0`); kernel `reliefCut` cuts each loop's footprint ∩ each panel's slab where the panel lies and ∩ each flat bend strip `wrapped` onto the bend, so `flatBody` unrolls it; controller `pickRelief` toggles the corner nearest the click; panel checkboxes `data-sm=rc:<key>`, `useRatio`, `rratio`). 1.340.0: sheet metal gusset (`smgusset`: `bend` id, `at` (absent = centred), `depth`, `width`; `sheet.ts` `gussetGeom` works in the plane square to the bend at the inside virtual sharp V (X along face A, face B at π − θ): rib = triangle V, d·A, d·B; groove = outside face lines ∩ the chord moved T towards V, wg = w − 2T; checks the chord clears the bend axis by r + T; `SheetState.gussets` `GussetMark` (3D triangles + `e`); kernel `prism3` fuses the rib and cuts the groove, `removedPieces` subtracts every groove (`grooveOf`) so the flat pattern leaves gussets out; `unfoldSheet` refuses E-SM-UNFOLDGUSSET; controller `pickGusset` sets the bend (and `at` from the click when not centred), panel `data-sm=gmid`). 1.348.0: normal cut (`smcut`: `sketch` closed regions, `end` absent = link to thickness (depth T) / blind (`depth`) / through (2 × sheet reach), `flip`; kernel `normalCut` sweeps the `sketchProfile` regions along the sketch normal, meets each panel's mid-surface face (z = T/2) with `BRepAlgoAPI_Common` and pushes each piece through the panel square to it, and each bend's neutral surface via `neutralLoops` (split out of `unrolled`) → flat strip loops → strip ∩ footprint `wrapped` back (radial cut); nothing hit = E-SM-NCUTMISS; the flat pattern gets it through `flatBody` like any cut). Convert to Sheet Metal (`smconvert`, a sheet base like `smbase` (`isSheetBase`): `face` FaceRef, `edges` EdgeRefs on its outline, `thickness`/`radius`/`k`/`gap`; kernel `convertSolid` (SheetApi `face`/`edge` resolve refs on the body) turns the face's wires into a synthetic sketch (ents `e<k>`, points by key) in a frame with z into the solid and runs `baseFlange`, then `sheet.ts` `convertFlanges` = per-edge `addFlange` (length = reach of the face across the edge, angle = between the outward normals, material inside) + `flangeCorners`/`mitre`; the result is `common`ed with the solid when that trims it; controller `pickConvert` (face first, then edges of it), picks and the preview work like fillet: `displayDoc` rolls back to the feature, `previewAt` overlays its faces). Corner relief options: `ReliefKind` "obround" and `SmReliefFeature.round` (rect only); `reliefLoop(c, kind, w, round)` → `sheet.ts` `filletLoop(pts, r | r[], ent)` (convex ccw polygon, each corner rounded, clamped to half the shorter side); obround keeps radius w at the `P(−w, −w)` corner so the face corner stays cut; panel `data-sm=rround` (default T/2). Gusset options: `SmGussetFeature.depth2` (along face B, `PB`), `count`/`spacing` (`gussetSheet` returns `marks`, one `gussetGeom(st, f, shift)` each, shifts (i − (n − 1)/2)·spacing; kernel tags `rib<i>`/`g<i>`); panel `data-sm=gd2|gcount`. Insert Bends (`sminsert`, a sheet base: `face`, `radius`, `k`, `gap`; `convertSolid` shared with Convert: T = distance to the nearest antiparallel face behind the fixed face, bend edges = straight outline edges whose face lies behind the fixed plane and has its own antiparallel face T behind it (so end faces across the thickness are left out); the sheet ∩ solid gets the bend solids fused back, since a thin solid has no material inside large bends; only walls touching the fixed face). 1.356.0: further walls (`convertSolid` walks each wall's far straight edge parallel to the bend edge to the face across it: Insert Bends when that face is a wall (`isWall`), Convert when the edge is picked (`chainEdges`, controller `pickConvert` accepts edges sharing a face with a picked one); the profile in the edge's (outward, into-solid) plane becomes `ConvertLevel`s {angle, L, same, up} and `convertFlanges` adds them on `<pid>:tip` like miter flange levels, panels/bends numbered after the first-level ones; corners mitred on the first level only; 1.407.0: every level is mitred, see below). Corner relief at returns and three-bend corners (`reliefCorners`: a bend whose start panel is another's far panel (`bendLinks`) with its start line near that bend's end = `ret` corner; a return at a two-bend corner joins it: key `<a>+<b>+<c>`, `c`, `box` [u0, u1, v0, v1] covering all three strips, used by `reliefLoop`). Gusset tips (`SmGussetFeature.tip` round / `fillet`; `GussetMark.rr`/`rg`; kernel `prism3(..., round)` fillets the chord's two end edges; `grooveOf` stays sharp, a superset for `removedPieces`; panel `data-sm=gtip|gfil`). 1.391.0: Tab and Slot (`smtabslot`: `edge` = panel outline EdgeRef (`findEdge`), `face` = the slot face (any body; kernel `facePlane` must face the edge, square to the tabs), `length`/`height` (past the slot face)/`count`/`offset` or `spacing`/`clearance`/`round`; `sheet.ts` `tabSpans` places the tabs, `tabSlot` adds each tab as a panel `<fid>:p<k>` (strip `side0|tip|side1|root` beside the edge, so the flat pattern has it; the edge goes into `used`) and returns `SlotCut`s (the tab section grown by the clearance, `filletLoop` when rounded, radius ≤ (2 + √2)·clearance, depth height + clearance), which kernel `slotTool` cuts from the whole body before the tabs are fused; controller `pickTabSlot` (a straight top/bot outline edge = tab edge, else a face = slot face)). 1.401.0: Rip (`smrip`: `edges` EdgeRefs between two flat faces, `gap`, `side` a/b = along the first/second face (absent = both); runs before or without a sheet base; kernel `ripCut`: both ways = a g-wide slot on the bisector through the corner (outside edges too), one way = only inside edges (classifier IN along nB − nA, else E-SM-RIPSIDE), slot in the kept face's plane through the other wall; depth found by a classifier march + bisection, prism along the edge; panel select `data-sm=side`, edges picked by `pickSmEdge`). 1.404.0: Sheet Metal Properties (command `smprops`, not a feature: `cutprops.ts` pure `sheetCutProps(st)` = smallest-area box (`minBox`, rotating calipers, flat X kept on ties), thickness/R/k, bends + folds, net flat area, outer/inner cutting lengths and cut outs by loop containment parity over `flatOutline` (the stored `flatEdges` chained, else `flatLoops` + pre-pierce holes), `cutPropRows`/`cutPropsCsv`; `propsUi.ts` `showSheetProps` fills dialog `#sm-props` rows `tr[data-prop]`; controller `sheetProps` passes mass = volume × `doc.material.density`). Kernel `wireOf(..., cw)` reverses a lone circle hole (a base flange circle used to add the disc on). 1.412.0: further Convert walls mitred at every level (`convertFlanges` builds level by level across the chains, panel/bend ids unchanged via `pid(i, k)`/`bid(i, k)`; level 0 by `mitre`, later levels by `planeCut` on the bisector plane through the fixed face's corner when `sameUpTo`, else `mitre` unless parallel; a level runs on past a corner only when the other chain has it too); `mitre(..., mids)` keeps the side the edge middle (`edgeMid`) projects to; `convertSolid` rims: a face further on that another chain edge or picked wall also touches ends the lip at the nearest parallel edge across the bend edge's middle (`near`), not the farthest. 1.427.0: bend tables (`SmBaseFeature.calc` "table" + `table` `BendTable` {kind k/ba/bd, `radii` mm, `angles` deg, `v`[radius][angle]}; pure `bendTable.ts`: `tableValue` bilinear with ends held, `tableProblem` (E-SM-TABLE in `startState`), `parseBendTable`/`bendTableText` (tab/; or space/comma cells), `dinTable(T)` (K = (0.65 + 0.5·lg(r/T))/2, 0.5 from 5T), `defaultTable(kind, T)`; `bendAllowance` uses `SheetState.table`; panel `tableFields`: `data-sm=tkind|table|tdin`, textarea in `.pm-field.pm-col`; `sheetCutProps` K = a K table at R, 90°). 1.443.0: mitres lean (`Panel.bevels` {o, k, s}: the outline line o·q = k is cut at o·q = k + s·(z − T/2) in the folded solid; `mitre` and `planeCut` add them through `addBevel(P, o, k, N)` with N = the seam plane's normal out of the panel (bisector of the two walls for `mitre`); kernel `panelSolid` → `bevelled` fuses a strip outside each such line and cuts the wedge past the face, so walls meet on the bisector and the flat outline stays the mid-plane) and Auto relief on Convert/Insert Bends (`relief` ReliefKind, absent = none, new drafts `rect`; `reliefRatio` × T; kernel cuts `reliefLoop` for every `reliefCorners` corner right after `convertSolid`; panel `autoRelief`, `data-sm=arelief|aratio`). 1.568.0: Sheet Metal Properties material (`cutprops.ts` `SheetMaterial` {name, density} → rows Material (`text`, `translate="no"`) and Density (g/cm³) in `cutPropRows`/`cutPropsCsv`; controller `sheetProps` weighs shown bodies by `bodies[k].material` through `bodyMasses` + `combine`, `massOverride.mass` wins). Convert lips: `convertFlanges` `stopAtWalls` gives a level-k wall whose corner neighbour stops at level k − 1 `off0`/`off1` up to that neighbour's inner face (− gap), so lip and bend don't run into it. Arrows: `arrowFeature` takes `smhem` (`drawFlangeArrow`, panel `<id>:p0`) and an open-profile `smbase` (`smOpenBase`, `drawBaseArrow` = depth from the sketch's point centre along ±n, mid when `mid`). Edge Flange `end: "vertex"` + `vertex` VertexRef (`edgeFlange(prev, f, at)`: trial `flangeWith` at s = max(s, 1), the vertex in `<id>:p0`'s flat frame gives s from its `att` edge; kernel `SheetApi.vertex` = `Modeller.vertexPos` with the evaluate frames; controller focus `smvertex`, picks with `pickVertex`; no length arrow) and `position: "offset"` + `offset` (`addFlange` with d < 0 adds a coplanar panel `<pid>x` |d| wide beyond the edge, the bend starts at its far side). 1.571.0: Convert lip bends mitred (`Bend.cuts` = half-spaces m·(x − p) > 0 cut off by kernel `bendSolid` via `halfSpace`, cut face `<bend>:miter`; `convertFlanges` adds them at level ≥ 1 corners cut by `planeCut`; `removedPieces` unrolls full − mitred bend off the flat strip). 1.579.0 (sheet metal exam #6/#9/#10/#12): `fit.ts` (pure: `fitSeg` turns an edge's samples back into a line, arc/circle or Catmull-Rom spline, `arcRationals` = quarter-turn rational quadratics) feeds kernel `wireSegs`, so `neutralLoops`/`unrolled` and `wrapped` (pcurves: `Geom2d_Line`, rational/cubic `Geom2d_BezierCurve`, exact under the affine flat → (angle, height) map) keep outlines as a few faces instead of one per sample; `reposition` and `reliefCut` skip pieces by box and use `SheetApi.boolAll` (= `Modeller.fuseAll`/`cutAll`); Unfold refuses only bends with a gusset; `ModelKernel.pump` replaces the worker after any failed model call (an aborted OCCT stays broken) and says `E-KRN-MEMORY` (`KERNEL_MEMORY`) when it ran out of memory; toasts: warn 10 s, error 12 s (`ERROR_MS`, 1.582.0; 20 s in 1.579.0), held while hovered. 1.582.0 (exam #1/#3/#4/#5/#8/#14/#16/#17/#19): `tab` snaps outline corners within min(0.5, 0.1 T) onto the host face outline (corner first, else the nearest line point) and also accepts a host corner on the tab loop; `formBody` joins all outers in one `boolAll` fuse (retried as body + `fuseAll(outers)` when the volume is off: touching louvers dropped one) then all inners in one cut, E-SM-FORMFAIL under half the volume. `miterFlange`: `profIn` = the profile starts on the face the first bend turns towards (offset of its start from mid-thickness along the panel z, beyond T/4), then setbacks/arc radii swap outside/inside per bend; picked edges of different panels whose ends meet (within 3T + 2·gap, lines within T) are corners too, cut on the plane through the lines' closest points bisecting the edge directions, and every level's bends there get `Bend.cuts`. Flat outline: kernel `topEdges` drops edges met twice and `fit.ts` `joinShort` turns runs (≥ 3) of lines under 0.5 mm through degree-2 points into a line/arc/circle/poly (exam #16); controller `flatSheet()` (Sheet Metal Properties, Export flat DXF) asks the worker `flatState` (`ModelKernel.flatState(flatPatternDoc(doc))`, the part's own modeller) when cuts follow the sheet base and the part is folded (exam #14); `toDxf(..., what)` header. 1.584.0 (exam #11/#15): `reposition` fuses the moved added pieces one at a time with a fuzzy value (T·1e-3, `SheetApi.boolAll(..., fuzzy)` → `Modeller.fuseAll(tools, fid, fuzzy)`) when the one fuse leaves a piece apart; `sheet.ts` `flatFormLines` = form footprints on the flat top face, drawn by controller `drawDecor` (0xe8590c) while flattened. 1.589.0 (exam #18): Insert Bends on a face whose outline edges are concave (the floor inside an open box) retries `convertSolid(..., fixed)` with the antiparallel face T behind it, nearest by centroid; `wrapped` makes a side face a plane (`MakeFace_15(wire, true)`) when its two edges are coplanar, so relief cuts on bends take ~0.1 s instead of 1 to 2 s.
- Modify box and panel sizes (1.140.0, FR-UI-114/115, FR-UI-002): `src/modifyBox.ts` `ModifyBox` (fixed on `document.body`, input keeps class `sk-input`; `preview`/`live`, `accept` returns false to stay open, `cancel`); the sketch editor `editDim` previews by solving a copy, the part controller `showInstant` (double-click a face → `instant` feature id), `drawInstant` (`.mc-fdim` labels from `drawing/items.ts` `modelItems`), `editInstant` (⟲ previews through `instantDoc` in `displayDoc`, ✓ = one `store.change`). `src/resize.ts` `makeGrip` (role separator, localStorage `mc:size:<key>`, double-click = default; `#app` columns use `--left-w`/`--right-w`) and `rememberDialogSizes` (`dialog { resize: both }`, `.sized`). `ModelKernel.stop()` rejects calls in flight with `STOPPED` (a parked assembly or part must not wait for ever); `AsmController.gen` ends a build that outlived `close()`.
- `docs/FRS-structural.md`: functional requirements for structural analysis (meshless approach on the exact geometry; phases SA0 spike, SA1 part static, SA2 part modal, SA3 assembly static with connections, SA4 assembly modal; IDs `FR-STU/SUP/LOAD/SOL/RES/CON-…`, tests `AT-SA…`). SA0 spike (1.607.0, report `docs/structural-spike.md`): `src/sim/` pure TS, units mm/N/MPa. `basis.ts` (Legendre products, hierarchical: degree-1 functions first), `quad.ts` (`Inside` may carry `hits(axis, p)` exact line cuts and `hint(lo, hi)` {axis, edge}; `volumePoints` octree → `exactLeaf` Gauss lines split at kinks, edge leaves split 3 levels more; `fitPoints`/`fitFacePoints` moment fitting to (2p+1)ⁿ Gauss points; `gridTrianglePoints` cuts boundary triangles at grid planes), `body.ts` (`MeshBody` over a triangle soup, `insideOf`), `grid.ts` (cells, aggregation under 25 %, `Cell.fine` + `Grid.faces` kept so `refit(g, q, fit)` serves the next degree), `static.ts` `solveStatic` (SIPG γ = 3(λ+2μ)(p+1)²/h, Nitsche fixed/`sliding` supports, force/pressure loads, `disp`/`stress`, `grid` option reuses a pass's cells), `sparse.ts` (`BlockMatrix`, RCM + `Envelope` Cholesky), `pcg.ts` (CG with block Gauss–Seidel + exact degree-1 coarse solve; used above `DIRECT_LIMIT` 6 000 unknowns). `src/kernel/simgeo.ts` `simGeometry(oc, shape, faces, lin, ang)` = double-precision triangles with their face index, `faceTris`. p = 1 locks in bending: passes start at p = 2. Tests `tests/unit/sim.test.ts`, `sim-part.test.ts` (bracket via `Modeller`). SA1 static studies (1.608.0): `PartDoc.studies` (`src/sim/types.ts` `Study`: `supports` fixed/sliding/hinge/dirs and `loads` force/pressure/moment/gravity with `faces` FaceRefs, `settings.passes`, `suppressed` ids; values in N, MPa, N·mm, mm/s²; results are never stored). `static.ts`: `Support.mode`, `Load.moment`, `gravity`, `reactions`/`applied`, `freeModes` (E-SIM-FREE: n before solving), `sample(pos, nrm)`. `run.ts` `studyProblem(st, body)` (problems E-SIM-REF/EMPTY/HINGE per item) and generator `runStatic` (passes p = 2, 3, 4 on one grid, `PassResult` per pass, stops when the energy change ≤ `energyTol` %); `sim.worker.ts` + `client.ts` `SimClient.solve(input, onPass)`/`cancel` (E-SIM-STOPPED). Kernel worker request `simGeo` (`ModelKernel.simGeo(lin, ang)`: `lastBody` as fine triangles with face names, `solids`). `plotmesh.ts` `refineMesh` (longest-edge bisection of the display mesh to edges ≤ model size/70, crack-free, capped by `plotTarget`): results are sampled and drawn on it, never on the two-triangle CAD faces. UI `src/sim/ui.ts` `SimController` (main.ts `sim`, debug `__sv.sim.state()`): command tab `simulation` pane `#sim-tools` (`[data-s=new|mat|fixed|sliding|hinge|dirs|force|pressure|moment|gravity|solve|stop|results]`), side tab `#t-sim`/`#tab-sim` with panel `#sim-pm` (✓/✕, `.sim-faces`, inputs `data-n=value|c0..2|m0..2|g0..2`, checkboxes `data-k`) and tree `#sim-tree` (rows `.sim-row[data-id]` with `data-a=edit|toggle|del`, `select[data-k=passes]`, `.sim-kv`, `table.sim-passes`), legend `#sim-legend` (`data-k=kind|deformed|scale`). It reaches the part through `ModelController.simAccess()` and `simHook` (view clicks and Enter/Esc while a panel is open, `changed()` on every store change/open/close) and `partListeners` → `rebuilt()`. The plot is an overlay mesh on `part.mesh.parent` (`userData.simPlot`), the part's mesh material is swapped for a ghost (deformed) or an invisible one and put back by `clearPlot`; results are stale when `keyOf` (features, rollback, vars, material, study set-up) differs. Marks: `marks` group on `part.group` (`userData.simMark`/`simArrow`). Smoke block "sim 1.608" (shard 1), hands-on `tests/human/sim.mjs`. 1.609.0: `simHook.plotting()` makes `drawPlanes` leave planes off a plot (`SimHost.redraw` = `drawDecor`); probes (`probing`, `probes` = plot-mesh triangle + weights, `probeAt` raycasts the overlay, labels `#sim-probes .sim-probe[data-probe]` with `[data-rm]`, placed on `viewer.onRender`; legend `data-a=probe|noprobes`; `active` is true while probing so view clicks come through `simHook.click`; `state().probes` = values). 1.610.0: error estimate: `solveStatic({ prev })` (the pass before's coefficients on the same grid; hierarchical basis, so they are padded with zeros) → `eta` per element = strain energy of the difference, `prevError` % in the energy norm, `sample().err` local %; `run.ts` `PassResult.errors` (one per pass: known from the next pass, the last = `passError` extrapolation or null) and `err`; UI plot kind `err` (from pass 2), `dd[data-v=error]` (`≈` extrapolated, `<` bound), passes table column, `convergenceChart` `svg.sim-conv`, `state().errors`. Quadrature: `volumePoints(..., rich)` gives whole octants of a cut cell `LEAF_RICH` 5 Gauss points per direction (3 lost strain modes at p = 4 and made the matrix indefinite when a face lay near a cell mid-plane), cut faces start from 5; `makeGrid` ignores an overhang under 1e-4 cell; the penalty of an element is divided by its root cell's volume fraction (`gam`, at most ×5). 1.611.0: report (FR-REP-001): `src/sim/report.ts` (layout only: `SimReport` blocks h/p/table/image/chart, `chartGeo`/`chartSvg` shared with the Study tab, `reportHtml` self-contained page, `reportPdf(jsPDF, rep, fontBase64)` A4 with DejaVu Sans); UI `report(as?)` (bar `data-s=report`, tree `data-a=report`, `chooseModal` pdf/html), `reportOf` builds the blocks in the UI language, `capture(kind)` draws vm/u/sf plots of the current view into a canvas with the colour scale and puts the plot back; `state().report`; results show the FR-RES-060 statement (`.sim-assume`). Tests `tests/unit/sim-report.test.ts`. 1.612.0: forces on edges and points (FR-LOAD-001): `StudyLoad.on` edges/points with `edges` `StudyEdge` {faces: the two face names, hint} or `points` `StudyPoint` {faces meeting there, at}, components only; `run.ts` `edgeLines(body, fa, fb)` (triangle edges both faces share in the solver's tessellation, 9 numbers: ends + a direction into the body) and `cornerPoint(body, faces, at)` (vertex common to all the faces nearest `at`); `static.ts` `Load.lines` (cut at grid planes, Gauss per piece, force per length) / `Load.points` (equal shares), located by `cellNear`; UI panel select `data-k=on`, `pickEdgeOrPoint` through host `snapAt` (snapper pick in part coordinates), `pickCount`/`pickText`, marks = LineSegments / Points with `userData.simMark`. 1.613.0: local refinement: `BlockMatrix(nel, m | sizes)` (`ms`/`off` per element; `Envelope` and `pcg` follow), `solveStatic({ degrees })` (per element of `grid`, `p` = their highest: leading functions of the hierarchical basis, each cell integrated by its own degree via `refit(g, q, fit, degree)`, faces by the higher side, penalty by the element's degree; `x` is returned padded to the highest degree's layout); `run.ts` `GLOBAL_PASSES` 3 / `MAX_PASSES` 6 / `LOCAL_DEGREE` 6, `markElements(ind, deg, share = 0.6)` (fewest elements holding the share of the indicator; an element's indicator = its `eta` when its degree was last raised), `PassResult.raised`; UI passes select 1..6, `input[data-k=tol]` (`settings.energyTol`, 0..20), `.sim-local` notes, `state().passes[].raised`. 1.614.0: hot spots (FR-RES-004, FR-SOL-015, FR-RES-012): pure `hotspots.ts` `hotSpots(pos, index, passes, radius, count = 5)` (local maxima of von Mises on the plot mesh, vertices welded by place, peaks within `radius` = model size/12 are one; `singular` = the peak near the point grew > `SINGULAR_STEP` 8 % in each of the last two passes and > `SINGULAR_GROWTH` 25 % over both while the energy change ≤ `SETTLED` 2 %; the list runs on until `count` real peaks); UI `hotList()` (memo per pass), `table.sim-hot` rows `tr[data-a=hot][data-hot]` (`.sing`, `.sel`; a click sets `hotSel` and shows the stress plot), `dd[data-v=maxvm|realvm|sf]` (the safety factor uses the highest real peak when a singularity leads), `.sim-sing`; labels `.sim-peak[data-peak=h<i>|max|min]` (`.warn` = singular) share `#sim-probes` with the probes (`pins`, `peakLabels`), legend checkbox `data-k=marks`; `state().hot`/`labels`. Tests `tests/unit/sim-hot.test.ts` (L bracket, sharp and rounded). 1.615.0: supports on edges and points (`StudySupport.on`/`edges`/`points` for fixed and dirs, `types.ts` `ON_TYPES`; `static.ts` `Support.lines`/`points` held by a penalty 2γh per length / 2γh² per point (`heldAt`, `onLines` shared with edge loads), reaction = −w P u, `freeModes` samples them and adds a faint whole-body term so rank-deficient supports compare soundly; `run.ts` `linesOrPoints`; panel select `data-k=on` labelled Holds) and probes kept in the study (`Study.probes` = points of the undeformed model, not in `keyOf`; pure `probes.ts` `locate` (nearest point of the plot mesh), `valueAt`, `probeCsv`; UI `setProbes` (undo steps), `syncProbes`, legend `data-a=probecsv`, `state().probeAt`/`probeCsv`). Tests `tests/unit/sim-probes.test.ts`. 1.616.0: `values.ts` (FR-LOAD-021: `loadValue(text, type, vars)` = `expr.ts` number expression with a trailing unit per load type (N/kN/lbf, MPa/psi/bar, N·m, m/s²/g; a unit counts only after a digit, space or bracket) and global variables as plain numbers, `typedValue` → `Num` in N/MPa/N·mm/mm/s² with `expr` kept unless a plain number, `shownValue`, `studyTargets`, `refreshStudyValues`; `params.ts` `refreshExprs` and `renameEverywhere` cover them), results in the disk cache (FR-SOL-030: after a finished solve `cachePut(cacheKey("sim", keyOf))` of `Kept` {passes, ids, ms, mesh}; `restore()` from `changed()` once per doc + study state (`asked`) when there are no results, sets `res.cached`, plot off; `.sim-kept` note, `state().cached`/`cacheHits`), probe table `table.sim-ptable` in the Study tab (`probesHtml`, `[data-a=rmprobe][data-i]`, `[data-a=probecsv]`). Tests `tests/unit/sim-values.test.ts`, smoke "sim 1.616". SA2 modal studies (1.617.0): `Study.kind` "modal" (`newStudy(list, kind)`, `settings.modes`, `MAX_MODES` 30, `MODAL_PASSES` 3; supports only). `solveStatic({ modal: { count, start } })` skips the free check, builds the consistent mass per element (`Mel`, same for each component), shifts K by σM (σ = 1e-4 E/ρL²) when `freeModes` > 0 and runs `eigen.ts` `subspace` (subspace iteration + Rayleigh–Ritz `reduced`, direct `Envelope` up to `MODAL_DIRECT` unknowns, else `pcg.ts` `pcgSolver` kept for all right-hand sides with warm starts); result `modes` (`Mode` hz/lambda/rigid/eff/x with φᵀMφ = 1), `use(k)` points `sample`/`disp` at mode k, `space` = the next pass's start (padded: hierarchical basis), `mass`. `run.ts` `runModal` (passes p = 2, 3, 4, `PassResult.modes`/`shapes` scaled to largest displacement 1/`mass`, `change` = largest elastic frequency change %); worker request type "modal" when `RunInput.modal`. UI: bar `data-s=newmodal`, tree `[data-v=kind]`, `input[data-k=modes]`, `modalHtml` (`dd[data-v=f1|mass]`, `table.sim-modes` rows `tr[data-a=mode][data-mode]`, `.rigid`, `.sim-rigid` note), legend `select[data-k=mode]` + `[data-k=animate]` (`startAnim`: rAF swing of the overlay positions, `MODE_PERIOD`), plot kinds `MODE_PLOTS`, `modalReport`; `keyOf` appends "modal"; `state().kind/modes/mode/mass/animating/frames`. Tests `tests/unit/sim-modal.test.ts`, smoke "sim 1.617", hands-on `tests/human/modal.mjs`. 1.618.0: `SimController.recordGif(maxW = 640)` (legend `[data-a=gif]`: `GIF_FRAMES` 24 over one swing, the overlay posed per frame, `viewer.bareKeep` + `render()`, `motion/gif.ts` `encodeGif`; `state().gifs`/`gif`) and `Study.excite` (text, not in `keyOf`; pure `excite.ts` `parseExcitation` Hz/kHz/rpm, `nearExcitation` ±`EXCITE_BAND` 10 %; tree `input[data-k=excite]`, rows `tr.near`, `.sim-excite(.warn)`, report line, `state().near`). Tests `tests/unit/sim-excite.test.ts`, smoke "sim 1.618". 1.619.0: `settings.range` {from, to} Hz (tree `select[data-k=find]` count|range, inputs `data-k=rfrom|rto`; `RunInput.modal.range`/`max`: `runModal` doubles the count from `DEFAULT_MODES` until a mode lies above the range or `MAX_MODES` (now 50) are found, keeps the modes in the range, `PassResult.more` → `.sim-more`; none = `.sim-norange`), view-only `cumul` (`[data-k=cumul]`, `modeRows(r, true)` running sums, also a report table) and `speed` (legend `select[data-k=speed]`, animation and GIF fps); `state().speed/cumul/more/range`. `tests/unit/sim-plate.test.ts` = free square plate benchmark (AT-SA2-3). Smoke "sim 1.619". 1.620.0: multibody parts are analysed bonded (AT-SA2-5): `Modeller.bonded(body)` = `fuseAll` of `bodyList` (names carried, contact faces gone; `parts` solids before, `solids` pieces after), used by the kernel worker's `simGeo` (reply `solids`, `parts`); UI: `solids` ≠ 1 = E-SIM-BODIES (a body touches nothing), a body with its own `bodies[k].material` = E-SIM-BODYMATS, `Results.parts` (kept in the disk cache) → `.sim-bonded` note, `state().bodies`. Tests `tests/unit/sim-bonded.test.ts`, smoke "sim 1.620", hands-on `tests/human/bonded.mjs`. 1.621.0 (FR-SUP-005/006, FR-LOAD-007): support types `elastic` (`kn`/`kt` N/mm³ → `Support.spring`: a Robin term w N_a N_b (kt I + (kn − kt) n nᵀ), reaction −K u; `mode` sliding/tangent/fixed only feeds `freeModes`) and `disp` (`dirs` + `disp` mm → `Support.value` on mode dirs: Nitsche right-hand side −(P σ(v) n)·g + 2γ (P g)·v, edges/points by the penalty; `energy` is then the integrated strain energy; in `ON_TYPES`), load type `centrifugal` (`value` rpm via `values.ts`, `axis` x/y/z at the origin or cylindrical `faces` → `run.ts` `axisLine`, `accel` rad/s² → `StudyProblem.spin` → `StaticProblem.spin` body force ρ(ω² r⊥ − α a × r); one per study, E-SIM-ONESPIN). E-SIM-NOLOAD is not raised when a support moves or the part spins. Panel: plain numbers in `input[data-q=kn|kt|d0..2|accel]`, `.sim-dv` rows, `select[data-k=axis]`. Tests `tests/unit/sim-loads2.test.ts`, smoke "sim 1.621", hands-on `tests/human/loads2.mjs`. 1.622.0 (FR-LOAD-005/008/009): load types `torque` (`value` N·mm, `flip`; cylindrical faces → `axisLine`, axis turned by `run.ts` `positiveAxis` towards +X/+Y/+Z → `Load.torque` {p, axis, value}: traction k a × r with k scaled so the total is the value), `bearing` (`comp` N, the part along the axis left out, none left = E-SIM-BEARING → `Load.bearing`: pressure ∝ max(0, −n·f̂), scaled so the resultant is the force) and `hydro` (`value` kg/m³, `axis` + `flip` = up, `level` = the surface's coordinate on that axis → `Load.hydro` {p, down, gamma = ρ·1e-12·`GRAVITY`}; `static.ts` `wetted` cuts the triangles at the surface). Panel: `select[data-k=up]`, `input[data-q=level]`. Smoke "sim 1.622", hands-on `tests/human/loads3.mjs`. 1.623.0 (FR-LOAD-006): load type `remote` (`at` point mm, `comp` force N, `mom` moment N·mm; `studyProblem` turns it into a `force` load plus a `moment` load of mom + (at − centre) × force on the same faces, the centre being `momentField`'s; `values.ts` `studyTargets` gives each kept expression its `type`, `mom` as "moment"). Panel: `input[data-q=a0..2]`, `[data-k=pickat]` (`pickingAt`: the next view click sets the point from `snapAt`, else the face hit), force `data-n=c0..2`, moment `data-n=r0..2`; marks = a dot at the point joined to the faces' centre. Smoke "sim 1.623". 1.624.0 (FR-SOL-012): `Study.refine` `StudyRefine` {id `r<n>`, type "refine", faces, level 1..`MAX_REFINE` 3; in `keyOf` only when present, so older cached results keep their key} → `StudyProblem.refine` {tris, level}; `runStatic` then makes the grid itself (`makeGrid` as the first pass would), `boostOf(grid, refine)` = degrees ahead per element (the elements holding the faces' cut triangles), and the global passes run with `degrees` = min(`LOCAL_DEGREE`, k + 2 + boost); local passes go on from those. UI: bar `data-s=refine` (Solve group), `Edit.kind` "refine", panel `select[data-k=level]`, rows in `ul.sim-refs` under Accuracy (`.sim-dot.ref`, blue marks `REFINE_COLOR`), report table, `state().refine`. Static studies only. Tests `tests/unit/sim-refine.test.ts`, smoke "sim 1.624", hands-on `tests/human/refine.mjs`. 1.625.0 (FR-RES-030): results on section planes. `runStatic(inp, keep)` hands each pass's `Sampler` (`solveStatic().sample(pos, nrm, hits)`; `hit` = inside the grid's box) to the worker, which keeps the last one (`kept`, by solve id) and answers request `sample` {of, pos, scale} with `run.ts` `sampleAt` (scale > 0: points of the deformed shape traced back by 4 fixed-point steps); `SimClient.sample(pos, scale)` / `solution` (0 while solving, after a stop, for modal studies and for results from the disk cache). `SectionManager.capSource` (geometry cut by the stencil passes: the deformed overlay) and `onCaps` (end of `syncCaps`); `SimHost.section`; UI `capsSoon` (section `rebuild` after every plot change), `capsChanged` → `paintCaps`: each cap gets a `CAP_GRID` 160 plane geometry with vertex colours on the plot's range (`userData.simCap`, values outside the cells taken from neighbours), `solved` = the results the worker holds, legend note `[data-v=capnote]` when it holds none, `state().caps` {n, painted, points, hit, min, max}. Static studies only. Tests `tests/unit/sim-section.test.ts`, smoke "sim 1.625", hands-on `tests/human/section.mjs`. 1.626.0 (FR-RES-031): results along a path. `Study.path` = two points of the undeformed model (not in `keyOf`); pure `path.ts` (`pathPoints` 101 points, `pathRows`, `pathCsv`, `pathChart`/`pathChartSvg` `svg.sim-pathchart`, gaps where the path is outside the part); the worker's `sample` request takes `solid` (`sampleAt(..., inside)` → `SampleResult.solid`). UI: legend `[data-a=path]` (`pathing`, `pathFirst`, hint `[data-v=pathhint]`, `plotPointAt` shared with probes), `setPath` (undo step), `syncPath` (values from the plot surface via `locate` at once, then from the kept solution: `pathData.source` surface|solution, note `[data-v=pathsurface]`), `drawPath` (`userData.simPath` on the part group), Study tab `pathHtml` (`[data-v=pathinfo]`, `[data-a=pathcsv|rmpath]`), `state().path`. Static studies only. Tests in `tests/unit/sim-section.test.ts`, smoke "sim 1.626", hands-on `tests/human/path.mjs`. 1.627.0 (FR-LOAD-022): load cases. `Study.cases` `StudyCase` {id `c<n>`, name, off = load ids left out} (`types.ts` `newCase`, `caseStudy(st, c)` = the study with those loads suppressed; ids and `off` in `keyOf`, names not); UI `solve` runs `studyProblem(caseStudy)` per case one after another (`SimClient.solve(..., more)` keeps the earlier solutions in the worker's `kept` map, `sample(..., of)`), `Results.cases` `CaseResult` {id, name, passes, job}, `caseSel` (−1 = envelope) and `applyCase` point `res.passes` at the shown case or at `[run.ts envelope(last passes)]` (max von Mises/error per point, displacement of the case moving the point most; no reactions, no solution, so section caps stay plain and a path reads the surface); `job()` = the shown results' solution id; a stop or error part-way drops all the cases' results. Study tab: `.sim-cases` (`.sim-case[data-c]` with `input[data-k=cname]`, `[data-a=rmcase]`, `input[data-k=cload][data-l]`), `[data-a=addcase]` (shown from 2 loads, `MAX_CASES` 8), results `select[data-k=case]` + `table.sim-casetable` rows `tr[data-a=case][data-case]`, `.sim-env`; cases in the disk cache (`Kept.cases`) and the report; `state().cases/caseSel/enveloped/caseResults`. Static studies only. Tests `tests/unit/sim-cases.test.ts`, smoke "sim 1.627", hands-on `tests/human/cases.mjs`. 1.628.0 (FR-SUP-007): inertia relief. `StudySettings.relief` (static; in `keyOf` through the settings) → `studyProblem` leaves every support out and sets `StudyProblem.relief` → `StaticProblem.relief`: `solveStatic` skips the free check, takes mass, centre of mass and inertia tensor from the cells' quadrature points, a = F/m and α = I⁻¹M about the centre of mass from `applied`, adds the body force −ρ(a + α × r) (not counted in `applied`), then solves K + σM (σ = 1e-4 E/ρL², as the modal shift) and corrects once with σM x on the right (`reliefM`), so the result has no rigid motion; result `accel` {a mm/s², alpha rad/s², com, mass} → `PassResult.accel`. E-SIM-DENSITY without a density. UI: `input[data-k=relief]` in the Supports section (`.sim-relieved` greys the rows, note `RELIEF_NOTE`), `dd[data-v=accel|alpha]`, report lines, `state().relief/accel`; `pct()` writes percentages under 0.01 as "< 0.01" in the passes table. Tests `tests/unit/sim-relief.test.ts` (free bar pulled and pushed, against beam theory), smoke "sim 1.628", hands-on `tests/human/relief.mjs`. 1.629.0: bodies with their own materials. `grid.ts`: `Cell.dom`, `Grid.maps` (one cell map per domain; `at(i, j, k, d)`, `domainOf`, `cellOf(x, y, z, dom?)`), `makeGrid({ domains })` builds each body's cells from its own `Inside`; `aggregate` joins slivers per round from a snapshot (a row of slivers used to chain into one long element) and cells with no volume are dropped. `static.ts` `StaticProblem.domains` {inside, mat, tris}: `lam`/`mu` follow the element (`setMat(e)`, `domE`), density per element (`rhoOf`), and the domains are joined at the points of one's surface that lie on another's (a step of 2e-3 h along the normal ends inside it) by the interior-penalty `pair` terms with each side's own tractions; `sample().dom`. `run.ts` `RunInput.regions` {tris, mat} → `domainsOf` (undefined when the materials are the same: the bonded single solve of 1.620), `PassResult.dom`. Kernel worker `simGeo` also returns `regions` {key (Bodies folder key, `lastParts`), tris} for a multibody part. UI: `Results.bodyMats` {name, sy} by `dom` (kept in the disk cache), `yieldAt`/`hasYield`/`lowSf` (safety factor per body), `keyOf` adds the bodies' materials when any, E-SIM-BODYMAT (a body material without E or ν), tree `.sim-bodymats`, report table `bodyMatBlocks`, `state().bodyMats`. Tests `tests/unit/sim-bodymats.test.ts`, smoke "sim 1.629", hands-on `tests/human/bodymats.mjs`. SA3 assemblies (1.630.0): `AsmDoc.studies`; `static.ts` `StaticProblem.gap` (domains are joined where one's surface point, stepped `gap` + 2e-3 h along its normal, lies in another) and `links` {a, b (domain indices), type sliding (only the normal part of the jump and traction is coupled, `slideN`) | none}; `StaticResult.contacts` `Contact` {a, b, area, type}; `freeBodies(sets, nd)` (one `RigidSet` per support and pair, 6·nd Jacobi) → `E-SIM-LOOSE: i,j` naming the domains nothing holds. `run.ts` `RunInput.asm` {gap, links} (domains always made), `PassResult.contacts`. `kernel/simgeo.ts` `asmSimGeometry` (face names `<comp>@<face>`, one region per solid), worker request `asmSimGeo` → `ModelKernel.asmSimGeo(doc, lin, ang)`. `Study.links` `StudyLink` {a, b = region keys `<comp>` or `<comp>#<solid>`, type}, `settings.gap` (`DEFAULT_GAP` 0.1). `src/sim/model.ts` `SimModel` (geometry, faces, edges, frame, meshes: what a study is drawn on) = `partModel(part)` or `asmModel(viewer, which)` (the shown components joined into one world-coordinate mesh, frame `sim-frame` in `viewer.modelRoot`); the UI uses `SimHost.model()` (never `viewer.parts[0]`), `stateKey()`, `asm()`, `doc()`. `AsmController.simAccess()`/`simHook` (view clicks, `changed()` from `renderAll`; study changes are undo steps without a rebuild); main.ts switches the SimController's host with the live document (`partSim`/`asmSim`), and the part's `simHook.active` is false while an assembly is live. Connections table one cell with `span[data-v=cforce|cmoment]` (two columns made the tab scroll sideways) (title = components), note `[data-v=cforcenote]`, report columns, `state().contacts[].force/moment/at`. 1.632.0 (FR-CON-003/004): Find connections before solving: pure `connect.ts` `findConnections(bodies, gap, only?)` (each triangle near the other body's box cut into pieces ≤ smaller size/30, a piece is on the other body when a step of eps or the gap along its normal is inside it (`MeshBody`), gap by bisection; `Connection` {a, b, area, gap = area mean, at, pa, pb = both sides' patch triangles}); worker request `connect`, `SimClient.find(bodies, gap)` in a worker of its own (runs beside a solve, a newer search ends the older); in `keyOf` and `foundKey` when present); the UI's `solve` drops left-out components with `run.ts` `withoutFaces` (triangles gone, names blanked so items on them give E-SIM-REF, which `errorText` explains) and from the regions, the plot mesh is made from the kept faces only, `Results.off` → `showPlot` gives those meshes a ghost material; `SimHost.comps()` (asm `simAccess().comps`), Study tab `compsHtml` (`.sim-comps`, `ul.sim-complist li[data-comp]`, `input[data-k=compon][data-c]`, `[data-a=compmat][data-c]` → `pickCompMat`), `state().compList/excluded`. 1.634.0 (FR-CON-030): results by component: pure `comps.ts` `componentRows(dom, vm, u, count, sy)` (per solver domain on the plot mesh) and `aloneIndex(index, dom, d)` (other triangles made degenerate, so face indices keep naming the same triangle); The part smoke block is split: second half (1.621 on) in shard 2. 1.636.0 (rest of FR-CON-001): rigid components. `StudyComp.rigid` (Study tab `[data-a=comprigid][data-c]` toggle, `aria-pressed`; `toggleRigid`) → `RunInput.regions[].rigid` → `StaticProblem.domains[].rigid`: `grid.ts` `GridOptions.rigid` makes one element over all of a rigid domain's cells, `static.ts` keeps it at degree 1 (12 unknowns: six rigid motions + six uniform strains) with E = `RIGID_STIFF` 1e3 × the stiffest elastic material (density kept), joins to an elastic domain carry only the elastic side's traction and penalty (also in the connection forces), `sample` gives a rigid domain no stress and no error, `eta` 0 so it is never refined; the UI needs no E/ν for it, E-SIM-ALLRIGID when every component is rigid, `Results.bodyMats[d].rigid` → `[data-v=crigid]` cell in `table.sim-comptable` and the report, `state().compList[].rigid`. Tests `tests/unit/sim-rigid.test.ts` (rigid block on a cantilever against beam theory), smoke "sim 1.636" (shard 3), hands-on `tests/human/simasm.mjs` SAS-12. 1.637.0 (FR-CON-020/021, FR-LOAD-010): bolts. `static.ts` `StaticProblem.bolts` `Bolt` {a, b = patch triangles, d, E, preload}: `patchOf` turns a patch into 6 rows over the unknowns of its elements (mean displacement, mean rotation J⁻¹∫r × u), `beamStiffness` (12 × 12 Timoshenko beam of round section in global axes) is added as Wᵀ K W over every pair of those elements, a `RigidSet` per bolt feeds `freeBodies`; preload: `tighten` solves a unit pair of forces per preloaded bolt, then the small system that leaves each preload in its bolt once all are tight (E-SIM-PRELOAD when nothing but the bolt resists, i.e. the clamped parts are not joined), `energy` is then the integrated strain energy; result `bolts` `BoltResult` {axial, shear, moment, torsion, a, b, length} → `PassResult.bolts`; `RunInput.bolts` (modal solves take the stiffness only). Pure `bolts.ts`: `findBolts(bodies, fast, gap = 0.05)` (per screw `FastenerBody` {body, kind screw|nut|washer, d, pitch, o, z = axis towards the head}: nuts and washers on its axis; `findConnections` between fasteners and the other bodies gives the pieces lying on them; head patch = pieces looking at the head, far patch = pieces the nut presses on looking away, else the walls of another body (tapped hole); `missed` head|nut|thread), `stressArea`, `boltCheck` (stress on the stress area from tension + bending and shear + torsion, SF against the proof stress). `asm/fasteners.ts` `fastenerOfId` (role, d, pitch from the part id `fastener:<kind>:<size>[x<len>]`), `PROPERTY_CLASSES`/`proofStress`/`DEFAULT_CLASS`; asm `simAccess().comps()[].fastener` {role, d, pitch, o, z}. Worker request `bolts`, `SimClient.bolts(bodies, fast)` (own worker). `Study.bolts` `StudyBolt` by screw component id {off = analysed as a solid, cls, preload N or N·mm with `torque`, k}, `boltPreload`, `NUT_FACTOR` 0.2; `keyOf` has `boltSettings` (off and preload, not the class). UI: `boltsOf(st, regions)` (kept per `boltKey` in `boltsFound`; a bolt's fasteners join `off` for the solve and for Find connections), Study tab `boltsHtml` (`.sim-bolts`, `ul.sim-boltlist li[data-bolt]` with `input[data-k=bolton]`, `[data-v=boltnote]`, `.sim-boltset` `select[data-k=boltcls]` / `input[data-k=boltpre]` / `select[data-k=boltby]` n|nm; `.sim-boltall` with `data-c="*"` from 2 screws), `setBolt`, results `boltRows()` → `table.sim-bolttable` rows `tr[data-bolt]` (`[data-v=baxial|bshear|bsf]`, `.bad` under 1), report table, marks `userData.simBolt` (line + dots between the patch centres, `BOLT_COLOR`), `Results.bolts` `BoltKept` (in the disk cache), `state().bolts/boltList/boltMarks`. Tests `tests/unit/sim-bolts.test.ts`, smoke "sim 1.637" (shard 5 since 1.640.0: shard 2 hit the 12 min limit), hands-on `tests/human/simbolt.mjs`. Same release: asm `holeNear` takes a click on the face beside a hole seen straight on (no ray meets the wall then: the nearest cylinder of that part whose rim is within 0.6 r of the hit), toolbox parts get steel with E and ν (older ones are given them in a study), `.sim-tree` is one `minmax(0, 1fr)` column so a wide row cannot widen the Study tab. 1.640.0 (FR-CON-022): welds. `static.ts` `StaticProblem.welds` `Weld` {lines = 9 numbers per segment as `edgeLines` gives (ends + a direction into the body the edge is on), k N/mm² per length, default the softer body's E}: Gauss points along the lines; domain a = the body at the point stepped along the direction, domain b = another body found a step (or the gap) away in 26 directions, points beside none are skipped (none at all = `E-SIM-WELD: i`); K += k w N_a N_b I over both elements (a line spring between the two displacements), a `RigidSet` per weld and domain pair feeds `freeBodies` (one straight weld alone is a hinge → E-SIM-LOOSE); result `welds` `WeldResult` {force = what a passes to b, length, peak and mean force per length, at} → `PassResult.welds`; `RunInput.welds`. `Study.welds` `StudyWeld` {id `w<n>`, type "weld", edges `StudyEdge[]`, throat mm, `DEFAULT_THROAT` 3; ids + edges in `keyOf`, the throat not}. UI (assemblies only): bar `data-s=weld` (Solve group), `Edit.kind` "weld" (edge picks only, `input[data-q=throat]`), section `.sim-sec.sim-welds` between Bolts and Connections (rows like supports, `.sim-dot.weld`, toggle = `suppressed`), results `weldRows()` → `table.sim-weldtable` rows `tr[data-weld]` (`[data-v=wforce|wpeak|wstress]`, stress = peak / throat), report table, orange marks `WELD_COLOR`, `Results.welds` (in the disk cache), `state().welds/weldList`. Tests `tests/unit/sim-welds.test.ts`, smoke "sim 1.640", hands-on `tests/human/simweld.mjs`. Same release (FR-CON-023): connectors and masses, in parts and assemblies, static and modal. `static.ts` `StaticProblem.connectors` `Connector` {a, b patch triangles, k [kx, ky, kz] N/mm (absent = rigid), kr N·mm/rad (absent = no relative turn)}: both patches through `patchOf` (the bolts' six mean motions), tied at the midpoint of their centres: d = u_b + [h]× θ_b − u_a + [h]× θ_a, φ = θ_b − θ_a, K = Bᵀ D B assembled with the bolts' loop (`connSet`); rigid = 1e2 × stiffest E × R (R = √(patch area / 2)), turning × R²; its `RigidSet` holds only the directions with a stiffness (at the midpoint alone when it does not resist turning); result `connectors` `ConnectorResult` {force and moment a passes to b, stretch, a, b}; `E-SIM-CONNECTOR: i`. `StaticProblem.masses` `FaceMass` {tris, mass t}: `massPts` (element, point, mass) through `eachMass` add to gravity, spin, inertia relief (mass, centre, inertia, body force, σM) and the modal mass (`Mel`, `Sel`, `mass`); `E-SIM-MASS`. `run.ts` `studyProblem` makes both (`StudyProblem.connectors`/`connectorIds`/`masses`; E-SIM-EMPTY, E-SIM-REF, E-SIM-SPRING for a spring with no stiffness), `PassResult.connectors`. `types.ts` `Study.connectors` `StudyConnector` {id `k<n>`, faces, faces2, kind rigid|spring, k, kr}, `Study.masses` `StudyMass` {id `m<n>`, faces, mass kg}, `StudyExtra`, `extrasOf(st)` (welds, connectors, masses), `DEFAULT_SPRING`. UI: bar group Connections (`data-s=weld|connector|mass`), `Edit.kind` "connector" (boxes `.sim-faces[data-side=1|2]`, `connSide`; a face is on one side only; `select[data-k=ckind]`, `input[data-q=k0|k1|k2|kr]`, kr typed in N·m/rad, empty = rigid) and "mass" (`input[data-q=mass]`), sections `.sim-conns` / `.sim-masses` after Supports, results `connRows()` → `table.sim-conntable` rows `tr[data-conn]` (`[data-v=kforce|kmoment|kstretch]`), report `connBlocks`, `Results.conns` (in the disk cache), `state().conns/connList/massList/connSide`. Tests `tests/unit/sim-connectors.test.ts`, smoke "sim 1.640" (the modal block in shard 1 and the assembly block in shard 3), hands-on `tests/human/simconn.mjs`. SA4 (1.641.0): a modal study of an assembly runs through the same path (regions, links, bolts without preload, welds, connectors, masses); tests `tests/unit/sim-asm-modal.test.ts` (AT-SA4-1/2). Compare (FR-STU-023): pure `compare.ts` (`Summary` = peak values of a result: static maxVm/maxU/sf, modal mass kg + elastic frequencies; `noteSummary(store, id, s)` keeps `now` and, when the state key differs, the older one as `before`; `compareRows(a, b)` rows maxvm|maxu|sf or mass|f1..f6 (`COMPARE_MODES`) with change %; `parseSummaries`), UI `noteResult()` after a finished solve and a cache restore (localStorage `mc:simSum:<doc id>`, key = hash of `keyOf`), `compareWith`/`compared`/`compareHtml` (`select[data-k=cmp]` values `prev` | study id, view-only `cmpSel`; `table.sim-cmptable` rows `tr[data-cmp]`, `[data-v=cmpnone]`), `compareBlocks` in both reports, `state().compare`; head button `[data-a=dupstudy]` (copy named "<name> (k)", becomes the active study; with the same set-up its results come back from the disk cache).
