Same release: head `[data-a=renstudy]` (view-only `renaming`, `input[data-k=sname]`), `setActive(id)` keeps the study used last in localStorage `mc:simStudy:<doc id>`; pure `exports.ts` (`loadBalance` → `dd[data-v=rtotal|balance]`, `hotCsv` → `[data-a=hotcsv]`, `stlBytes` → `[data-a=stl]`, `shapeScale(r)`); iso-clipping = view-only `iso` {on, below, value in solver units}, legend `[data-k=iso|isoside|isoval]`, `comps.ts` `isoIndex` on the overlay's index (the part meshes' ghost stays visible); `state().iso/balance/stl/hotCsv/renaming`. Smoke "sim 1.641" in shard 1 too, hands-on `tests/human/simextras.mjs`. The compare table is not a `.sim-passes` table (smoke checks count those rows). 1.642.0 (FR-SOL-020, FR-STU-022): `grid.ts` `memoryBytes(grid, size)` (8 bytes × Σ s² per element + 2 s_a s_b per pair of elements with cells side by side), `static.ts` `MEMORY_LIMIT` 1.5 GB, `staticMemory(grid, p, degrees)`, `StaticProblem.memory` (budget), `E-SIM-MEMORY: <MB>` before the matrix is made, result `memory`; `run.ts` `RunInput.memory`, `PassResult.memory`/`capped` (MB the next pass would need: `runStatic`/`runModal` end with `done` there); UI `?simmem=<MB>` (tests), `cappedNote` `[data-v=capped]`, Unknowns cell title, `state().passes[].memory/capped`. `Study.auto` (not in `keyOf`; Accuracy checkbox `[data-k=auto]`): `autoSoon()` from `changed()` and `rebuilt()` → `autoSolve()` after `AUTO_DELAY` 700 ms (waits while running, a panel is open or the host is not idle; nothing without a support and a load or a material; once per state `autoFor`), `state().auto/autoRuns`; the Loads section is `.sim-loads`. Tests `tests/unit/sim-memory.test.ts`, smoke "sim 1.642" (shard 1), hands-on `tests/human/simauto.mjs`. 1.643.0 (FR-CON-024/025): spot welds = `StudyWeld.on` "points" with `points` `StudyPoint` {faces: [the clicked face], at} and `dia` (`DEFAULT_SPOT` 6; bar `data-s=spot`, panel `input[data-q=dia]`, a click within a radius of a spot removes it); `run.ts` `spotPoints(body, points)` (6 numbers per spot: the point and the direction into the body at that face) → `Weld.spots` + `d`: `static.ts` marches through the body the point is on to where it ends, takes the body behind it (on it or within the gap) and ties the two over a disc there (3 radial Gauss × 12 angles, stiffness per area = the joining penalty, `tie` shared with line welds), `WeldResult.spots` (count; `length` = area, `peak`/`mean` = force of one spot, N); UI `weldRows()[].spot/spots/dia`, `table.sim-spottable` (`[data-v=scount|speak|sstress]`, stress = peak over πd²/4), E-SIM-WELD text for spots. Adhesive: `StudyLink.type` "adhesive" with `t` mm and `E` MPa (`DEFAULT_GLUE`, `glueStiffness` → kn = E/t, kt = G/t with `GLUE_NU` 0.4) → solver link `kn`/`kt`: the joining points get a spring w N N (kt I + (kn − kt) n nᵀ) instead of the interior-penalty terms, `Contact.type` "adhesive", its force = the spring's; Connections table option Adhesive, row `tr.sim-glue[data-glue]` with `input[data-k=gluet|gluee]`, `[data-v=gluenote]`, E-SIM-ADHESIVE. Tests `tests/unit/sim-spots.test.ts`, smoke "sim 1.643" (shard 3), hands-on `tests/human/simspot.mjs`. 1.644.0 (FR-SMAT-003, FR-CON-008): `Study.mats` (parts: `MatRef` by Bodies folder key, "" = the part's; in `keyOf` when present); UI `matOf(doc)` / `bodyMatOf(doc, key)` / `bodyKeys(doc)` are the only readers of a part's materials in a study (never `doc.material` there), `pickStudyMat(key)`, `SimHost.bodies()` (model `simAccess().bodies`), Material section `.sim-mat` with `.sim-smat [data-a=smat][data-b=""]` and for 2+ bodies `ul.sim-smats li[data-body] [data-a=smat]` (`.own` = picked for the study; the picker's No material goes back). `SimHost.mates()` (asm `simAccess().mates`: unsuppressed hinge and concentric mates by top component) → `linksHtml` adds `p.sim-matehint[data-v=matehint]` with `[data-a=matelink][data-la][data-lb]` for each listed pair without a typed link, which makes it sliding; bolts already come from the fasteners themselves. Smoke "sim 1.644" (shards 2 and 5), hands-on `tests/human/simmat.mjs`. 1.648.0 (NFR-SIM-022): validation against CalculiX in `docs/structural-validation.md`, run by `bench/simval.bench.ts` + `bench/simval.py` (offline: `ccx`, Gmsh; never shipped; faces listed by `bench/simfaces.bench.ts`). Fixes it brought: `Modeller.bonded` retries the fuse with the fuzzy value `BOND_GAP` 1e-3 mm when bodies stay apart; `run.ts` `faceOf(body)` maps every face of one name to the first (a bore cut in two by a slot is one pick: `trisOf`, `edgeLines`, `cornerPoint`, `spotPoints` use it); `static.ts` measured penalty: before the interior faces, for every cut non-rigid element T = ∫(σn)ᵀ(σn) over its interior faces, held triangles and domain surfaces, `topEigen(T, K_e, n)` = largest λ of T x = λ K x (shifted Cholesky + power iteration), `gam[e]` raised to `PENALTY_SAFE` 1.15 × 2λ, at most `PENALTY_CAP` 40 × the formula (aggregated cells needed up to 85 × at degree 5; the formula let the energy overshoot and the matrix go indefinite). It lives in `measurePenalty`, outside `solveStatic`: with the block inline Chrome stopped optimising `solveStatic` (assembly 5 × slower in the browser, not in node), so keep new work in that function in helpers of their own and time a solve in the packaged app. `grid.ts` `aggregate`: a domain's roots are its cells at least min(0.25, half its fullest cell's fraction) full, so a body thinner than a cell (a plate in an assembly) still joins its rim slivers to neighbours (`tests/unit/sim-thin.test.ts`); `bench/simasm.bench.ts <file.mcadasm> [left-out name]` solves an assembly file in node. Test set: `tests/human/set-engine.mjs` (three engine parts against CalculiX's displacement), `set-shaft.mjs` (engine stage assembly: a component left out, one rigid, bearing load, connection forces), `lib.mjs` `openFiles`/`saveModel`/`eyeDir`/`boreSpot`; `node tests/human/run.mjs sim-all` = every structural scenario (models saved in `HT_MODELS`). 1.652.0 (solver speed): `sparse.ts` `envelopeCost(A)` (order, values, Σ row width²) and `static.ts` `directFits` keep the direct solver above `DIRECT_LIMIT`/`MODAL_DIRECT` while the factorisation is cheap (`DIRECT_FLOPS` 4e10, `MODAL_FLOPS` 2e11, `DIRECT_VALUES` 5e7: slender parts; the p-multigrid CG took 160 s on a 12 000-unknown shaft's modal pass); `Envelope` factors and solves on per-row `subarray` views; `subspace({ exact })` takes K y = the right-hand side after a direct solve; `volumeCell`/`massCell`/`facePair` + `mirrorBlocks`/`addSym` are top-level (inside `solveStatic` Chrome ran the stiffness loop unoptimised: 37 s vs 3 s) and sum the blocks b ≥ a only; `quad.ts` `fitPoints` fits only the moments of total degree ≤ q − 1 (all a degree-p basis integrates with q = 2p + 1) and evaluates them by direction. `bench/simmodal.bench.ts <file.mcadprt> [held face]` times a modal study in node (`PROF=1` for a CPU profile); always time in the packaged app too.
- `docs/FRS-thermal.md`: functional requirements for thermal analysis (phases TH1 board thermal maps = STEP + the design app's thermal package (contract in its section 15.2, formats `tubecad-thermal`/`mobiuscad-thermal` v1), TH0 solver spike, TH2 part steady state, TH3 assemblies and boards, TH4 transient, TH5 thermal stress; IDs `FR-TPKG/TMAP/TSTU/TMAT/TLOAD/TBC/TCON/TBRD/TSOL/TRES/TREP-…`, tests `AT-TH…`). TH2 thermal studies of parts (1.651.0): `src/sim/thermal.ts` `solveThermal` (scalar heat conduction on the structural cells and basis: units mm, W, K, conductivity W/(mm·K); SIPG with the measured penalty, `ThermalSink` = fixed temperature (Nitsche) | film `h` W/(mm²·K) + `ambient` | `emissivity` (Newton steps on 4εσT³ until settled, `iterations`), `ThermalSource` = `power` W over `tris` or the whole body, or `flux` W/mm²; result `flows` `SinkFlow` {out, area, mean} whose sum equals `applied`, `sample` temp/flux/err; E-TH-NOK, E-TH-NOSINK; one domain only). Study side: `StudyKind` "thermal", thermal items live in the study's `supports` (types `temp`/`conv`/`rad`: `temp` °C, `h` W/(m²·K), `emis`, `ambient`) and `loads` (`heat` W with `body` = whole body, `hflux` W/m²), `types.ts` `THERMAL_SUPPORTS`/`THERMAL_LOADS`/`isThermalType`, `settings.ambient` (`DEFAULT_AMBIENT` 25), `FILM_PRESETS`, `THERMAL_PASSES` 3; `studyProblem` skips thermal items, `run.ts` `thermalSetup(st, body)` (problems E-SIM-REF/EMPTY, E-TH-VALUE) and generator `runThermal` (passes p = 2, 3, 4; `PassResult.vm` = temperature °C, `u` = heat flux W/mm², `thermal` {min, max, applied, flows, solves}, `change` = largest temperature change as % of the range; E-TH-NOHEAT; copy any array kept between passes, the worker transfers a pass's own), worker request type "thermal" when `RunInput.thermal` {k, setup}. UI: the Simulation bar starts with `.sim-mode` radio buttons `[data-s=mode-structural|mode-thermal]` (`mode`, `modeFor` = the study it was last set from; `setMode` opens the last study of that kind, `useStudy(thermal)` from `command()` opens or makes one for a command of the other analysis; Thermal is disabled over an assembly); Thermal pane `[data-s=newthermal|mat|ambient|heat|hflux|temp|conv|rad|solve|results|report]`; a modal study hides the Loads group; Study tab sections `.sim-heat`, `.sim-cool` with `input[data-k=ambient]`, `[data-v=kcond]`, results `thermalHtml` (`dd[data-v=tmax|tmin|heatin|flow|heatout|balance]`), panel `input[data-q=temp|hc|emis|amb]`, `select[data-k=film]`, `input[data-k=body]`; plot kinds `temp`/`flux`/`err` (`THERMAL_PLOTS`), never deformed; `thermalReport`; `state().barMode/thermal`. Tests `tests/unit/thermal.test.ts`, `sim-thermal-run.test.ts`, smoke "thermal 1.651" (shard 6), hands-on `tests/human/thermal.mjs`.
- Thermal studies of assemblies (1.654.0, FRS-thermal TH3 first slice): `thermal.ts` `ThermalProblem.domains` `ThermalDomain` {inside, k, tris} (the structural grid's domains: `Ctx.ke`/`dom` per element), `gap`, `links` `ThermalLink` {a, b, type "none" | "resistance", h W/(mm²·K)}; `joinTerms` joins the domains like static.ts does (a surface point of a stepped 2e-3 h or the gap along its normal inside b): perfect = the interior-penalty `pair` terms with each side's own conductivity, resistance = h w [N][N]; `contactFlows` → result `contacts` `ThermalContact` {a, b, area, type, heat W from a to b, ta, tb mean side temperatures}; `looseDomains` (union of contacts, sinks by element domain) → `E-TH-LOOSE: i,j`; `ThermalSource.doms` (volume heat in those domains only), result `volumes`, `sample().dom`. `run.ts`: `RunInput.thermal` {k, setup, ks per region, gap, links} with `regions`, `thermalSetup(st, body, regionKeys?)` (a `heat` load with `body` in an assembly = the components of its picked faces), `PassResult.dom`, `thermal.contacts`/`comps` {min, max, mean, power}. `types.ts` `StudyLink.type` "resist" (`h` W/(m²·K), `DEFAULT_TCC` 3000) / "pad" (`t` mm, `k` W/(m·K), `DEFAULT_PAD`) / "none", `linkConductance`; a structural solve ignores resist/pad links. UI: Thermal mode is on over assemblies (no Material button there), `solveThermal` builds regions from `simGeo().regions` with `st.comps[id].material ?? region mat` (E-TH-COMPMAT without k), fasteners stay solids (`findLinks` takes no bolts out, `foundKey` marks thermal), Study tab `compsHtml` thermal variant (`[data-v=compk]`, no Rigid), `thermalLinksHtml` (`.sim-tlinks`, `select[data-k=tlink]` perfect|pad|resist|none, `input[data-k=padt|padk|tcch]`, `[data-v=carea|cheat|cdrop]`), `thermalCompsHtml` (`table.sim-tcomps`, rows `tr[data-a=rcomp]`, `[data-v=tcmax|tcpower]`), heat panel `data-k=body` keeps the picked faces in an assembly, `Results.bodyMats[].k`, `state().thermal.contacts/comps`. Host `comps()[].k`. Tests `tests/unit/thermal-asm.test.ts`, `sim-thermal-run.test.ts` (assembly), smoke "thermal 1.654" (shard 1), hands-on `tests/human/thermalasm.mjs`. Next: a study straight from a board STEP with its thermal package (Heat from package, board material, heatsink).
- Board studies from the thermal package (1.655.0, FRS-thermal J5, FR-TSTU-004, FR-TBRD-001/002/010/011): `Kernel.bodyBreps()` (one BREP text per mesh of the last `load`, viewer order) → worker request `importBodies` → `ModelKernel.importBodies` → `AsmController.fromModel(buffer, fileName, mats, names)` = a new assembly with one fixed component per body (part with one `import` feature, material and name per body), returns the component ids in body order. Pure `src/thermal/study.ts`: `boardConductivity(t, stack)` (along = layers in parallel, through = in series; the solver is isotropic, so the board's material `BOARD_MATERIAL` "PCB (effective)" takes the in-plane value for now), `findBoard`, `guessMaterial` (`GUESS` library names), `boardPlan(manifest, match, bodies)` → board index, `mats`, `names`, `heat`. Thermal bar `#th-tools [data-th=simulate]` → `ThermalHost.simulate` → main.ts `simulateBoard` (needs `modelSrc`) → `asm.fromModel` + `SimController.boardStudy({ ambient, emissivity, heat })`. Study side: load type `pkgheat` with `parts` `PackageHeat` {id, name, comps, p, off, limit} (`PACKAGE_MIN_POWER` 10 mW; not a bar button; panel `table.sim-pkparts tr[data-pk]`, `input[data-k=pkon][data-i]`, `input[data-q=pkp][data-i]`), `StudySupport.all` on conv/rad (`thermalSetup` → the whole surface less the faces a fixed temperature or another item of its kind is on, `ThermalSink.free`: `thermal.ts` `freePoints` drops the points lying on another domain; panel `input[data-k=all]`, row text "all open surfaces"), `StudySupport.rth` K/W on conv (h = 1 / (Rth × area); panel `select[data-k=film]` value "rth", `input[data-q=rth]`). `unpicked(d)` says which items take no picks. Results: `compLimits()` → `[data-v=tover]`, `td[data-v=tclimit]`, `tr.bad` in `table.sim-tcomps`. Tests `tests/unit/thermal-study.test.ts`, `sim-thermal-run.test.ts` (board study), smoke "thermal 1.655" (shard 2 since 1.724.0: shard 6 reached the 12 minute limit), hands-on `tests/human/thermalboard.mjs`. 1.656.0 (FR-TBRD-010): conductivity by direction. `thermal.ts` `ThermalDomain.k3` [kx, ky, kz] W/(mm·K) along the world axes (`Ctx.k3` 3 per element; every k ∂N/∂n is Σ k_a g_a n_a, `ke` = the largest for the penalty; domains only). `thermal/study.ts` `plateAxis(tris)` (flat plate square to an axis: thinnest box extent, ≥ 60 % of the area looking along it) and `boardK(tris, stack, t0)` → {along, through, t, k3?}. `StudyComp.board` = `BoardStack` + `t` (in `keyOf` through `comps`); `RunInput.thermal.k3s`; UI thermal `compsHtml`: `[data-a=compboard]` toggle (`toggleBoard`, `compThickness` from the shown model), row `.sim-boardset input[data-k=blayers|bcopper|bcover][data-c]` (`setBoard`), `[data-v=compk]` shows along / through; `boardStudy({ board: { comp, t } })`; `Results.bodyMats[].kz`; `state().compList[].board`. A structural study ignores `board`. 1.657.0 (FR-TBRD-003): `PackageHeat.board`/`sink`/`path` (the package's split, from `PlanHeat`), `StudyLoad.board` (the board component) and `split` (1.712.0: always kept with a board, `types.ts` `keepsSplit(l)` = `!!l.board`; a saved `false` is ignored, `boardStudy` writes `true`; the panel has no switch, only `[data-v=splithelp]`); 1.713.0: `PackageHeat.into` `HeatInto[]` {disc [x, y, r] | rect [x0, y0, x1, y1] mm in the board plane, w} → `thermal/study.ts` `intoPatch` (disc = 24-gon of the disc's area, both via `clipFacing`), watts scaled to the board share, a region off the board falls back to `contactPatch`; `[data-v=intohelp]`, tests `tests/unit/thermal-into.test.ts`: `thermalSetup` then puts each part's board share on `thermal/study.ts` `contactPatch(boardTris, partTris)` (the board's face towards the part clipped to its footprint + 0.5 mm) as a `{ tris, power, dom }` source and drops a chassis share; `ThermalSource.dom` is bookkeeping for `comps[].power`; `runThermal` `comps[].out` = power − heat conducted away (contacts). UI `heatPaths()` (per package part: board = contact heat into the board domain + direct share, air = `out`, other = rest; shares can be negative) → `table.sim-paths` rows `tr[data-path]` (`[data-v=pboard|pair|pother]`, title = package split), `[data-v=pathnote]`, report table, `state().thermal.paths`. 1.658.0 (FR-TBC-004/010/011): natural convection. `thermal.ts` `ThermalSink.natural` {factor, down, lv/lh per triangle = the face's height along gravity and area/perimeter, mm} → `naturalH(up, dT, lv, lh)` (laminar air plates: upright 1.42, hot side up 1.32, down 0.59 × (ΔT/L)^¼; a cold face counts as turned over), `naturalPoints` locates face by face, `naturalTerms` Newton-linearised (1.25 h) in the radiation loop, `SinkFlow.h` = mean film coefficient. `run.ts` `faceSizes(b, idx, down)`; `StudySupport.natural`, `StudySettings.enclosure` (`ENCLOSURES` open/vented/closed = factor 1/0.8/0.55, ambient 25/35/40; custom `factor`, `enclosureFactor`) and `down` (`DOWNS`). UI: film option `nat`, tree `select[data-k=enclosure|down]`, `input[data-k=efactor]`, results `dd[data-v=hnat]`. `thermal/study.ts` `boardDown(orientation, bodies, board)`, `BoardPlan.enclosure`/`down` → `boardStudy` (natural convection). 1.659.0 (FR-TMAP-030/031): map compare. Pure `src/thermal/compare.ts` (`mapOnto(a, b, shift)` = B's temperature at each point of A: copied when the meshes are the same, else the nearest point of B on the same part by a hash grid; `partChanges` pairs parts by designator, board first, sorted by |ΔT|, `onlyA`/`onlyB`; `deltaRange` symmetric, ≥ ±1 K). `ThermalMaps` `Attached.cmp` {pkg, stats, tb, delta, changes, view a|b|d}: `attach` with a map present asks `chooseModal` compare|replace, `compare`/`uncompare`/`setView`, key B flips a↔b; A and B share one range, ΔT uses the diverging palette; legend `select[data-k=show]`, `[data-a=uncompare]`; side panel `table.th-ctable tr[data-cpart]` (click zooms), `[data-v=onlya|onlyb]`; `state().compare`. Fixture `tests/fixtures/thermal/make-b.ts` → `amp-vented-thermal.zip`; tests `tests/unit/thermal-compare.test.ts`, smoke "thermal compare 1.659" (shard 3), hands-on `tests/human/thermalcompare.mjs`. 1.660.0 (FR-TMAP-020/042): `Attached.iso` (part shown alone: `isolate(id | null)` from a second click on a parts/compare row (`detail === 2`, since the first click redraws the table), a canvas `dblclick`, bar `[data-th=isolate]`, legend `[data-v=iso]` + `[data-a=unisolate]`, Esc; `show()` puts the other parts' triangles in `fade` (`userData.thermalFade`, shared attributes, `ISO_FADE`), so hover/probes pick only the isolated part, labels show only it); `pictureText()` (title, scale ends, lines: design, isolated part, hottest + parts over limit, accuracy, ambient) and `decorate(canvas)` (labels + colour scale box bottom left) called by main.ts `exportPng` while `thermal.active()`; bar `[data-th=png]` → `ThermalHost.savePicture`; `state().iso/faded/picture`. - Transient thermal (1.661.0, FRS-thermal TH4): `StudySettings.transient` {end, out, t0?} on a thermal study (kind stays "thermal"; bar `data-s=transient` toggles {3600, 60}); `StudyLoad.profile` `PowerProfile` step {at} / duty {on, period, delay} / table {pts [s, share]} (heat panel `select[data-k=profile]`, `data-q=pat|pon|pper|pdel`, `textarea[data-k=ptable]`). Pure `src/sim/transient.ts` (`profileFactor`/`profileBreaks`, `outputTimes` ≤ `MAX_FRAMES` 201, `parseProfile`, `timeToReach`, `transientCsv`, `BOARD_RC`, `timeChartSvg`). `thermal.ts` `solveThermal` is split into `build`/`sourceLoad`/`linearised`/`samplerOf`; `transientThermal(pb, run)` = consistent capacity ρ·cp (J/(mm³·K) = g/cm³ × 1e-6 × J/(kg·K)), backward Euler then variable-step BDF2, step halved when an element mean moves more than max(2 K, …), doubled after 2 quiet steps, clipped to profile breaks, load read at the step middle, factorisations cached by step and linearisation, radiation/natural convection re-linearised as temperatures move; outputs interpolated. `run.ts` `runTransient` (steady passes first when heat is made, then the transient on the last degree; E-TH-NOCP: name, E-TH-TIME) → `PassResult.transient` {times, frames, min, max, put, out, steps, rejected, capacity, t0, steady}. UI: `res.tr` holds the run, the shown frame replaces the last pass (`useFrame`), range fixed over the run, `paintFrame` recolours on the slider/Play (legend `.sim-time` `[data-a=tplay]`, `input[data-k=time]`, `[data-v=ttime]`); Study tab Time section `.sim-tsec` (`data-k=transient|tend|tout|tstart`, `[data-v=rcp]`), results `[data-v=tstart|tend|tsteady|tsteps|tcap|stored]`, `svg.sim-tchart`, `input[data-k=reach]` → `li[data-v=treach]`, `[data-a=tcsv]`; `state().transient`. Tests `tests/unit/thermal-transient.test.ts`, smoke "thermal transient 1.661" (shard 3 since 1.761.0; shard 6 reached the 12 minute limit), hands-on `tests/human/thermaltransient.mjs`. 1.662.0: `recordGif` on a transient result runs `recordWarmGif` (legend `[data-a=gif]` in `.sim-time`: one frame per output time via `useFrame` + `paintFrame` (else `showPlot`), time + hottest temperature + colour scale drawn on each frame, delays at the playback rate, the shown time put back; `state().gif`). 1.699.0 (FR-TRES-006): `transient.ts` `componentSeries(cstats, names)` (each domain's max per output time, hottest at the end first) → UI `compSeries()`; the chart, `sim-legline`, time to reach and the CSV (`<name> max`) take the components in `compsShown` (view-only `tcompSel` {key = `res.key`, ds}, default the three hottest; `details.sim-tcomps-pick` with `ul.sim-tcomplist input[data-k=tcomp][data-d]`); legend `.sim-time select[data-k=speed]` restarts a playing warm-up; `state().transient.comps/compsShown/speed`. Tests `tests/unit/thermal-compseries.test.ts`, smoke "transient per component 1.699" (shard 2), hands-on `tests/human/thermaltcomps.mjs`.

- Thermal stress (1.663.0, FRS-thermal TH5, FR-LOAD-011, FR-TREP-012): load type `temperature` in static studies of parts (`StudyLoad.value` °C everywhere, or `from` = a thermal study id; `ref` stress-free °C, `DEFAULT_REF` 20; one per study, E-SIM-ONETEMP). `run.ts` `studyProblem` → `StudyProblem.thermo` {ref, uniform | from, tris = the surface less the faces of fixed/sliding/hinge/dirs/disp face supports}; `RunInput.temp` {alpha 1/K, thermal = the source study's own `RunInput`}; `tempField(inp)` (uniform, or runs `runThermal` and samples its last pass: T and ∇T = −flux / k; misses retried a little inwards, else the mean). `static.ts` `StaticProblem.thermo` {alpha, ref, field: `TempField`, tris}: `thermoLoads` (outside `solveStatic`) adds the consistent loads of σ = D(ε − α ΔT I): traction β ΔT n on `tris` and body force −β ∇T, β = (3λ + 2μ) α; `sample`/`stress` subtract β ΔT from the normal stresses; one material only (E-TH-STRESSMATS). UI: Loads ▸ Temperature (`data-s=temperature`), `unpicked`, panel `select[data-k=tfrom]` ("" = uniform, `nf("value")`) + `input[data-q=tref]`, `tempInput(doc, st, geo, regions)` (α from `matOf(doc).alpha` × 1e-6, E-TH-ALPHA; source study's material k, else E-TH-NOFIELD), `keyOf` adds the source study's set-up (stale when it changes), `tempText` (study name in `translate=no`). Tests `tests/unit/sim-thermostress.test.ts`, smoke "thermal stress 1.663" (shard 5 since 1.724.0), hands-on `tests/human/thermalstress.mjs`. 1.664.0: assemblies and parts with body materials. `RunInput.temp.alphas` (per region, 1/K, 0 = rigid) → `StaticProblem.thermo.alphas`; `static.ts` `thermoBeta` = β per element by its domain (none for a rigid one); `thermoLoads(..., multi)` puts each surface point's traction on the body just inside it (2e-3 h, `grid.cellOf(.., d)`) and adds at every non-adhesive joining point the thermal part of the shared traction, −{β} ΔT n·[v] (weights ½/½, or 1 on the elastic side of a rigid pair), which cancels for one material and makes unlike bodies push; it returns that {β} ΔT per join, which the connection forces subtract (σ = D ε − β ΔT I). UI `tempInput(doc, st, geo, alphas, got)` (E-TH-COMPALPHA: name), `thermalParts(st, got)` = the thermal study's bodies/conductivities/links/setup shared by `solveThermal` and the temperature load's source study of an assembly; `keyOf` adds the source study's links and comps. Tests `tests/unit/sim-thermostress-asm.test.ts` (series bar, bimetal strip against Timoshenko, field from an assembly thermal study), smoke "thermal stress 1.664" (shard 5), hands-on `tests/human/thermalstressasm.mjs`. 1.665.0 (FR-TMAT-004): conductivity by temperature. `Study.kt` rows [°C, W/(m·K)] by "" (the part) or component id (in `keyOf`, and in the temperature source study's key); pure `ktable.ts` (`parseKTable`, `kTableText`, `ktFactors` = factors on the body's k, `kAt`); `thermal.ts` `ThermalProblem.kt` / `ThermalDomain.kt` [°C, factor, …], `ktFactor` (linear, ends held), `solveThermal` = `build(pb, scale)` + `solveOnce` repeated with each element's factor at its constant coefficient (mean temperature) until no k changes by more than `KT_TOL` 0.5 % (`KT_STEPS` 30; result `kSteps`), `transientThermal` rebuilds with the element temperatures whenever they moved more than the step allowance (the radiation re-linearisation); `run.ts` `RunInput.thermal.kt`/`kts`, `PassResult.thermal.kSteps`; `tempField` now divides the flux by each point's own body k (`ks[dom]`, `k3s`, table) for ∇T. UI: `ktHtml(st, key)` (part: `.sim-ktbtn [data-a=ktab][data-b=""]` in the Material section; components: `[data-a=ktab][data-b=<id>]` "k(T)"; editor `.sim-kt textarea[data-k=ktab]`, `[data-a=ktclear]`), view-only `ktOpen`, `setKt`, `ktRange` in `[data-v=kcond]`/`[data-v=compk]`, results `[data-v=ksteps]`; boards ignore a table. Tests `tests/unit/thermal-kt.test.ts` (Kirchhoff slab, two bodies, transient), smoke "conductivity by temperature 1.665" (shard 6), hands-on `tests/human/thermalkt.mjs`. Radiation between faces (1.666.0, FR-TBC-007): pure `src/sim/viewfactor.ts` `viewFactor(a, b, all, rays = VIEW_RAYS 16384)` (area-weighted points, cosine-weighted directions, median-split BVH, fixed seed, only front-face hits; the scene is the whole surface so other faces block; both ways averaged by reciprocity) and `exchangeResistance` (grey two-surface R). `thermal.ts` `ThermalProblem.exchanges` `ThermalExchange` {a, b triangles, ea, eb, f} → `exchangeOf`; the Newton loop linearises Q = σ(Ta⁴ − Tb⁴)/R about the sets' mean temperatures: a film Ga/Aa, Gb/Ab on each set plus the rank-two coupling to the other set's mean, solved by Woodbury (`withLowRank` over the factored `Solver`, `factorSystem`), also in `transientThermal`; result `exchanges` `ExchangeFlow` {heat a → b, ta, tb, areas, f, da, db}; exchanges join domains in `looseDomains`. Study side: support type `radx` (`faces` + `faces2`, `emis`/`emis2`, default 0.9; `run.ts` `thermalSetup` → `ThermalSetup.exchanges`/`exchangeIds`, `exchangesOf` caches the view factor per set-up), `PassResult.thermal.exchanges`, `comps[].out` adjusted. UI: bar `data-s=radx` (Cooling), panel `.sim-faces[data-side=1|2]` via `twoSided` (shared with connectors), `data-q=emis|emis2`, results `table.sim-xtable tr[data-x]` (`[data-v=xf|xheat|xt|xnote|xnone]`), report table, `state().thermal.exchanges`. Tests `tests/unit/viewfactor.test.ts`, `thermal-radx.test.ts`, smoke "radiation between faces 1.666" (shard 3 since 1.761.0), hands-on `tests/human/thermalradx.mjs`. Forced air (1.667.0, FR-TBC-005): `thermal.ts` `forcedH(speed m/s, L mm)` (air `AIR` k/ν/Pr: laminar flat plate Nu = 0.664 Re^½ Pr^⅓, above `RE_TURBULENT` 5e5 (0.037 Re^0.8 − 871) Pr^⅓), `ThermalSink.forced` {h per triangle W/(mm²·K)} located by `groupedPoints` (shared with `naturalPoints`; per-point values in `lens`), `filmTerms` takes a per-point h array, `flowOf` reports the mean `h`. `run.ts` `flowFilm(b, idx, dir, speed)` = per face L = extent along the flow (a face facing it, extent < 0.2·√area, takes √area). Study side: `StudySupport.forced` `ForcedAir` {speed, dir, fan? {q m³/h, a cm²} → `fanSpeed`} on a conv item, `DEFAULT_FORCED`, `FLOWS`. - Thermal validation (1.694.0, NFR-TH-020/021): `tests/unit/thermal-analytic.test.ts` (pin fin cosh, annular fin Bessel with A&S I0/I1/K0/K1, heated cylinder; passes p = 2..4 like a study) and `bench/thval.bench.ts` + `bench/thval.py` (offline CalculiX: cases from boxes, Gmsh fragment = shared faces, C3D10, `*FILM`/`*DFLUX BF`/`*BOUNDARY 11`; the bench solves the same bodies and faces with `runThermal` and compares at the corner nodes; `THVAL_REUSE=1` reuses CalculiX's answer, `THVAL_CELLS`/`THVAL_PASSES`/`THVAL_SIZE`; `pip install gmsh` needs libglu1-mesa, `apt-get install calculix-ccx`). Results in `docs/thermal-validation.md`: heatsink 0.14 %, boards 3.6 % / 13 % cool by default (resolution at part footprints). Fix: `grid.ts` `cellOf` asks the point's own body (`doms[only](p)`) even where one body's cell is there, and `domainOf` steps along the 8 diagonals too (edges and corners). Next: FR-TSOL-003 local degrees for thermal, cells by the thinnest body.
- Thermal pass warm start (1.716.0): `thermal.ts` `solveOnce` starts the radiation / natural convection loop from `pb.prev` (`startFrom`: the pass before's coefficients in this pass's layout), so later passes take 2 to 3 solves; `PassResult.thermal.solves` of a later pass can be 1. Profile of a board study (`bench/thboard.bench.ts <file.mcadasm>`, `THBOARD_PASSES`): `linearised` (film terms made again each step) about a third, linear solves a fifth, `makeGrid` and `build` (point location) a sixth each; all on one thread.
- Thermal local passes and joints (1.695.0, FR-TSOL-003): `ThermalProblem.degrees` per element (`Ctx.nbs`/`off`/`deg`, `BlockMatrix(nel, nbs)`, `Basis.eval(..., n)` takes the leading n functions, `facePts(c, ca, axis, d)` at the higher side's degree; x padded to the uniform layout; no matrix reuse with degrees, transient strips them); `run.ts` `runThermal` runs local passes after `GLOBAL_PASSES` like `runStatic` (`markElements`, `PassResult.raised`, `THERMAL_PASSES` 6, study default 3; UI `localNotes` in the passes table and report). Joints between domains weigh each side by the other's normal conductivity (`thermal.ts` `joinWeights`, penalty wa·γa + wb·γb; plain averages left boards 4 to 13 % cool against CalculiX). `run.ts` `thermalCellSize(body, regions)` sizes an assembly's cells by its thinnest region (3t, up to `THERMAL_MAX_CELLS` 400) unless `cells` is given; transient keeps 150. `thermal.ts` `bodiesOf` caches each surface triangle's own body for `located`; `grid.ts` `fills` = a full cell answers `cellOf` without asking the body. Tests `tests/unit/thermal-local.test.ts`, smoke "thermal 1.695" (shard 6), hands-on `tests/human/thermallocal.mjs`.

- Analysis on all cores and solve progress (1.717.0, FR-SARC-005, FR-SOL-023): `src/sim/blockchol.ts` `BlockCholesky(A, { order, plan })` (`solve`, `values`) replaces `Envelope` as the direct solver of `static.ts` and `thermal.ts` (`pcg.ts` keeps `Envelope` for its coarse solve): `minDegreeOrder` over the element graph, `cholPlan(A)` (symbolic: `rows`, `blockAt`, `diagAt`, `flops`, `size`; `directFits` in static.ts compares those with `DIRECT_FLOPS`/`DIRECT_VALUES`), right-looking steps of `factorDiag` → `solveBlock` → `updateBlock` (row ranges, 2 × 2 row blocking); E-SIM-SINGULAR, E-SIM-MEMORY past 2²⁸ values. `threads.ts`: `factor(P, data)` shares a step of ≥ `PAR_STEP` multiplications between this thread and helpers over `SharedArrayBuffer` (`CholJob`; tasks claimed with one `Atomics.add` on a word holding generation + index, so a late helper finds nothing; `serve(job)` = a helper's loop; `setHelpers`, `factorBuffer`, `threadStats()` {jobs, parallelSteps, joined, helpers}); serial and threaded results are equal bit for bit. `sim.worker.ts` request `threads` {n} makes `n − 1` `chol.worker.ts` helpers when `crossOriginIsolated` and waits (≤ 3 s, `ready`) for them to report in before a solve: a worker made by a worker does not start while its parent is busy, though messages to a running one arrive. `client.ts` `threadsFor(cores)` (cores − 1 from 4 up; `?simthreads=<n>`), `SimClient.threads` from the done reply. Isolation: `scripts/sw-plugin.mjs` `isolated(r)` adds COOP same-origin / COEP require-corp / CORP same-origin to what the service worker serves, so the page is isolated from the second load on (the first visit solves on one thread); never load a cross-origin resource without CORS in the app. No GPU in the solve (WebGPU has no 64-bit floats). `progress.ts`: `progress(stage, done, total)` (throttled 80 ms) from grid/static/thermal/eigen/run, `progressPass`, `progressTo(sink)` set by the worker per solve → reply `{ id, progress }` → `SimClient.solve(..., onProgress)` → sim ui `onProgress`/`renderStatus` fill `#st-sim` in the status bar (`.st-sim-t`, `.st-sim-bar[role=progressbar]`, `.st-sim-p`, `.st-sim-s`, `button[data-a=simstop]` = `client.cancel()`; hidden and emptied when not `running`), `overall(p)` = one 0..1 number (a pass weighs about its degree squared; a study that converges early ends below 100 %); `state().threads/isolated`. 1.756.0: the status line's stage text `.st-sim-t` is `flex: 1 1 0` (bar, per cent, time and Stop never move), `.st-sim-p`/`.st-sim-s` 5ch right-aligned, `.status` uses tabular digits and hides `.snaps` while a solve runs; `run.ts` `ownTemps` samples a component no plot point fell on at its own triangles (it read 0 °C before). Measured in Chrome on 4 cores, bracket with 6 passes: 154 s (1.715.0) → 86 s on one thread → 69 s on four; what is left is mostly "building the equations" (cell quadrature and assembly, one thread). `bench/simstatic.bench.ts <file.mcadprt>` (`SIMSTATIC_PASSES`, `SIMTHREADS`). Tests `tests/unit/sim-threads.test.ts`, smoke "sim 1.717" in the 1.608 block (shard 4) and the isolation check in the service worker block, hands-on `tests/human/simprogress.mjs`.
- Helper jobs through ports (1.731.0): `sim.worker.ts` gives every `chol.worker.ts` helper a `MessageChannel` port at start-up and posts jobs on it (`HelperLike` wraps the port). Chrome delivered a message posted straight to a worker's worker only once the busy parent was back in its event loop, for one of the helpers, so one thread fewer worked than `helpers` said (`threadStats().joined`/`loopJoined` < helpers). Check those two numbers in the packaged app (`tests/human/simtime.mjs`) after any change to how helpers start. Smoke "sim 1.731" (shard 6, `?sw&simthreads=2`).
- Building the equations on all cores (1.718.0): `threads.ts` `kernel(kind, { init, run })` registers a loop over independent tasks (by the module that owns it, so every thread has it: `chol.worker.ts` imports `static`/`thermal`), `parallelFor({ kind, n, …shared arrays }, tick)` posts the job to the helpers and claims tasks itself with one `Atomics.add` (`serve` takes `CholJob | ParJob`; a throw in a helper = E-SIM-THREADS), `worthSharing(work)` (helpers and ≥ `loopFloor` 2e7, `setLoopFloor` for tests), `sharedF64`/`sharedI32`; `threadStats().loops/loopJoined`. Loops: `fit` (grid.ts `refit`: the cut cells' fine points move once to `Grid.shared` {a, at}, each task = `fitPoints` of one cell into a shared output the cells' `pts` then view), `volume` (static.ts `volumeTerms`: points packed per element, the diagonal blocks filled in one shared buffer and taken by `BlockMatrix.adopt`), `faces` (static.ts `faceTerms`: batches of ≤ 512 faces within `FACE_SCRATCH` 96 MB; helpers sum Saa/Sab/Sbb per face into a shared scratch, this thread adds them in the faces' order). Every task runs the same code in the same order as the serial path, so results are equal bit for bit. Measured in node, bracket (leuenberger 1-01.4) 5 passes: 50 s on one thread, 24 s on four; left on one thread: `makeGrid` (7 s), the measured penalty, boundary terms, thermal's own volume and face loops. Tests in `tests/unit/sim-threads.test.ts`.
- Thermal studies on all cores (1.724.0): thermal.ts `systemSolver(c, A)` (one answer per `Ctx` in `solverPlans`: static.ts `directFits` with `DIRECT_FLOPS`, else `pcgSolver`; the `CholPlan` is kept, since the linearised and time-step matrices of one `Ctx` have the same blocks) serves `factorSystem` and the transient `solverFor`; `solveOnce`'s radiation / natural convection loop keeps the first step's factorisation (`kept0`, direct solver and no low-rank exchange only) and solves later steps by `polished` (conjugate gradients preconditioned by it, `POLISH_STEPS` 40, `POLISH_TOL` 1e-11, null = factor again). Loops on `parallelFor`: `tvolume` (`conductCell` per element into a shared buffer, `BlockMatrix.adopt`) and `tfaces` (`faceBlocks` sums a face's four blocks from zero into a slot, `addFace` adds them in the faces' order; the one-thread path goes through the same two functions, so the sums are equal bit for bit). `bench/thbox.bench.ts` = a board with parts from boxes, film + radiation on every open face, no OpenCASCADE (`THBOX_PASSES`, `SIMTHREADS`, `PROF=1`): 30 s → 28 s on one thread, 16 s on four. Left on one thread: `filmTerms` (made again each loop step), `measure`, point location, `makeGrid`. Tests in `tests/unit/sim-threads.test.ts`.
- Modal solves on all cores (1.727.0): blockchol.ts `cholSolve(SolvePlan, L, b, y, x)` (the solve as a top-level function), `BlockCholesky.solveAll(bs)` = one task per right-hand side, loop `cholsolve` (registered in threads.ts, `sharedSolvePlan`; needs the factor on shared memory and `worthSharing(4·q·values)`), same numbers as `solve`; eigen.ts `EigenOptions.solveAll` (every M x made first, then all solves together; static.ts passes it with the direct solver). Bracket modal 3 passes on four cores 10.6 → 8.0 s. `bench/simmodal.bench.ts` takes `SIMTHREADS` and `PROF=1`.
- Factor kernel (1.728.0): blockchol.ts `updateBlock` takes four rows against two columns per step of q (8 sums for 6 loads; on a diagonal block the columns below the four rows, then each row's rest one sum at a time; the upper triangle is no longer written). Every value is still its own sum in q order, so results did not change and row pieces made by several threads stay equal. Bracket 5 passes: 51.6 → 46.4 s on one thread, 16.7 → 16.0 s on four.
- Kept factorisations (1.729.0): sparse.ts `matrixKey(A)` (layout + every value, two 32-bit hashes over the words); static.ts `factorOf(A, plan)` keeps direct factors by that key (`keptFactors`, newest last, ≤ `KEEP_VALUES` 6e7 values together; `reusedFactors`, `dropFactors` for tests), so a static solve whose matrix comes out the same bit for bit (next load case, a changed load in the same worker) skips the factorisation in its whole-body passes; local passes differ by case (their degrees follow the answer) and factor as before. Bracket 3 passes, second case: 6.2 → 4.2 s on four cores. `bench/simstatic.bench.ts` `SIMSTATIC_AGAIN=1`. Tests `tests/unit/sim-factorreuse.test.ts`.
- Flat boundary faces (1.732.0): static.ts `surfacePoints(grid, tris, tq, q)` (top level, kept per grid and triangle list in `surfaceKept`, so the penalty measuring, the support terms and the reactions of a pass locate a face once) returns 8 numbers per point (element, place, weight, normal); the points of one cell in one plane (normals and offsets within 1e-7) go through quad.ts `fitPlanePoints(src, idx, n, q, tiny)` = the q × q Gauss points of their bounding rectangle in the plane, weights from the Legendre moments of total degree < q (q = 2p + 1; null when that is no fewer points). `located` and `measurePenalty` use it; curved faces keep their points. The cell is decided before fitting (a fitted point may lie off the body). Bracket fifth pass 240,000 → 7,000 points, five passes in Chrome on four cores 32 → 24 s, energies equal to 12 digits. Not in thermal.ts yet (film terms there are not polynomial with radiation). Tests `tests/unit/sim-planefit.test.ts`.
- Held faces (1.730.0): static.ts support terms of a face with no spring and no enforced displacement go through `heldSum` (per point S = Σ w N_a N_b and G = Σ w N_a ∂N_b of the element) and `heldWrite` (the block from S, G, P and the material, once per run of points with one element, support and normal; `heldDone` in `solveStatic`); a moved support keeps `heldBlock` per point, springs and edge/point penalties `penaltyBlock`. A bracket held on a big face spent 7.5 s of a fifth pass there (boundary points × 9·ne² products each); in Chrome five passes 51 → 31 s on four cores. Answers changed in the tenth digit. `tests/human/simtime.mjs` times the bracket study step by step in the packaged app (the node bench holds a small face, so it never showed this). Tests `tests/unit/sim-heldsum.test.ts`.
- Static penalty measuring on all cores (1.726.0): static.ts `measurePenalty(grid, nel, nbs, gam, p, rigid, facePts, lameOf, A, bas, surf)` collects every cut element's samples (place, weight, normal; interior faces in cell order, then the located support and domain surfaces; `SMEAS` numbers per element, a copy of its stiffness block) and runs `measureCell` (T from `traction` + `topEigen`) per element, loop `smeasure`; the sums are in the old order, so results did not change. Bracket 5 passes on four cores 20.2 → 16.6 s. `bench/simstatic.bench.ts` takes `PROF=1`. Left on one thread in a static solve: boundary terms and point location, joins, the solve after the factorisation.
- Thermal surface terms on all cores (1.725.0): thermal.ts `filmTerms`/`naturalTerms` go through `surfaceTerms` → `filmPlan(c, L, per, stride)` (kept per point list `L` in `filmPlans`: the points ordered by element, `TFILM` numbers per element, shared memory when worth it) and `filmCell` (one element's matrix + load summed from zero into the plan's `out`), loop `tfilm`; the caller adds each element's sums to its block and load in the elements' order. `measure` collects every cut element's samples (place, weight, normal; `TMEAS` per element, a copy of its conduction block) and runs `measureCell` (T + `topEigen`) per element, loop `tmeasure`. thbox bench 14.0 → 12.5 s on four. Left on one thread: point location (`located`/`freePoints` ask the bodies), `heldTerms`, `joinTerms`.
- Cells on all cores (1.724.0): `insideOf(MeshBody)` carries `Inside.tris`; grid.ts `makeGrid` goes through `cellAt` (one cube: state, fine points, fitted points) and, when every domain is a mesh body and `worthSharing`, `sharedCells`: loop `cells` over all cubes, each thread makes the bodies once per grid from the shared triangles (`cellBodies` by `key`), points go into a round's shared memory by an `Atomics.add` bump (`CELL_ROOM` 1.5e6 points; a cube with no room left gets status 3 and goes into the next round; whole cells' Gauss points stay with the caller), records `CELL_REC`; the cells keep views of the rounds' memory and `refit` moves the fine points to `Grid.shared` as before. Same cells bit for bit. Bracket first pass 7.5 s → 2.7 s on four.
- Thermal flat faces and direct solves (1.733.0): thermal.ts `fitPlanar(c, L)` (end of `located`, and of `freePoints` after its filter) replaces the points of one element in one plane by `fitPlanePoints` at q = 2·deg + 1 (groups too small keep theirs); `build` shares a sink's points with an earlier sink on the same triangle array, and run.ts `gather` returns one array for the same triangle list, so convection and radiation on all open surfaces locate once. `systemSolver` takes the direct solver up to `THERMAL_FLOPS` 1.5e11 / `THERMAL_VALUES` 7.5e7 (`directFits(..., values)`). `tests/human/thtime.mjs` times the amp board study in the packaged app (`SAVE` = save the assembly for `bench/thboard.bench.ts`, which takes `SIMTHREADS` and `PROF=1`). Tests `tests/unit/thermal-planefit.test.ts`.
- Element matrices from moments (1.734.0): `src/sim/moments.ts` `momentTables(p)` (per thread: 1D expansions of L_i L_j and L_i' L_j' in Legendre polynomials, term lists per pair of functions a ≥ b, moments of r + s + t ≤ 2p at (r·m + s)·m + t), `momentsAdd`/`momentsPoint` (Σ w L_r L_s L_t over points in the element's coordinates, any weight per point), `conductFromMoments` (∫ k ∇N_a·∇N_b, moments to 2·deg − 2) and `massFromMoments` (Σ w N_a N_b, moments to 2·deg); always `momentsClear` after use (one scratch per thread). thermal.ts `conductElement` (loop `tvolume`, `TVOL` 11 with the degree) and `filmCell` (weights w·h per point, so radiation and natural convection too) use them; the sums over points they replace are equal to rounding, not bit for bit. Not yet in static.ts (volume, mass), the capacity matrix, or face terms between elements. `bench/thboard.bench.ts` prints seconds per stage of each pass. Tried and dropped: nested dissection by breadth-first levels (more fill than minimum degree on these graphs) and conjugate gradients with the degree p − 1 space as the coarse level (45 iterations, slower than factoring). Tests `tests/unit/sim-moments.test.ts`. The bolt smoke block (sim 1.637) moved to shard 6, then shard 5 in 1.742.0.
- Factor kernel in WebAssembly (1.735.0): `native/chol-kernel/kernel.c` (`update`, `solve`, `diag`: the loops of blockchol.ts `updateBlock`/`solveBlock`/`factorDiag` with 128-bit SIMD; every sum = two lanes over q added at the end, then the last q of an odd length, the same in every path, so pieces made by several threads equal a block made whole) → `npm run build:cholwasm` (`scripts/build-cholwasm.mjs`: clang wasm32 + wasm-ld, no libraries) → committed `src/sim/cholKernel.wasm.ts` (two modules as base64: shared and plain imported memory). `src/sim/cholwasm.ts`: `kernelBuffer(n, shared)` = a `WebAssembly.Memory` holding the factor one page in (`HEAD`), `kernelOf(data)` (by `data.buffer`; null for an ordinary array, then the JS loops run: only when the memory cannot be had or `setKernel(false)`), `memoryOf`/`adoptMemory` (a `CholJob` carries `mem`; a helper makes its own view and instance from it), `kernelMemories()`. threads.ts `factorBuffer` asks for kernel memory first. Rebuild and commit the .ts after any change to the C. A single `BlockCholesky.solve` goes block by block through the kernel too (`kernelSolve`: `fdiag`/`fblock`/`bblock`/`bdiag`, the right-hand side in `scratchOf(data, n)` = room kept after the factor); `cholSolve` in JS (several right-hand sides on threads, ordinary arrays) sums the same way. Measured in Chrome on four cores: amp board thermal 66.5 → 48.4 s (third pass factor 17.6 → 4.3 s), bracket static 24 → 16.6 s. Tests in `tests/unit/sim-threads.test.ts`.
- Thermal build (1.736.0): thermal.ts `faceBlocks` sums per point N c' + c N' with c = (γw/2) N ∓ (w/2) k ∂N/∂n of each side (aa and bb lower triangles mirrored, ba = ab turned over); `measureCell` sums the lower triangle of T; `energies` = xᵀ K x with each element's conduction matrix from its moments; a whole-body source's load = q × the moment of each function's degrees (`Basis.ex`); `fitPlanar` runs its groups as loop `tplanefit` (`planeFitGroup`, the same function on one thread). Amp board in Chrome on four cores 48.4 → 42.5 s, same temperatures. Left in a pass-3 build (about 9 s): point location (`located`, twice: sinks and domain surfaces), cut-cell fitting (`fitPoints`), film terms.
- Kept moments and plot cells (1.737.0): quad.ts `fitPoints(src, lo, hi, q, mom, have)` keeps a cut cell's moments (total degree < `MOM_CAP` 13, `MOM_SIZE` numbers) in `Grid.mom` {a, have, slot} (grid.ts `momentsOf`; `refit`'s loop `fit` passes slot and have in `FIT_TASK` 12), so a later pass sums only degrees have ≤ i + j + k < q; `Grid.cellsOf(pos, nrm, eps)` remembers the cell of each sampled point per `pos` array (WeakMap), used by thermal.ts `samplerOf` and static.ts `sample` (`at(px, py, pz, known)`). Bracket five passes 16.6 → 14.8 s, amp board 42.5 → 41.4 s in Chrome on four cores, same results.
- Cell cutting (1.738.0): body.ts `triBox` (separating axis test through `axisMeets`, plain numbers only), `MeshBody.inside` (counts the crossings beyond x itself, the same numbers as `hits(0, …)` without the list and sort) and `hint` (triangles seen once by a stamp array) make no arrays: they run per cube and per sample point on every thread, and their garbage was half of the first cell cutting (amp board 5.9 → 2.9 s, bracket 2.3 → 1.3 s in Chrome on four cores). Same decisions, same cells. Timings in the cloud container vary by ±3 s from run to run: compare stages over two or three runs.
- Kept surface points (1.739.0): thermal.ts `build` with several bodies sets `Ctx.loc` (`LocKept`, dropped at its end): `keptPoints` keeps each triangle's located points by its nine numbers and body (`triHash`/`locFind`; `cutPoints` is the plain path), `freePoints` remembers open/covered per kept point (`free`), `fitPlanar(c, L, ids)` takes a plane group fitted before from the same kept points (`fitKept`/`fitKeep`). Sinks face by face, radiation from all open surfaces and the domains' surfaces then cut, locate, test and fit once; same numbers (`setKeptPoints(false)` = the old way, tests). `Basis.values` (no gradients) in `filmCell`, `flowOf` of sinks that are not held, exchange means. Amp board in Chrome on four cores about 39 → 36.5 s. Tests `tests/unit/thermal-keptpoints.test.ts`.
- Moments by lines (1.740.0): quad.ts `fitMoments` (cut-cell fitting with kept moments) takes consecutive points that share two coordinates (the Gauss lines `volumePoints`/`exactLeaf` push) as one line: Σ w L along it once (`legendre`), times the two other directions' values; `fitPlanePoints` evaluates its output one direction at a time. Sums differ from 1.739.0 in the last digits (same on every thread). Amp board in Chrome on four cores about 36.5 → 34 s, bracket five passes 14.8 → 13.1 s. Tests `tests/unit/sim-linemoments.test.ts`.
- Kept values at surface points (1.741.0): thermal.ts `FilmPlan.vals` (shared memory when the plan is): per point `filmRecord(nb)` numbers = the functions' values, then the Legendre values of its moments (moments.ts `legendrePoint`; `momentsKept` sums from them), made by the first `filmCell` over an element (task slot 10 = place, negative until made), up to `FILM_VALUES` 24e6 numbers per build (`filmKept` by `Ctx.off`; `setFilmValues` for tests); `flowOf` reads them through `filmValues(c, L)` (`where` per point). Radiation's powers are multiplications (`T2 * T2`), `naturalH` makes no array. Same numbers with and without kept values. `bench/thboard.bench.ts` `THBOARD_TRANSIENT=<end>,<out>` and `tests/human/thtime.mjs` `WARMUP=1` time a warm-up: amp board hour in Chrome on four cores 26.8 → 24.4 s (node 26 → 18 s of time stepping), steady 33.8 → 32 s. A warm-up relinearises at nearly every step (step control and the relinearisation both use 2 K); the board has 220,000 surface points at degree 3 (about 640 per element, from the fine triangles of curved faces at (p + 1)² points each): fewer points there is what is left. Tried and dropped: time steps solved by conjugate gradients on an older factorisation (no gain, the factorisation is a small part). Tests `tests/unit/thermal-filmvalues.test.ts`.
- Results saved with the model (1.742.0, FR-SOL-030): `src/sim/resultsFile.ts` (`.mcadres` = ZIP: `results.json` manifest {format `mobiuscad-results`, version, `doc` = the model's document id, model name, studies [{id, name, kind, `key` = `keyHash(keyOf)`, at, file, thermal?}]}, `<study>.json` + `.bin` = `encodeValue` (typed arrays moved into the binary, NaN/±Infinity marked), `thermal/<model> <study>-thermal.*` = a steady thermal study as a thermal package (`packageFiles`, no picture); `packResults`/`unpackResults`/`resultsHead`, `resultsName`; `ResultsHub` set by the SimController, used through `resultsOf(doc)`/`takeResults(docId, bytes)`). Sim ui `files` (doc id → study id → `SavedStudy`, data = `Kept` with `at`): `keep` (after every solve: disk cache + `remember`), `restore` takes a file entry whose key fits, else the disk cache, else an older file entry as `Results.old` (key `old:…`, so `stale()`; no `noteResult`); `packFor` fills the live document's studies from the disk cache first; `keptNote` (`.sim-fromfile`, solved date). Where it goes: `packPart(..., results)` / `packAsm(..., results)` entry `results.mcadres` (part save, vault, single-file assembly save, `partBytes(results = true)`, asm `bytes(results = true)`); asm `saveToFolder` writes `<name>.mcadres` beside (inside only when the assembly references no part files), `resultsBeside` reads it in `attachFolder`/`chooseFolder`; Pack and Go puts `<stem>.mcadres` beside each part and assembly (`partBytes(false)`); main.ts `openFiles` opens `.mcadres` after the models in the same pick (`openResults`). Asm `stateKey` leaves out the doc name, the parts' `file`/`link` and their own studies, so results fit after a save into the folder. Tests `tests/unit/simResults.test.ts`, smoke "sim 1.742" (shard 3).
- Surface rules by piece (1.743.0): quad.ts `triangleRule(a, b, c, deg, out)` (symmetric rules `TRI_RULES` of 1, 3, 6, 7, 12, 16 points for degree 1, 2, 4, 5, 6, 8; `TRI_RULE_MAX` 8, above it `triangleGauss`), `gridTrianglePoints(..., degAt)`: with `degAt(t, x, y, z)` (source triangle, the piece's middle) each piece of a cut triangle gets the symmetric rule of the degree asked (never above 2n − 2); without it the n × n collapsed rule as before (static.ts and the joins between bodies keep that: their per-point inside tests are not polynomial, and fewer points moved a contact area by 1 %). thermal.ts `pieceDegree(c, nrms, bodyOf)` = twice the degree of the element the piece lies in, used by `cutPoints` and `keptPoints` (`setPieceRules(false)` = the old way, tests): with local degrees (pads) the third pass of a board study has p = 6 but most elements 4, so 16 points per piece instead of 49. Open/covered tests of "all open surfaces" (`freePoints`) see fewer points too; board temperatures did not move in the digits shown. Amp board in Chrome on four cores: steady about 32 → 30 s, warm-up 24.4 → 23.7 s. Not in static.ts yet (`surfaceKept` is keyed by the grid and tq only, and local passes change degrees on one grid). Tests `tests/unit/sim-trirule.test.ts`, `thermal-piecerules.test.ts`.
- Shared matrix blocks (1.744.0): thermal.ts `copyMatrix(A)` (every linearisation of radiation / natural convection, every time-step matrix of a warm-up) copies only the diagonal blocks; the blocks between elements are the same arrays as in `A` (surface and capacity terms only write diagonal blocks: never write an off-diagonal block of such a copy, and never change `Built.A` after `build`). Same numbers; the copy was the whole matrix (about 400 MB in the third pass of a board study). Amp board warm-up in Chrome on four cores 23.7 → 22.5 s. Profile of the main thread after it (steady, node): factor kernel 4.9 s, garbage collection 1.9 s, `faceBlocks` 1.9 s, `fitMoments` 1.6 s, `measureCell` 1.6 s, `energies` 1.0 s (one thread), `BlockMatrix.block`/`mul` 1.7 s; a warm-up factors at nearly every step (6.5 of 11.8 s of time stepping).
- Warm-up linearisations (1.745.0): thermal.ts `RELIN` 2: `transientThermal` linearises radiation / natural convection again once an element mean has moved `RELIN` × the step allowance (2 K) from the last linearisation (a k(T) table keeps 1 ×: it is read as it is, not by a tangent). Amp board hour: 106 → 66 linearisations, 119 → 78 factorisations for 258 steps, temperatures within 0.007 K (3 × gave 0.02 K), Chrome on four cores 22.5 → 17 to 20 s. What a warm-up still spends: `filmCell` at each linearisation (two passes over 220,000 surface points), `flowOf` per output frame, a factorisation per step size and linearisation. `bench/thboard.bench.ts` `TRDBG=1` prints four frames' component temperatures. Tests in `tests/unit/thermal-transient.test.ts` (radiating block against the lumped equation).
- Warm-up set-up and sink flows (1.746.0): thermal.ts `keepBuild(on)` / `takeBuild()` (run.ts `runTransient` around its steady passes) keep the last steady solve's `Built` (`noteBuild`: only one degree everywhere, no k table, no air link) and `TransientRun.built` hands it to `transientThermal`, which takes it when `sameBuild` (same p, h, box, body, sinks/sources/links arrays, domains, exchanges) instead of `build` (cells, matrix, surface points made twice before). `flowOf` of a sink that holds no temperature goes through `planFlow`: `flowCell` sums each element's film heat, radiation, area, ∫T, ∫h from zero into the plan's `out` (loop `tflow` when `worthSharing`, the same function on one thread), added in the elements' order; no kept values = the old point loop (sums differ in the last digits). Amp board hour: time stepping in node 10.5 → 7.5 s, Chrome on four cores 17 to 20 → 15.8 s. Tests `tests/unit/thermal-warmbuild.test.ts`.
- Thermal energies on all cores (1.747.0): thermal.ts `energies` runs `energyCell` (one element's xᵀ K x and error share from its moments) per element, loop `tenergy` when `worthSharing` (points packed like `tvolume`, `x` and `prev` copied to shared memory), summed in the elements' order: same numbers. Amp board pass 3 solve stage 2.4 → 1.9 s in node. Left on one thread in a steady study: `BlockMatrix.mul` in `polished` (blocks are not on shared memory), garbage collection.
- Boards with many small bodies (1.748.0): thermal.ts `holdThin(c, A)` (end of `build`): an element whose cut cell's `box` reaches less than `THIN_REACH` 0.25 of the element along an axis (grid.ts `Cell.box`, `elementReach`) has the functions with Σ degree × lg(1/reach) > `THIN_DIGITS` 3 held by a big diagonal term (a 0.6 mm lead in one cell was singular at degree 5/6; `Ctx.held`). `joinTerms` joins each pair of bodies from the surface of the one with less surface (`surfaceArea`; contacts may have a > b), so a wire end on a big face or a body wholly inside another is always found; perfect-contact terms are summed per run of one element pair (`pairLower` + `pair`, `flush`). quad.ts `smallTriangleDegrees(tris, h)` + `gridTrianglePoints(..., degAt, small)`: a triangle far smaller than a cell takes a symmetric rule of degree 2 to 6 (`cutPoints`, `keptPoints`, joins). grid.ts `cubeRange`/`domsNear` (a body's cubes only within its box), body.ts `inside`/`insideAlong` reject beyond the box along the ray. sim ui `openedWith`: a study a document was opened with is not auto-solved until something changes. `bench/thboard.bench.ts` `THBOARD_ONLY=<names>` keeps only those components, `TRDBG=1` prints contacts and boxes. Tests in `tests/unit/thermal-asm.test.ts` (thin wire, enclosed body). Left: `inside` tests on a board body with sliver faces (about a third of a pass), `asmSimGeometry` reading (30 s on that board), the same thin-body and join rules in static.ts.
- Thermal geometry reading (1.749.0): sim ui `THERMAL_ANG` 0.2 rad = the angle the thermal solve and a thermal Find connections pass to `simGeo` (structural keeps 0.05): above a radius of 0.4 mm the chord deviation decides anyway, and `BRepMesh` on a flat face with hundreds of finely cut holes is far worse than linear (board body 25 s → 2.5 s, 387,000 → 117,000 triangles for the whole board, which also shortens every inside test). `bench/asmgeo.bench.ts <file.mcadasm>` = time of `asmSimGeometry` per component (`ASMGEO_ONLY`, `ASMGEO_LIN`, `ASMGEO_ANG`); `bench/thboard.bench.ts` `THBOARD_ANG`.
- Analysis memory (1.750.0): grid.ts `Grid.shared` {bufs, buf, at} = the fine points stay in the cell loop's round buffers (no second copy; `FIT_TASK` 14: slot 12 = which buffer, 13 = the degree to sum the moments to); when a model has more than `DROP_POINTS` 4e6 fine points, `refit` sums a cut cell's moments to `MOM_CAP` at its first fit (quad.ts `fitPoints(..., upto)`; the same sums, so the same numbers) and `dropFine` then lets the fine points of cells with more than `MOM_CAP`³ of them go (`c.fine` keeps `n`, `a` is empty; the other cells' points are copied out so the rounds are freed; E-SIM-FINE if such a cell is ever asked for its points). `setDropPoints` for tests. Board study peak 6.0 → 4.4 GB (node; `bench/thboard.bench.ts` `THBOARD_MEM=1` prints memory per stage). The helpers of `tests/unit/sim-threads.test.ts` load the test file itself: wrap a long test in `if (isMainThread)`. Opening that board's assembly peaks at 3 GB (kernel workers), not looked at yet.
- Factor memory handed back (1.751.0): cholwasm.ts `releaseBuffer(data)` keeps a factor's `WebAssembly.Memory` (at most `SPARE` 2) for the next `kernelBuffer` that fits it (same kind, up to half again as big; zeroed; `reusedMemories()`; a request none fits lets them go), since such a memory is only freed by the collector; `BlockCholesky.release()` (then `solve` throws E-SIM-RELEASED), thermal.ts `Solver.free` (direct solver; `withLowRank` passes it on): `solveSystem`, the radiation loop's `kept0`, a reuse solver that is not kept, `dropReuse`, and the warm-up's `cache` (stale linearisations go before the new factorisation, at most `KEEP_STEPS` 3 step sizes kept, all freed at the end) call it. Only free a solver nothing else holds (static.ts `keptFactors` and thermal `kept` are never freed while kept). grid.ts `dropFine` also copies fitted points that are views of a cell-loop round, and `refit` no longer makes a buffer for points that were let go. thermal.ts `points8(n)` = surface point lists on shared memory when there are helpers (`shared8` and `filmPlan` then hand the list itself to the threads). `bench/thboard.bench.ts` `THBOARD_GC=1` (with `NODE_OPTIONS=--expose-gc`) collects before each memory line. A WeakRef or FinalizationRegistry cannot count live arrays in a bench: the whole run is one job.
- Radiation loop stop (1.752.0): thermal.ts `radSettled(d, dBefore, top, it)` ends `solveOnce`'s radiation / natural convection loop when the largest change of an element's constant coefficient is ≤ 1e-4 × the top temperature, or (from the third step) ≤ 1e-3 × it and more than half the change before: Newton shrinks quadratically, so a small change that stays is rounding of badly conditioned elements (a board's third pass went 17 steps of 2 s; pass 3 solve 35.6 → 8.4 s, same temperatures to the digits shown). `bench/thboard.bench.ts` prints seconds per stage; time the loop steps before touching the limits. Tests in `tests/unit/thermal.test.ts`.
- Many bodies (1.753.0): thermal.ts `triangleBodies(doms, tris, eps)` (exported; `bodiesOf` keeps its answer per triangle list) returns an `Int32Array`: the 8-bit list it replaced read every body past the 127th as −1, so a third of a board's surface triangles went through `grid.cellOf(…, −1)` (inside tests per point) and could land in the neighbour's cell. Element and sample domains (`Ctx.dom`, static.ts `domE`, `sample().dom`, `PassResult.dom`) are `Uint16Array` (8-bit wrapped past 255 bodies); never keep a body index in an 8-bit array. `joinTerms` collects the perfect-contact points (`JPT` 13 numbers: place, w, γ, each side's weight and k·n) and runs of one element pair, then sums each run with `joinBlocks` (loop `tjoin`, `TJOIN` numbers per run, batches within `TFACE_SCRATCH`; the same function on one thread) and adds them in order: same numbers. `keptPoints` reads a point's cell straight from the body's `grid.maps` (falls back to `cellOf`); grid.ts `cellOf` makes no arrays. Board with 240 bodies: pass-3 assemble 31 → 27 s, temperatures within 0.2 K. Tests `tests/unit/thermal-manybodies.test.ts`.
- Open surfaces on all cores (1.754.0): grid.ts `Grid.bodies` {key, tris} = the bodies' triangles on shared memory when the cells were cut on several threads, `threadBodies(b)` = this thread's bodies for them (the cell loop's, else made again). thermal.ts `freePoints` collects the points not asked before and runs `coveredAt` (the one test: bodies near the point's cube, box, then inside a step or the gap along the normal) in loop `tfree` (`TFREE` 7 numbers per point, chunks of `TFREE_CHUNK`), else on this thread: the same answers. body.ts `MeshBody.hitsIn(axis, p, a, b)` = the cuts of `hits` strictly between a and b through the 3D boxes the stretch passes (a line along a board's column held every hole wall); quad.ts `exactLeaf`'s `cuts` and `exactFace` use it (`Inside.hitsIn`), the main lines keep `hits` (they need the parity beyond the cell). Board with 240 bodies, three passes: 77 → 72 s, same numbers. Profile after it (main thread, 72 s): `hits` and `fitMoments` in the cell cutting (the first fit sums moments to `MOM_CAP`), `Basis.values`, `filmCell`, `keptPoints` (copies, one thread), `measureCell`. Tests `tests/unit/sim-hitsin.test.ts`, `sim-threads.test.ts`.
- Body materials, emissivity and heat bodies in thermal studies (1.755.0): `Material.emis` (0..1], in `NUMS`, picker row "Surface emissivity"). `run.ts` `thermalSetup(st, b, regions, bodies?: OwnBodies)` {emis, names, tris per region}: a radiation item on all open surfaces is split into one sink per emissivity (`ownEmis` = per triangle: by the region's own triangles for a component of several solids, else by component; NaN = the item's value), `ThermalSetup.sinkRow` + `mergeFlows` put the sinks' flows back into one row per study item (every `sinkIds.push` also pushes `sinkRow` once it exists). A package part's own heat goes into `PackageHeat.hot` (component ids, from the manifest's `heat_bodies` via `PlanHeat.hot`) or `PackageHeat.bodies` (body names inside the part: `sameName`, matched with the region's `body` names = typed Bodies name, key, feature name, from `Assembler.bodyKeys` → worker `asmSimGeo` regions `body` → asm `simGeo`); none matched = all its bodies. `partTemps`: a junction part with `hot` or `die` (the caller's hottest point of the named bodies, sim ui `partTempRows` from `res.comps[].body`) reports that, `PartTemp.die`, Rjc unused. Manifest: `materials` {name, k_WmK, cp_JkgK, density_kgm3, emissivity}, part `body_materials`/`material`/`heat_bodies`, `board.emissivity` → `ThermalPart.mats/mat/heat`, `BoardPlan.given`/`boardEmis` → main.ts `simulateBoard`. Contract for writers in `docs/thermal-package-export.md`. Tests `tests/unit/thermal-bodymats.test.ts`, smoke "thermal 1.755" (shard 4), hands-on `tests/human/thermalbodymats.mjs`.
- Body temperatures inside the body (1.757.0): the plot mesh holds surface points only, so `run.ts` `innerPoints(domains, INNER_GRID 7)` (a grid over each body's box, the points its `inside` holds; made once per run) + `innerTemps(P, sample, out)` widen each component's min/max in `runThermal` and every `runTransient` frame (a point counts only when the sampler's `dom` is that body; `ThermalComp.deep` = the highest is inside, beyond 0.02 K; kept through `envelope`); sim ui `td[data-v=tcmax][data-deep]` with a title; `thermal.max` and the plot stay surface values. Solve while an assembly builds: `SimHost.building()` (asm `simAccess().building`), sim ui `built()` (one wait at a time: `waiting`, `waits`; toast + `#st-sim [data-v=building]` from `renderStatus`; polls every 100 ms) before `solve` and `findLinks`, `autoSolve` looks again later; `state().waiting/waits`. `bench/thboard.bench.ts` gives every solid its own region and body material like the app, `THBOARD_AT="x,y,z;…"` prints temperatures at points. Tests `tests/unit/thermal-inner.test.ts`, smoke "thermal 1.757" in the 1.755 block (shard 4), hands-on `tests/human/thermalinner.mjs`.
- Heat bodies with a split (1.758.0): `run.ts` `thermalSetup` pkgheat: a part whose heat bodies (`hot`/`bodies`) are some but not all of its regions makes the whole `p` in them; each share put on a board patch (`into`, contact) or a heatsink patch is also pushed as a negative `{ tris, power: -w, dom: caseDom }` source on `thermal/study.ts` `nearPatch(partTris, patch, 0.6)` (the part's triangles whose box meets the patch's grown box: leads, seat), so die power − shares never happens in the die; nothing of the part near the patch = the old way (share off the die). Component powers still sum to p − shares over the part (the case component shows a negative power), so `heatPaths` is unchanged; note `[data-v=dienote]`. Chassis shares still come off the die. Tests `tests/unit/thermal-diesplit.test.ts`, smoke "thermal 1.758" (shard 2), hands-on `tests/human/thermaldiesplit.mjs`.
- `docs/FRS-modelling.md`: functional requirements for growing the viewer into a parametric modeller (phases P0–P7, requirement IDs `FR-…`, acceptance tests `AT-…`). Modelling work follows it; cite the IDs in PRs.

## Conventions

- Internal units are millimetres and radians; format only for display (`units.ts`).
- STEP circles often arrive as two 180° arcs; use `circlesOf`/`faceCircle` in `measure.ts` rather than checking `type === "circle"`.
- Part groups may be turned, not only moved (motion studies, mate previews `previewTfs`): map part data (`PartData`, `FaceInfo`, `EdgeInfo`, hit normals) with `Viewer.toWorld` / `dirToWorld` / `toPart` / `placing(p)`, never by adding `group.position` (1.576.0 fixed Smart measure, Snapper, highlights, section profiles and csys for this).
- `computeBoundsTree({ indirect: true })` keeps triangle order matching B-rep faces; don't drop `indirect`.
- Debug handle: `window.__sv` in dev or with `?debug` in the URL.
- Face naming is part of a part file's contract: mates, drawings and features store face names. `PartDoc.naming` 2 (new parts, 1.410.0) matches sweep side faces to sketch curves by a point on the edge (`Modeller.edgeOnPoint`); older files keep the centre-of-mass match. Never change how existing files name faces without such a version switch.
- MIT licence; the `occt/` folder in builds ships occt-import-js/OCCT under LGPL-2.1, unmodified except that `scripts/copy-occt.mjs` runs `scripts/occt-patch.mjs` on opencascade.full.js (embind invokers made on first call; the WASM is untouched).
- Mates and motion tests (1.771.0): test list `/mnt/project-files/tests/mates-motion-tests.md`, hands-on `tests/human/mates.mjs` (exports `plateAndPin`, `faceSpot`, `doc`, face tests `CYL`/`TOP`), `mates2.mjs`, `motion.mjs`. `evaluate.ts` `frameTime(s, n)` = min(duration, n/fps): the last frame is solved at the study end and `tfsAt` blends between frame times (a 1.25 s study at 30 fps ended at 1.2417 s). Asm `MateDraft.same` (second face on the same component replaces the first, status says so); `viewMates` + `showMatesOf(id | null)` (component menu View mates: Mates folder row "Mates of {name}" `.ft-vmates` with `[data-a=vmclear]`, Esc clears; mates matched by `topOf`).
- Still air across gaps (1.761.0, FR-TCON-004): `StudySettings.airSpan` (default `DEFAULT_AIR_SPAN` 2 mm, 0 = off; ui input `data-k=airspan`) → `ThermalProblem.airSpan`; in `joinTerms` a surface point that touches nothing marches along its normal in `AIR_STEP` 0.25 mm steps up to the span, halves the last step 4 times to the other body and joins the two with hc = airK(`ThermalProblem.airT` ?? `startTemp`) / distance, as `ThermalContact.type` "air" (contacts keyed apart from touching ones of the same pair). run.ts passes `airT` = `airMean(contacts)` of the pass before (reuse key gets `|air<T>`). ui `thermalLinksHtml` sums a pair's contacts (`still` = air only → "through still air"); `heatPaths` takes the board from the study's `comps[id].board` when the pkgheat load has no `board` (Tube Amp CAD heat-only exports). Tests `tests/unit/thermal-stillair.test.ts`.
- Add surroundings (1.762.0): `types.ts` `hasSurroundings(st)` (an unsuppressed temp/conv/rad/chassis support) / `addSurroundings(st, emis)` (natural convection + radiation on all open surfaces); sim ui Cooling section `[data-v=surround]` + `[data-a=surround]` (one undo step) when a thermal study has none. Tube Amp CAD 3.267.0 exports heat sources only (no ambient, enclosure, down or supports). Tests `tests/unit/thermal-surround.test.ts`, smoke "thermal 1.762" (shard 2).
- Core modelling round (1.774.0, tests list `/mnt/project-files/tests/core-modelling-tests.md`, hands-on `tests/human/core.mjs`): kernel `shell` with no face removed cuts the offset solid from the body (outward: body from the reversed grown solid), outward shells try `GeomAbs_Intersection` first (`sharpOk`: valid and not empty), else Arc; `doc.ts` `setSuppressed(d, id, on)` (dependants suppressed with `supBy` = the parent, unsuppress wakes the parents and everything with `supBy` in that set; `supBy` and `comment` are left out of the rebuild chain key); controller `draftFailure` blocks ✓ while the shown draft (`shownDraft` = JSON of the draft of the last finished rebuild) fails and is new or changed; a tree row click refocuses the redrawn row. `lib.mjs` `clickAt` holds `modifiers` on the keyboard.
- Linear pattern direction arrows (1.774.0): kernel `linearPattern` adds spots `@1`/`@2` (seed centre, `PatternSpot.d` = the direction, flips included) beside the instance dots (filter keys starting with "@" when you want instances); controller `drawPatternArrows` = `placeArrow` keys `spacing`/`spacing2` (value = distance to the first copy for Up to reference, no label then), `moveArrow` skips an upTo direction, a click without movement (`endArrowDrag`) toggles `flip`/`flip2`. Hands-on C3-arrows/C3-drag in `tests/human/core.mjs`, smoke "model 1.774" (shard 2).
- Study set-up shortcuts (1.770.0): sim ui `command(c)` wraps `command0`: a new item takes the faces picked before (`SimHost.picked` = part controller `simAccess().picked`, Smart measure face + Ctrl picks; `takeFaces` skips items without picks or round faces for round types) and clears them (`clearPick`, so a Smart measure label is not left under the next click); `menu(faceId)` = study commands for part controller `geometryMenu` (via `simHook.menu`, only on the Simulation tab, idle); `valueText` = a load's size in its tree row; the results file keeps `active` (study on screen) and reopened file results are plotted when the Simulation tab is on (`showKept`); Duplicate study keeps fresh results; part/asm `save` ask for the file before packing results. Hands-on `tests/human/simsetup.mjs`, test list `/mnt/project-files/tests/analysis-tests.md`.
- Simulation bar flyouts (1.775.0): sim ui `MORE` (sup/load/cool ids kept behind ▾) + `FLY_TITLE`; `renderBar` `fly(b, key)` puts `button.mc-caret[data-fly]` after the last shown button of the group, `flyMenu(caret)` fills `#mc-menu` (`[data-s]` items, arrows, Esc back to the caret). Tests reach any command with `simCmd(pg, s)` (smoke.mjs top, `tests/human/lib.mjs`). `#sim-pm` keydown Tab on a changed text field takes the value itself (`tabbed` skips the change event that follows) and focuses the next field, since the redraw lost the tab order. main.ts `openFiles`: an assembly in the pick opens before a part (a whole folder). Hands-on `tests/human/simsetup2.mjs`.
- Core modelling round 2 (1.776.0): hands-on `tests/human/core2.mjs` (rows A4, A11–A13, B2–B3, C9, D4, E4–E5, F11); controller `syncModeStatus` (from `renderBar` and `renderPanel`) = `#mc-status` per fillet/chamfer/shell mode and focus; `chamferSides` puts two-distance / distance-angle chamfer callouts (`.mc-callout[data-side=d1|d2|a]`) on the faces they are measured on (Distance 1 = `faces[flip ? 1 : 0]`, as the kernel); shell multi-thickness walls get `.mc-mtcall[data-mt]` callouts in `drawValueCallouts`; variable radius rows `li.pm-vr`.
